# Seri 3 - Flyway Practical Setup with Maven, PostgreSQL, Docker Compose, and Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus ke setup praktis **Flyway** untuk microservice Java berbasis:

```text id="v57js8"
Java 17+
Maven
Jersey / JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Docker Compose
Kubernetes-ready deployment model
```

Target akhirnya: engineer bisa membuat project migration yang:

- bisa dijalankan di local development;
- bisa divalidasi di CI;
- bisa dipakai untuk integration test;
- bisa dipakai di deployment pipeline;
- aman untuk PostgreSQL object seperti table, index, seed, view, materialized view, PL/pgSQL function, procedure, dan trigger.

Flyway mendukung migration berbentuk **versioned**, **repeatable**, dan **baseline**, serta migration dapat ditulis dalam SQL atau Java. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/migrations-271585107.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk PostgreSQL, dokumentasi Flyway saat ini menyebut dukungan PostgreSQL tersedia melalui dependency/module database PostgreSQL terpisah untuk Java usage. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/postgresql-database-277579325.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Target Project Shape

```text id="e7mr48"
case-service/
├─ pom.xml
├─ docker-compose.yml
├─ src/
│  ├─ main/
│  │  ├─ java/
│  │  │  └─ com/company/caseapp/
│  │  │     ├─ api/
│  │  │     │  └─ CaseResource.java
│  │  │     ├─ service/
│  │  │     │  └─ CaseService.java
│  │  │     ├─ repository/
│  │  │     │  └─ CaseRepository.java
│  │  │     └─ config/
│  │  │        └─ DataSourceFactory.java
│  │  └─ resources/
│  │     ├─ mybatis/
│  │     │  └─ mapper/
│  │     │     └─ CaseMapper.xml
│  │     └─ db/
│  │        └─ migration/
│  │           ├─ V001__create_enforcement_case_table.sql
│  │           ├─ V002__add_case_indexes.sql
│  │           ├─ V003__seed_case_status_reference.sql
│  │           ├─ R__view_case_summary.sql
│  │           ├─ R__fn_calculate_case_sla.sql
│  │           └─ R__trg_fn_case_audit.sql
│  └─ test/
│     ├─ java/
│     └─ resources/
└─ README.md
```

### Prinsip Struktur

| Folder / File | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `src/main/resources/db/migration` | Default lokasi migration Flyway pada banyak setup Java/Maven. |
| `V001__...sql` | Versioned migration, jalan sekali berdasarkan versi. |
| `R__...sql` | Repeatable migration, jalan ulang saat checksum berubah. |
| `mybatis/mapper` | SQL mapper yang harus sinkron dengan schema migration. |
| `docker-compose.yml` | PostgreSQL local untuk developer onboarding dan integration test. |
| `pom.xml` | Dependency driver PostgreSQL, Flyway plugin/library, test stack. |

---

## 3. Flyway Migration Naming Convention

Flyway punya beberapa tipe migration utama: **versioned**, **repeatable**, dan **baseline**. Versioned migration digunakan untuk perubahan yang dijalankan sekali; repeatable migration dijalankan ulang saat checksum berubah. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/migrations-271585107.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### 3.1 Versioned Migration

```text id="n8yjd7"
V001__create_enforcement_case_table.sql
V002__add_case_indexes.sql
V003__seed_case_status_reference.sql
```

Format mental:

```text id="dr0rtb"
V<version>__<description>.sql
```

Contoh yang baik:

```text id="j0fufo"
V001__create_enforcement_case_table.sql
V002__create_case_status_reference_table.sql
V003__add_enforcement_case_status_fk.sql
V004__create_case_created_at_index.sql
```

Contoh yang buruk:

```text id="ek5i1e"
V1__update.sql
V2__fix.sql
V3__misc.sql
```

Masalahnya: nama tidak menjelaskan niat perubahan.

---

### 3.2 Repeatable Migration

```text id="w1a5re"
R__view_case_summary.sql
R__fn_calculate_case_sla.sql
R__proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
R__trg_fn_case_audit.sql
```

Repeatable migration cocok untuk object yang definisinya dikelola dalam satu file dan bisa dibuat ulang saat definisinya berubah. Dokumentasi Flyway menyebut repeatable migration lazim digunakan untuk membuat ulang **views, procedures, functions, packages**, dan bulk reference data reinserts. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repeatable-migrations-273973335.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

### 3.3 Baseline Migration

Baseline migration digunakan saat mulai mengelola database yang sudah ada. Di Flyway, baseline migration termasuk salah satu jenis migration selain versioned dan repeatable. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/migrations-271585107.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```text id="bn1pe7"
B001__baseline_existing_schema.sql
```

Dalam praktik enterprise, baseline harus sangat hati-hati karena menyatakan:

```text id="thah38"
Kondisi schema existing dianggap sebagai starting point resmi.
Migration berikutnya dimulai setelah baseline.
```

---

## 4. Maven Setup

Ada dua pola penggunaan Flyway:

| Pola | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|
| **Flyway Maven Plugin** | Migration dijalankan dari Maven command / CI pipeline. |
| **Flyway Java API** | Migration dijalankan programmatically, misalnya saat test setup atau tool internal. |

Untuk production microservice di Kubernetes, saya lebih merekomendasikan:

```text id="6zrrhh"
Pipeline/Kubernetes Job menjalankan migration.
Aplikasi Jersey tidak otomatis menjalankan migration saat setiap pod startup.
```

---

## 5. `pom.xml` Basic Setup

> Catatan: versi dependency sebaiknya mengikuti standard perusahaan atau BOM/platform yang dipakai. Jangan hardcode versi tanpa governance dependency.

Contoh struktur dasar:

```xml id="jfn0z2"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.company</groupId>
    <artifactId>case-service</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>

    <properties>
        <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>

        <flyway.version>10.22.0</flyway.version>
        <postgresql.version>42.7.4</postgresql.version>
    </properties>

    <dependencies>
        <!-- PostgreSQL JDBC Driver -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
            <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
            <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <!-- Flyway core -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.flywaydb</groupId>
            <artifactId>flyway-core</artifactId>
            <version>${flyway.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <!-- PostgreSQL support module for Flyway Java usage -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.flywaydb</groupId>
            <artifactId>flyway-database-postgresql</artifactId>
            <version>${flyway.version}</version>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>

    <build>
        <plugins>
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.flywaydb</groupId>
                <artifactId>flyway-maven-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>${flyway.version}</version>

                <configuration>
                    <url>${flyway.url}</url>
                    <user>${flyway.user}</user>
                    <password>${flyway.password}</password>
                    <schemas>
                        <schema>case_app</schema>
                    </schemas>
                    <locations>
                        <location>classpath:db/migration</location>
                    </locations>
                </configuration>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>
</project>
```

Namun ada catatan penting: dokumentasi Redgate saat ini menyebut PostgreSQL support untuk Java usage berada di module `flyway-database-postgresql`, dan contoh Maven Redgate memakai group id `com.redgate.flyway` untuk module PostgreSQL pada distribusi Redgate. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/postgresql-database-277579325.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dalam project nyata, pastikan group/artifact/version mengikuti distribusi Flyway yang distandardkan perusahaan: OSS Maven Central, Redgate distribution, atau internal artifact mirror.

---

## 6. Docker Compose untuk PostgreSQL Local

```yaml id="x5pvfu"
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    container_name: case-service-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: case_service
      POSTGRES_USER: case_app
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: case_app_password
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - case_service_pgdata:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U case_app -d case_service"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 10

volumes:
  case_service_pgdata:
```

### Local Run

```bash id="45sfs8"
docker compose up -d postgres
```

```bash id="kevj0j"
mvn flyway:info \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app \
  -Dflyway.password=case_app_password
```

```bash id="cmvghl"
mvn flyway:migrate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app \
  -Dflyway.password=case_app_password
```

```bash id="mwecc6"
mvn flyway:validate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app \
  -Dflyway.password=case_app_password
```

Flyway Maven plugin memiliki goal seperti `migrate`, `clean`, `info`, `validate`, `baseline`, dan `repair`. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/maven-goal-277579365.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Configuration Precedence

Ini sering menjadi sumber bug di CI/CD.

Dokumentasi Flyway Maven plugin menyebut konfigurasi dimuat dengan urutan prioritas tertentu: **system properties** paling tinggi, lalu **environment variables**, custom config files, Maven properties, plugin configuration, credentials dari `settings.xml`, user-home config, lalu default plugin. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/maven-goal-277579365.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Implikasi Praktis

Jika konfigurasi di `pom.xml` berbeda dengan `-Dflyway.url=...`, maka command-line property bisa menang.

Contoh:

```bash id="jplc8d"
mvn flyway:migrate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://staging-db:5432/case_service
```

Bisa override konfigurasi dari `pom.xml`.

### Rule

```text id="638o85"
Production credentials jangan ditaruh di pom.xml.
Gunakan secret manager / CI secret / Kubernetes secret.
```

---

## 8. First Migration: Schema dan Table

```sql id="xfltmm"
-- V001__create_case_schema.sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_app;
```

```sql id="1i2rpz"
-- V002__create_enforcement_case_table.sql
CREATE TABLE case_app.enforcement_case (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number     TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL,
    priority        TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Catatan

Saya sengaja memakai schema eksplisit `case_app`.

Kenapa?

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Avoid ambiguity | Tidak bergantung penuh pada `search_path`. |
| Security | Privilege bisa dibatasi per schema. |
| Multi-service clarity | Setiap service bisa punya schema ownership jelas. |
| Migration clarity | SQL object reference lebih eksplisit. |

---

## 9. Reference Table dan Seed Data

```sql id="nsjcxq"
-- V003__create_case_status_reference.sql
CREATE TABLE case_app.case_status_reference (
    code        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    label       TEXT NOT NULL,
    sort_order  INTEGER NOT NULL,
    is_active   BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);
```

```sql id="ca9rtj"
-- V004__seed_case_status_reference.sql
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
VALUES
    ('DRAFT', 'Draft', 10, true),
    ('OPEN', 'Open', 20, true),
    ('UNDER_REVIEW', 'Under Review', 30, true),
    ('CLOSED', 'Closed', 40, true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

### Kenapa `ON CONFLICT`?

Karena seed data sebaiknya **idempotent**.

Artinya:

```text id="ti6s14"
Jika script terlanjur dijalankan ulang dalam local/test/recovery scenario,
hasilnya tetap konsisten.
```

---

## 10. FK dan Constraint

```sql id="l5w54n"
-- V005__add_case_status_fk.sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_enforcement_case_status
FOREIGN KEY (status)
REFERENCES case_app.case_status_reference (code);
```

Untuk production table besar, FK dan constraint perlu dipikirkan lebih hati-hati, terutama kalau data existing belum bersih.

Pattern aman:

```text id="aoqdk2"
1. Tambahkan reference table
2. Bersihkan data existing
3. Tambahkan FK / CHECK / NOT NULL
4. Validate
```

---

## 11. Index Migration

```sql id="dpw7j4"
-- V006__create_case_lookup_indexes.sql
CREATE INDEX idx_enforcement_case_status
ON case_app.enforcement_case (status);

CREATE INDEX idx_enforcement_case_created_at
ON case_app.enforcement_case (created_at);
```

Untuk table besar di production, pertimbangkan:

```sql id="v083w6"
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_enforcement_case_created_at
ON case_app.enforcement_case (created_at);
```

Tetapi PostgreSQL menyatakan `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak bisa dijalankan di dalam transaction block. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Maka migration tool perlu dikonfigurasi agar migration tersebut tidak dibungkus transaksi, atau migration dipisah dan dijalankan dengan strategi khusus.

---

## 12. Repeatable View Migration

```sql id="x818vi"
-- R__view_case_summary.sql
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.case_number,
    c.status,
    sr.label AS status_label,
    c.priority,
    c.created_at,
    c.updated_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
JOIN case_app.case_status_reference sr
    ON sr.code = c.status;
```

### Kenapa Repeatable?

View adalah object definitional. Saat query view berubah, file view bisa diperbarui dan Flyway akan mendeteksi checksum berubah lalu menjalankan ulang repeatable migration. Repeatable migrations memang lazim digunakan untuk view/procedure/function dan bulk reference data reinserts. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repeatable-migrations-273973335.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Risiko

View bisa menjadi contract tersembunyi.

Jika MyBatis mapper membaca dari view:

```xml id="ktrzs6"
<select id="findCaseSummary" resultType="CaseSummaryDto">
    SELECT
        id,
        case_number,
        status,
        status_label,
        priority,
        created_at
    FROM case_app.v_case_summary
    WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

Maka perubahan nama kolom view bisa mematahkan mapper.

---

## 13. Repeatable PL/pgSQL Function Migration

```sql id="zyif6f"
-- R__fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.fn_calculate_case_age_days(
    p_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ
)
RETURNS INTEGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF p_created_at IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NULL;
    END IF;

    RETURN FLOOR(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (now() - p_created_at)) / 86400)::INTEGER;
END;
$$;
```

### Hal yang Harus Diperhatikan

| Aspek | Kenapa Penting |
|---|---|
| Function signature | Mengubah parameter/return type bisa breaking. |
| Volatility | `IMMUTABLE`, `STABLE`, `VOLATILE` memengaruhi planner dan correctness. |
| Search path | Function sebaiknya schema-qualified. |
| Security | Hati-hati `SECURITY DEFINER`. |
| Dependency | View/trigger/procedure bisa bergantung pada function. |

---

## 14. Repeatable Trigger Function + Versioned Trigger Binding

Trigger function:

```sql id="rj4y6r"
-- R__trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at := now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

Trigger binding:

```sql id="u04u0a"
-- V007__create_case_updated_at_trigger.sql
CREATE TRIGGER trg_enforcement_case_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON case_app.enforcement_case
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at();
```

### Kenapa Dipisah?

```text id="daxfnw"
Trigger function body = definition → repeatable
Trigger binding to table = structural attachment → versioned
```

Jika body berubah, file repeatable cukup berubah.

Jika trigger baru dipasang ke table, itu perubahan structural yang harus historis.

---

## 15. Materialized View Migration

Materialized view menyimpan hasil query secara fisik seperti table-like object dan bisa di-refresh. PostgreSQL documentation menjelaskan materialized view menyimpan hasil query dan dapat di-refresh untuk memperbarui data. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/rules-materializedviews.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Definition

```sql id="c2tm6e"
-- R__matview_case_status_metrics.sql
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW IF NOT EXISTS case_app.mv_case_status_metrics AS
SELECT
    status,
    COUNT(*) AS case_count,
    max(updated_at) AS latest_update_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
GROUP BY status
WITH NO DATA;
```

### Index

```sql id="rrgsls"
-- V008__create_case_status_metrics_unique_index.sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_mv_case_status_metrics_status
ON case_app.mv_case_status_metrics (status);
```

### Refresh

```sql id="9tvti5"
-- V009__refresh_case_status_metrics_initial.sql
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_status_metrics;
```

### Catatan

Untuk production, refresh materialized view jangan dianggap hal kecil.

Perlu dipikirkan:

| Concern | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Stale data | Data materialized view tidak otomatis real-time. |
| Refresh cost | Bisa berat jika query besar. |
| Concurrent refresh | Perlu unique index untuk `REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW CONCURRENTLY`. |
| Scheduling | Refresh bisa via job, scheduler, batch, atau event-driven process. |
| API correctness | Jangan tampilkan data stale sebagai real-time tanpa metadata. |

---

## 16. Flyway Commands yang Perlu Dikuasai

Flyway Maven plugin menyediakan goal seperti `migrate`, `clean`, `info`, `validate`, `baseline`, dan `repair`. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/maven-goal-277579365.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Command | Fungsi | Praktik |
|---|---|---|
| `flyway:info` | Melihat migration applied/pending. | Jalankan sebelum migrate di CI/staging. |
| `flyway:validate` | Validasi file migration vs schema history. | Wajib di PR/CI. |
| `flyway:migrate` | Menjalankan pending migration. | Pipeline deploy. |
| `flyway:baseline` | Menandai existing DB sebagai baseline. | Legacy DB onboarding. |
| `flyway:repair` | Memperbaiki metadata schema history dalam kasus tertentu. | Harus lewat SOP incident. |
| `flyway:clean` | Menghapus object DB/schema. | Local/test only; disable di prod. |

---

## 17. Local Developer Workflow

### 17.1 Start PostgreSQL

```bash id="bub7my"
docker compose up -d postgres
```

### 17.2 Run Migration

```bash id="1l20ls"
mvn flyway:migrate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app \
  -Dflyway.password=case_app_password
```

### 17.3 Check Info

```bash id="edeb2j"
mvn flyway:info \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app \
  -Dflyway.password=case_app_password
```

### 17.4 Validate

```bash id="v04uzi"
mvn flyway:validate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app \
  -Dflyway.password=case_app_password
```

### 17.5 Reset Local DB

Untuk local saja:

```bash id="oo2xfi"
docker compose down -v
docker compose up -d postgres
mvn flyway:migrate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app \
  -Dflyway.password=case_app_password
```

Jangan jadikan `clean` sebagai kebiasaan production. Untuk local, reset volume Docker lebih eksplisit dan mengurangi risiko salah target environment.

---

## 18. Integration Test Pattern

Untuk integration test, alur ideal:

```text id="72z4yi"
1. Start ephemeral PostgreSQL
2. Run Flyway migration
3. Build SqlSessionFactory / DataSource
4. Run MyBatis mapper test
5. Run Jersey resource/service integration test
6. Tear down database
```

### Test Target

| Test | Tujuan |
|---|---|
| Migration test | Semua migration bisa jalan dari kosong. |
| MyBatis mapper test | SQL mapper cocok dengan schema aktual. |
| Repository test | Query dan transaction boundary benar. |
| Jersey integration test | API berjalan di atas schema hasil migration. |
| View/function test | Repeatable object menghasilkan output sesuai contract. |

---

## 19. Programmatic Flyway for Test Setup

Contoh Java API untuk test setup:

```java id="ioqg01"
import org.flywaydb.core.Flyway;

public final class TestDatabaseMigrator {

    private TestDatabaseMigrator() {
    }

    public static void migrate(String jdbcUrl, String username, String password) {
        Flyway flyway = Flyway.configure()
                .dataSource(jdbcUrl, username, password)
                .schemas("case_app")
                .locations("classpath:db/migration")
                .load();

        flyway.migrate();
    }
}
```

### Catatan

Gunakan ini untuk test/local tool, bukan otomatis di semua production pod.

Untuk production:

```text id="p1f3dn"
Preferred:
    CI/CD pipeline or Kubernetes Job

Avoid:
    every Jersey application pod calls flyway.migrate() on startup
```

---

## 20. Jersey/JAX-RS Integration Boundary

Migration tidak perlu “terlihat” langsung oleh resource JAX-RS. Resource cukup bergantung pada repository/service yang schema-nya sudah siap.

```text id="d1rxig"
HTTP Request
  ↓
CaseResource
  ↓
CaseService
  ↓
CaseRepository
  ↓
MyBatis Mapper
  ↓
PostgreSQL schema produced by Flyway migration
```

### Anti-pattern

```text id="ye5ihs"
CaseResource startup calls flyway.migrate()
```

Kenapa buruk?

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Multi-pod race | Banyak pod mencoba migration bersamaan. |
| Startup delay | App readiness tertunda oleh migration. |
| Failure ambiguity | App gagal karena migration, bukan business logic. |
| Privilege issue | App runtime perlu DDL privilege. |
| Harder rollback | Release lifecycle bercampur dengan app boot lifecycle. |

---

## 21. MyBatis Mapper Compatibility Example

Migration:

```sql id="mo4zxw"
-- V010__add_case_risk_score.sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

Mapper update:

```xml id="byrz8q"
<resultMap id="CaseResultMap" type="com.company.caseapp.CaseEntity">
    <id property="id" column="id"/>
    <result property="caseNumber" column="case_number"/>
    <result property="status" column="status"/>
    <result property="priority" column="priority"/>
    <result property="riskScore" column="risk_score"/>
    <result property="createdAt" column="created_at"/>
    <result property="updatedAt" column="updated_at"/>
</resultMap>
```

Safe deployment:

```text id="y7un9a"
Release A:
  Migration adds nullable risk_score

Release B:
  App reads/writes risk_score optionally

Release C:
  Backfill risk_score

Release D:
  Add NOT NULL / CHECK constraint if required
```

---

## 22. CI/CD Example: GitHub Actions Shape

```yaml id="x2fks5"
name: database-migration-check

on:
  pull_request:
    paths:
      - 'src/main/resources/db/migration/**'
      - 'src/main/resources/mybatis/**'
      - 'src/main/java/**'
      - 'pom.xml'

jobs:
  migration-check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:16
        env:
          POSTGRES_DB: case_service
          POSTGRES_USER: case_app
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: case_app_password
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U case_app -d case_service"
          --health-interval 5s
          --health-timeout 3s
          --health-retries 10

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'
          cache: maven

      - name: Validate Flyway migrations
        run: |
          mvn flyway:validate \
            -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
            -Dflyway.user=case_app \
            -Dflyway.password=case_app_password

      - name: Run Flyway migrations
        run: |
          mvn flyway:migrate \
            -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service \
            -Dflyway.user=case_app \
            -Dflyway.password=case_app_password

      - name: Run tests
        run: mvn test
```

### Tambahan untuk PR Quality

Tambahkan rule:

```text id="bw3s3z"
- Migration filename lint
- Ban DROP/TRUNCATE without approval label
- Detect CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY and require non-transactional strategy
- Run MyBatis mapper tests
- Run SQL explain test for critical queries if possible
```

---

## 23. Kubernetes Execution Model

### Recommended

```text id="tyf3m0"
Deployment Pipeline
  ├─ Build app image
  ├─ Build/prepare migration artifact
  ├─ Run migration once
  │    └─ Kubernetes Job / CI runner / migration container
  ├─ Deploy application pods
  └─ Verify health
```

### Kubernetes Job Shape

```yaml id="uttqib"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: Job
metadata:
  name: case-service-db-migration
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      restartPolicy: Never
      containers:
        - name: flyway
          image: case-service-migration:1.0.0
          env:
            - name: FLYWAY_URL
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: case-service-db
                  key: jdbc-url
            - name: FLYWAY_USER
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: case-service-db
                  key: username
            - name: FLYWAY_PASSWORD
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: case-service-db
                  key: password
          command: ["flyway"]
          args: ["migrate"]
```

### Notes

| Concern | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Secret | Ambil dari Kubernetes Secret / external secret manager. |
| Image | Migration image harus berisi migration files dan Flyway runtime. |
| Retry | Hati-hati retry pada migration non-idempotent. |
| Lock | Flyway punya locking/metadata mechanism, tapi jangan jadikan alasan menjalankan dari banyak pod. |
| Observability | Log migration harus tersimpan di CI/CD atau log aggregation. |

---

## 24. Production Profile

Untuk production, bedakan role:

| Role | Privilege |
|---|---|
| `case_app_runtime` | DML terbatas: SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE sesuai kebutuhan. |
| `case_app_migration` | DDL + migration changes. |
| `case_app_readonly` | Read-only untuk reporting/debug terbatas. |

Aplikasi Jersey sebaiknya memakai runtime role, bukan migration role.

```text id="qiw1h2"
Application pod:
  uses case_app_runtime

Migration job:
  uses case_app_migration
```

Kenapa?

- mengurangi blast radius;
- mencegah app melakukan DDL tak sengaja;
- lebih baik untuk audit;
- cocok untuk regulated systems.

---

## 25. Flyway Configuration File Example

Daripada memasukkan credential ke command panjang, local bisa memakai config file.

```properties id="c8i3ih"
flyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service
flyway.user=case_app
flyway.password=case_app_password
flyway.schemas=case_app
flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration
```

Command:

```bash id="vi2o9m"
mvn flyway:migrate -Dflyway.configFiles=flyway-local.conf
```

Untuk production, jangan commit file dengan credential asli.

---

## 26. Handling Failed Migration

### Scenario

```text id="pokz7g"
V012__add_case_priority_constraint.sql failed in staging
```

Jangan langsung:

```text id="td33gj"
DELETE FROM flyway_schema_history WHERE version = '12';
```

### SOP Aman

```text id="bganip"
1. Stop further deployment.
2. Capture Flyway info output.
3. Capture DB error message.
4. Check whether migration partially changed database.
5. Check transaction behavior.
6. Decide:
   a. Fix SQL and rerun if migration never committed.
   b. Add corrective migration if state changed.
   c. Use repair only if metadata needs correction and DB state verified.
7. Document action in incident/release note.
```

`repair` adalah command resmi Flyway Maven goal, tetapi harus dipakai sebagai recovery action yang terkendali, bukan kebiasaan harian. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/maven-goal-277579365.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 27. Practical File Set Example

### `V001__create_schema.sql`

```sql id="ckk3cb"
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_app;
```

### `V002__create_tables.sql`

```sql id="44xkyf"
CREATE TABLE case_app.case_status_reference (
    code        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    label       TEXT NOT NULL,
    sort_order  INTEGER NOT NULL,
    is_active   BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

CREATE TABLE case_app.enforcement_case (
    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number     TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status          TEXT NOT NULL,
    priority        TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT fk_enforcement_case_status
        FOREIGN KEY (status)
        REFERENCES case_app.case_status_reference (code)
);
```

### `V003__seed_reference_data.sql`

```sql id="jhfysd"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
VALUES
    ('DRAFT', 'Draft', 10, true),
    ('OPEN', 'Open', 20, true),
    ('UNDER_REVIEW', 'Under Review', 30, true),
    ('CLOSED', 'Closed', 40, true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

### `R__view_case_summary.sql`

```sql id="k2tl75"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.case_number,
    c.status,
    sr.label AS status_label,
    c.priority,
    c.created_at,
    c.updated_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
JOIN case_app.case_status_reference sr
    ON sr.code = c.status;
```

### `R__fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql`

```sql id="s5eam3"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.fn_calculate_case_age_days(
    p_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ
)
RETURNS INTEGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF p_created_at IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NULL;
    END IF;

    RETURN FLOOR(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (now() - p_created_at)) / 86400)::INTEGER;
END;
$$;
```

---

## 28. Common Setup Mistakes

| Mistake | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Migration dijalankan otomatis oleh semua pod | Race condition / startup failure. | Jalankan via pipeline/job. |
| Credential production di `pom.xml` | Secret leakage. | Pakai CI secret/Kubernetes secret. |
| Migration lama diedit setelah merge | Checksum mismatch. | Buat migration baru. |
| Seed tidak idempotent | Duplicate data saat rerun. | Pakai `ON CONFLICT`. |
| View/function repeatable tidak dites | Mapper bisa rusak diam-diam. | Tambahkan integration test. |
| `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` dalam transaction | PostgreSQL error. | Non-transactional migration strategy. |
| App runtime role punya DDL privilege | Blast radius besar. | Pisahkan runtime role dan migration role. |
| Baseline dilakukan tanpa schema snapshot | Sulit audit kondisi awal. | Dump schema + review dependency. |
| Menganggap materialized view real-time | API bisa menampilkan data stale. | Tambahkan refresh strategy dan metadata. |

---

## 29. Minimal Production-Ready Checklist

Sebelum Flyway dipakai production:

| Checklist | Status |
|---|---|
| Migration folder standard tersedia | Wajib |
| Naming convention disepakati | Wajib |
| `validate` jalan di CI | Wajib |
| Migration dijalankan di ephemeral PostgreSQL saat PR | Wajib |
| MyBatis mapper integration test | Wajib |
| App tidak menjalankan migration dari semua pod | Wajib |
| Runtime DB role berbeda dari migration DB role | Wajib |
| `clean` tidak bisa dipakai di prod | Wajib |
| Failed migration SOP tersedia | Wajib |
| Destructive migration approval tersedia | Wajib |
| View/function/procedure repeatable strategy jelas | Wajib |
| Backfill besar punya strategi batch | Wajib |

## 30. Ringkasan Seri 3

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| Setup Flyway | Cocok untuk Maven + PostgreSQL + MyBatis-heavy microservice. |
| Versioned migration | Untuk table, column, constraint, index, trigger binding, seed awal. |
| Repeatable migration | Untuk view, function, procedure, trigger function, reference data tertentu. |
| Docker Compose | Mempercepat local onboarding dan reproducible dev DB. |
| CI/CD | Minimal `validate`, `migrate`, dan test MyBatis/Jersey. |
| Kubernetes | Migration harus one-time execution, bukan tiap pod startup. |
| PostgreSQL specifics | Perhatikan transaction, lock, index concurrent, materialized view refresh. |
| Production safety | Pisahkan role runtime dan role migration, siapkan SOP failure. |

## Response:
7/13/2026, 6:59:33 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 4 dari 18**: Liquibase practical setup. Fokusnya tetap praktis untuk PostgreSQL-heavy service, jadi saya akan tekankan kapan memakai YAML changeset dan kapan lebih baik memakai `sqlFile` untuk view/function/trigger.

# Seri 4 - Liquibase Practical Setup with Maven, PostgreSQL, Changelog, Changeset, Context, Label, Preconditions, and Rollback

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas setup praktis **Liquibase** untuk microservice berbasis:

```text id="d2wfb7"
Java 17+
Maven
Jersey / JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
PL/pgSQL
Docker / Kubernetes
GitHub Actions / CI/CD
```

Fokusnya bukan hanya “cara menjalankan Liquibase”, tetapi bagaimana membuat migration yang:

- terstruktur;
- bisa diaudit;
- aman untuk environment berbeda;
- cocok untuk PostgreSQL object seperti **table, index, view, materialized view, function, procedure, trigger**;
- tetap kompatibel dengan MyBatis mapper dan Jersey API;
- tidak membuat production deployment rapuh.

Liquibase memakai **changelog** sebagai daftar perubahan database, dan changelog berisi **changeset** sebagai unit perubahan. Dokumentasi Liquibase menyebut changelog bisa menyertakan changelog lain, serta bisa memakai preconditions, contexts, labels, dan attribute lain untuk mengontrol changeset mana yang berjalan dan di environment mana. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-a-changelog?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Liquibase Mental Model

```text id="70kx34"
Liquibase Mental Model
────────────────────────────────────────────

db.changelog-master.yaml
  │
  ├─ tables/001-create-case-table.yaml
  │    └─ changeset
  │         └─ createTable / addColumn / addForeignKeyConstraint
  │
  ├─ seed/010-seed-reference-data.yaml
  │    └─ changeset with context: dev,test,prod
  │
  ├─ views/020-view-case-summary.yaml
  │    └─ changeset with sqlFile + runOnChange
  │
  ├─ functions/030-fn-calculate-sla.yaml
  │    └─ changeset with sqlFile + runOnChange
  │
  └─ triggers/040-create-case-audit-trigger.yaml
       └─ changeset binding trigger to table
```

Liquibase mencatat changeset yang sudah dijalankan di tabel **`DATABASECHANGELOG`**; Liquibase juga membuat **`DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK`** untuk mencegah lebih dari satu instance Liquibase memperbarui database pada waktu yang sama. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-the-databasechangelog-table?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 3. Kapan Liquibase Lebih Cocok

Untuk stack PostgreSQL-heavy + MyBatis, saya tetap melihat Flyway sebagai default yang lebih natural. Tetapi **Liquibase sangat masuk akal** jika perusahaan butuh:

| Kebutuhan | Kenapa Liquibase Cocok |
|---|---|
| **Governance formal** | Changeset punya `id`, `author`, file path, label, context, comment, dan metadata lain. |
| **Environment filtering** | Context dan label bisa mengontrol changeset mana yang jalan. |
| **Precondition** | Bisa guard execution berdasarkan state database. |
| **Rollback metadata** | Bisa menulis rollback eksplisit dalam changeset. |
| **Changelog modular** | Main changelog bisa include changelog lain. |
| **Legacy database** | Preconditions membantu menghindari migration yang salah pada schema existing. |
| **Audit-oriented release** | `DATABASECHANGELOG` menjadi tracking table untuk deployed changesets. |

Preconditions di Liquibase adalah tag yang ditambahkan ke changelog atau changeset untuk mengontrol eksekusi update berdasarkan kondisi database; jika precondition pada changeset gagal, changeset tidak dideploy. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-1/what-are-preconditions?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 4. Target Project Structure

```text id="7u73ny"
case-service/
├─ pom.xml
├─ docker-compose.yml
├─ src/
│  └─ main/
│     └─ resources/
│        ├─ db/
│        │  ├─ changelog/
│        │  │  ├─ db.changelog-master.yaml
│        │  │  ├─ tables/
│        │  │  │  ├─ 001-create-schema.yaml
│        │  │  │  ├─ 002-create-case-status-reference.yaml
│        │  │  │  └─ 003-create-enforcement-case.yaml
│        │  │  ├─ indexes/
│        │  │  │  └─ 010-create-case-indexes.yaml
│        │  │  ├─ constraints/
│        │  │  │  └─ 020-add-case-constraints.yaml
│        │  │  ├─ seed/
│        │  │  │  └─ 030-seed-case-status-reference.yaml
│        │  │  ├─ views/
│        │  │  │  ├─ 040-view-case-summary.yaml
│        │  │  │  └─ sql/v_case_summary.sql
│        │  │  ├─ materialized-views/
│        │  │  │  ├─ 050-mv-case-status-metrics.yaml
│        │  │  │  └─ sql/mv_case_status_metrics.sql
│        │  │  ├─ functions/
│        │  │  │  ├─ 060-fn-calculate-case-age-days.yaml
│        │  │  │  └─ sql/fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
│        │  │  ├─ procedures/
│        │  │  │  ├─ 070-proc-refresh-case-metrics.yaml
│        │  │  │  └─ sql/proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
│        │  │  └─ triggers/
│        │  │     ├─ 080-trg-fn-set-updated-at.yaml
│        │  │     ├─ 081-create-updated-at-trigger.yaml
│        │  │     └─ sql/trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
│        │  └─ liquibase.properties
│        └─ mybatis/
│           └─ mapper/
│              └─ CaseMapper.xml
```

### Prinsip Struktur

| Area | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Table, column, FK, constraint | YAML/XML modeled changeset atau raw SQL jika PostgreSQL-specific. |
| Index biasa | YAML modeled change atau SQL. |
| Concurrent index | SQL + `runInTransaction: false`. |
| View | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true`. |
| Materialized view | `sqlFile` untuk definition, versioned changeset untuk index/refresh. |
| PL/pgSQL function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true`. |
| Procedure | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true`, jika signature stabil. |
| Trigger function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true`. |
| Trigger binding | Versioned changeset, karena melekat ke table. |
| Seed data | YAML/SQL changeset dengan context/label dan idempotent SQL. |

Liquibase mendukung changelog dalam SQL, XML, YAML, dan JSON; untuk SQL kompleks, changeset bisa memakai `sql` atau mereferensikan file SQL eksternal lewat `sqlFile`. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2/what-is-a-changelog?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Maven Setup

Contoh `pom.xml` minimal:

```xml id="0hq9jz"
<project>
    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>

    <groupId>com.company</groupId>
    <artifactId>case-service</artifactId>
    <version>1.0.0-SNAPSHOT</version>

    <properties>
        <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>

        <!-- Ikuti versi standar perusahaan / platform BOM -->
        <liquibase.version>4.33.0</liquibase.version>
        <postgresql.version>42.7.4</postgresql.version>
    </properties>

    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
            <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
            <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>

    <build>
        <plugins>
            <plugin>
                <groupId>org.liquibase</groupId>
                <artifactId>liquibase-maven-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>${liquibase.version}</version>

                <configuration>
                    <propertyFile>
                        src/main/resources/db/liquibase.properties
                    </propertyFile>
                </configuration>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>
</project>
```

Untuk CI/CD, hindari menyimpan credential production di `pom.xml` atau file properties yang di-commit.

---

## 6. `liquibase.properties`

Contoh untuk local development:

```properties id="oyv2a3"
changeLogFile=src/main/resources/db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml

url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_service
username=case_app_migration
password=case_app_migration_password

driver=org.postgresql.Driver
liquibaseSchemaName=case_migration
defaultSchemaName=case_app

logLevel=info
```

### Catatan Penting

| Property | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `changeLogFile` | Lokasi master changelog. |
| `url` | JDBC URL database target. |
| `username/password` | Credential role migration, bukan role aplikasi runtime. |
| `liquibaseSchemaName` | Schema untuk tabel tracking Liquibase. |
| `defaultSchemaName` | Schema default object aplikasi. |

Untuk multi-schema project, Liquibase punya parameter `liquibase-schema-name` agar tabel tracking seperti `DATABASECHANGELOG` dan `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK` berada di schema tracking terpisah dari schema yang dikelola. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2/parameters/liquibase-schema-name?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7. Docker Compose untuk Local PostgreSQL

```yaml id="jvowrn"
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    container_name: case-service-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: case_service
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - case_service_pgdata:/var/lib/postgresql/data
      - ./local-init:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U postgres -d case_service"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 10

volumes:
  case_service_pgdata:
```

Contoh `local-init/001-create-roles.sql`:

```sql id="sid2zz"
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_app;
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_migration;

CREATE USER case_app_runtime WITH PASSWORD 'case_app_runtime_password';
CREATE USER case_app_migration WITH PASSWORD 'case_app_migration_password';

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA case_app TO case_app_runtime;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA case_app TO case_app_runtime;

GRANT USAGE, CREATE ON SCHEMA case_app TO case_app_migration;
GRANT USAGE, CREATE ON SCHEMA case_migration TO case_app_migration;
```

### Role Separation

```text id="zmk5v0"
case_app_runtime
  └─ dipakai oleh aplikasi Jersey saat runtime

case_app_migration
  └─ dipakai oleh Liquibase migration job / CI/CD
```

Ini menjaga aplikasi runtime tidak punya privilege DDL secara normal.

---

## 8. Master Changelog

`db.changelog-master.yaml`:

```yaml id="0bxlwl"
databaseChangeLog:
  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/tables/001-create-schema.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/tables/002-create-case-status-reference.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/tables/003-create-enforcement-case.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/indexes/010-create-case-indexes.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/constraints/020-add-case-constraints.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/seed/030-seed-case-status-reference.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/views/040-view-case-summary.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/functions/060-fn-calculate-case-age-days.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/triggers/080-trg-fn-set-updated-at.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/triggers/081-create-updated-at-trigger.yaml
```

Master changelog membantu:

- menjaga order deterministic;
- mengurangi konflik antar domain;
- memudahkan review;
- memisahkan table, seed, view, function, dan trigger.

---

## 9. Changeset Naming Convention

Liquibase changeset diidentifikasi oleh kombinasi **`id`**, **`author`**, dan **file path changelog**; `id` adalah identifier, bukan penentu urutan eksekusi. Urutan tetap mengikuti urutan changeset dalam changelog. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2/what-is-a-changeset?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Recommended Convention

```text id="fc5s5l"
id: <sequence>-<domain>-<object>-<action>
author: <team-or-service-name>
```

Contoh:

```yaml id="5d15uw"
changeSet:
  id: 003-case-enforcement-case-create-table
  author: case-service-team
```

### Jangan

```yaml id="xhcn4v"
changeSet:
  id: 1
  author: john
```

Karena kurang informatif untuk audit dan code review.

---

## 10. Create Schema

```yaml id="oyiaag"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 001-case-create-schemas
      author: case-service-team
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_app;
              CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_migration;
      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS case_app CASCADE;
              DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS case_migration CASCADE;
```

### Catatan

Rollback schema dengan `CASCADE` sangat destructive. Di production, rollback seperti ini hampir tidak boleh dipakai tanpa review eksplisit.

---

## 11. Create Table dengan YAML Modeled Change

```yaml id="95w0v5"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 002-case-status-reference-create-table
      author: case-service-team
      changes:
        - createTable:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: case_status_reference
            columns:
              - column:
                  name: code
                  type: text
                  constraints:
                    primaryKey: true
                    nullable: false

              - column:
                  name: label
                  type: text
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false

              - column:
                  name: sort_order
                  type: int
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false

              - column:
                  name: is_active
                  type: boolean
                  defaultValueBoolean: true
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false

      rollback:
        - dropTable:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: case_status_reference
```

### Kapan Pakai Modeled Change?

| Cocok | Kurang Cocok |
|---|---|
| Create table sederhana | PL/pgSQL function panjang |
| Add column | Procedure kompleks |
| Add FK/check sederhana | Trigger function |
| Rename column | PostgreSQL-specific DDL |
| Create index biasa | `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` |

Liquibase menyebut Change Type sebagai perubahan database-independent yang bisa ditulis dalam XML/YAML/JSON dan dipetakan ke SQL seperti `CREATE TABLE`; dokumentasi juga menyarankan praktik **one Change Type per changeset** untuk mengurangi risiko partial auto-commit statement. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-1-1/change-types/what-is-a-change-type?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 12. Create Enforcement Case Table

```yaml id="ao3txl"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 003-case-enforcement-case-create-table
      author: case-service-team
      changes:
        - createTable:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
            columns:
              - column:
                  name: id
                  type: uuid
                  constraints:
                    primaryKey: true
                    nullable: false

              - column:
                  name: case_number
                  type: text
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false
                    unique: true

              - column:
                  name: status
                  type: text
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false

              - column:
                  name: priority
                  type: text
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false

              - column:
                  name: created_at
                  type: timestamptz
                  defaultValueComputed: now()
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false

              - column:
                  name: updated_at
                  type: timestamptz
                  defaultValueComputed: now()
                  constraints:
                    nullable: false

      rollback:
        - dropTable:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
```

## 13. Add Foreign Key Constraint

```yaml id="3wqw4d"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 020-case-add-status-fk
      author: case-service-team
      preConditions:
        - onFail: HALT
        - tableExists:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
        - tableExists:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: case_status_reference

      changes:
        - addForeignKeyConstraint:
            baseTableSchemaName: case_app
            baseTableName: enforcement_case
            baseColumnNames: status
            referencedTableSchemaName: case_app
            referencedTableName: case_status_reference
            referencedColumnNames: code
            constraintName: fk_enforcement_case_status

      rollback:
        - dropForeignKeyConstraint:
            baseTableSchemaName: case_app
            baseTableName: enforcement_case
            constraintName: fk_enforcement_case_status
```

### Precondition Value

Precondition memastikan object yang diperlukan memang ada sebelum FK ditambahkan. Ini berguna pada legacy DB, multi-branch deploy, atau environment yang belum seragam.

---

## 14. Seed Data dengan Context dan Label

`030-seed-case-status-reference.yaml`:

```yaml id="e2zsj0"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 030-case-status-reference-seed-prod
      author: case-service-team
      context: prod,staging,dev,test
      labels: reference-data,case-status
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference
                  (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
              VALUES
                  ('DRAFT', 'Draft', 10, true),
                  ('OPEN', 'Open', 20, true),
                  ('UNDER_REVIEW', 'Under Review', 30, true),
                  ('CLOSED', 'Closed', 40, true)
              ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
              SET
                  label = EXCLUDED.label,
                  sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
                  is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DELETE FROM case_app.case_status_reference
              WHERE code IN ('DRAFT', 'OPEN', 'UNDER_REVIEW', 'CLOSED');
```

Contexts di Liquibase adalah tag yang mengontrol apakah command seperti `update` menjalankan changeset tertentu; filter context diberikan saat runtime. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-1-1/changelog-attributes/what-are-contexts?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Running dengan Context

```bash id="2py7dd"
mvn liquibase:update \
  -Dliquibase.contextFilter=dev
```

### Practical Rule

| Seed Type | Context |
|---|---|
| Production reference data | `prod,staging,dev,test` |
| Test fixture | `test` only |
| Local dummy data | `dev` only |
| Demo data | `demo` only |

Jangan pernah mencampur dummy data dengan production reference data dalam satu changeset tanpa context guard.

---

## 15. View dengan `sqlFile` dan `runOnChange`

`040-view-case-summary.yaml`:

```yaml id="36tmjc"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 040-case-view-case-summary
      author: case-service-team
      runOnChange: true
      labels: view,case-query-contract
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/views/sql/v_case_summary.sql
            splitStatements: false
            stripComments: false

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP VIEW IF EXISTS case_app.v_case_summary;
```

`views/sql/v_case_summary.sql`:

```sql id="bjjhdb"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.case_number,
    c.status,
    sr.label AS status_label,
    c.priority,
    c.created_at,
    c.updated_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
JOIN case_app.case_status_reference sr
    ON sr.code = c.status;
```

### Kenapa `runOnChange: true`?

`runOnChange` menjalankan changeset pertama kali dan setiap kali changeset dimodifikasi; Liquibase membandingkan MD5 checksum changeset dengan checksum yang tersimpan di `DATABASECHANGELOG`. Jika `runOnChange` tidak dipakai, perubahan changeset yang sudah pernah dideploy akan menghasilkan checksum error. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2/changelog-attributes/runonchange?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Kenapa `sqlFile`?

`sqlFile` memungkinkan SQL disimpan di file eksternal dan mendukung beberapa SQL statement dalam file yang sama; statement dipisahkan dengan `;` di akhir statement atau `GO` pada baris terpisah. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-1-1/change-types/sqlfile?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 16. Materialized View dengan `sqlFile`

`050-mv-case-status-metrics.yaml`:

```yaml id="ajwk0e"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 050-case-mv-status-metrics-definition
      author: case-service-team
      runOnChange: true
      labels: materialized-view,case-reporting
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/materialized-views/sql/mv_case_status_metrics.sql
            splitStatements: false
            stripComments: false

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP MATERIALIZED VIEW IF EXISTS case_app.mv_case_status_metrics;
```

`materialized-views/sql/mv_case_status_metrics.sql`:

```sql id="thhsaf"
DROP MATERIALIZED VIEW IF EXISTS case_app.mv_case_status_metrics;

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_status_metrics AS
SELECT
    status,
    COUNT(*) AS case_count,
    max(updated_at) AS latest_update_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
GROUP BY status
WITH NO DATA;
```

Index untuk materialized view sebaiknya versioned changeset terpisah:

```yaml id="vydx2z"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 051-case-mv-status-metrics-unique-index
      author: case-service-team
      labels: materialized-view,index
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_mv_case_status_metrics_status
              ON case_app.mv_case_status_metrics (status);

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP INDEX IF EXISTS case_app.uq_mv_case_status_metrics_status;
```

### Catatan Praktis

Materialized view bukan view biasa. Ia membawa lifecycle tambahan:

- kapan data di-refresh;
- apakah data stale boleh;
- apakah API boleh membaca dari materialized view;
- apakah refresh perlu `CONCURRENTLY`;
- apakah perlu unique index;
- apakah refresh dilakukan via scheduler/job, bukan migration.

---

## 17. PL/pgSQL Function dengan `sqlFile`

`060-fn-calculate-case-age-days.yaml`:

```yaml id="s4o36y"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 060-case-fn-calculate-case-age-days
      author: case-service-team
      runOnChange: true
      labels: function,plpgsql,case-domain
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/functions/sql/fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
            splitStatements: false
            stripComments: false

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS case_app.fn_calculate_case_age_days(timestamptz);
```

`functions/sql/fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql`:

```sql id="sstg0w"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.fn_calculate_case_age_days(
    p_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ
)
RETURNS INTEGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF p_created_at IS NULL THEN
        RETURN NULL;
    END IF;

    RETURN FLOOR(EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (now() - p_created_at)) / 86400)::INTEGER;
END;
$$;
```

### Function Migration Rule

| Perubahan | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Body berubah, signature sama | `runOnChange: true` aman relatif. |
| Parameter berubah | Buat changeset baru, karena caller bisa breaking. |
| Return type berubah | Buat changeset baru + app compatibility review. |
| Function dipakai index/check constraint | Sangat hati-hati, bisa berdampak ke planner dan validity. |
| `SECURITY DEFINER` | Review search path dan privilege. |

---

## 18. Stored Procedure dengan `sqlFile`

`070-proc-refresh-case-metrics.yaml`:

```yaml id="r6v4yk"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 070-case-proc-refresh-case-metrics
      author: case-service-team
      runOnChange: true
      labels: procedure,reporting
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/procedures/sql/proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
            splitStatements: false
            stripComments: false

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP PROCEDURE IF EXISTS case_app.proc_refresh_case_metrics();
```

`procedures/sql/proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql`:

```sql id="bau5qs"
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE case_app.proc_refresh_case_metrics()
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_status_metrics;
END;
$$;
```

### Procedure Governance

Stored procedure sebaiknya dipakai untuk:

- operasi DB-side yang memang butuh berada dekat data;
- refresh materialized view;
- administrative process yang eksplisit;
- batch operation dengan audit.

Jangan jadikan procedure sebagai tempat menyembunyikan business logic utama tanpa observability dan test.

---

## 19. Trigger Function dan Trigger Binding

### 19.1 Trigger Function - `runOnChange`

`080-trg-fn-set-updated-at.yaml`:

```yaml id="p6egoh"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 080-case-trg-fn-set-updated-at
      author: case-service-team
      runOnChange: true
      labels: trigger-function,plpgsql
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/triggers/sql/trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
            splitStatements: false
            stripComments: false

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at();
```

`triggers/sql/trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql`:

```sql id="bgnklb"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at := now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

### 19.2 Trigger Binding - Versioned

`081-create-updated-at-trigger.yaml`:

```yaml id="4x2xjc"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 081-case-create-updated-at-trigger
      author: case-service-team
      labels: trigger,binding
      preConditions:
        - onFail: HALT
        - tableExists:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              CREATE TRIGGER trg_enforcement_case_set_updated_at
              BEFORE UPDATE ON case_app.enforcement_case
              FOR EACH ROW
              EXECUTE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at();

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_enforcement_case_set_updated_at
              ON case_app.enforcement_case;
```

### Kenapa Dipisah?

```text id="aaae2e"
Trigger function body = definisi logic → runOnChange
Trigger binding ke table = perubahan structural → versioned changeset
```

---

## 20. Concurrent Index dan `runInTransaction`

PostgreSQL `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak boleh berjalan dalam transaction block. Di Liquibase, changeset secara default dijalankan sebagai satu transaction jika memungkinkan; behavior ini bisa diubah dengan attribute `runInTransaction`. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-1/changelog-attributes/runintransaction?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```yaml id="5l9mh6"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 090-case-created-at-index-concurrently
      author: case-service-team
      runInTransaction: false
      labels: index,online-ddl
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_enforcement_case_created_at
              ON case_app.enforcement_case (created_at);

      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS case_app.idx_enforcement_case_created_at;
```

### Warning

Liquibase documentation memperingatkan bahwa jika `runInTransaction=false` dan error terjadi di tengah changeset yang berisi beberapa statement, tabel `DATABASECHANGELOG` dapat tertinggal dalam state invalid. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2/changelog-attributes/what-is-a-changelog-attribute?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Praktik aman:

```text id="s6mz32"
Jika runInTransaction=false:
  - satu statement utama per changeset;
  - jangan campur banyak DDL;
  - test di staging dengan ukuran data realistis;
  - siapkan failure SOP.
```

---

## 21. Rollback Strategy

Liquibase rollback bisa dilakukan berdasarkan tag, date/time, atau jumlah changeset. Saat Liquibase melakukan rollback changeset, row terkait di `DATABASECHANGELOG` dihapus. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-1-1/what-is-a-rollback?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Namun jangan salah paham:

```text id="licm6s"
Liquibase mendukung rollback ≠ semua rollback aman untuk production.
```

### Modeled Changelog vs SQL Changelog

Liquibase dapat membuat rollback otomatis untuk banyak change type pada modeled changelog XML/YAML/JSON, seperti `createTable`, `renameColumn`, dan `addColumn`; tetapi untuk formatted SQL changelog, rollback harus didefinisikan manual. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2-1/init-update-and-rollback-commands/rollback?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Practical Rule

| Perubahan | Rollback Strategy |
|---|---|
| Create table kosong | `dropTable` bisa diterima di non-prod; production hati-hati. |
| Add nullable column | `dropColumn` mungkin aman jika belum dipakai. |
| Add index | `dropIndex` relatif aman. |
| Add FK/check | `dropConstraint` relatif aman. |
| Drop column | Roll-forward lebih aman; data bisa hilang. |
| Backfill data | Butuh inverse script atau roll-forward repair. |
| View/function body | Rollback ke definisi lama bisa, asal file lama tersedia. |
| Trigger | Drop trigger bisa, tetapi dampaknya ke audit/business rule harus jelas. |

---

## 22. Tag Release untuk Rollback Boundary

Contoh tag changeset:

```yaml id="effib8"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 999-tag-release-2026-07-13
      author: release-engineering
      labels: release-tag
      changes:
        - tagDatabase:
            tag: release-2026-07-13-case-service
```

Rollback ke tag:

```bash id="5u7gde"
mvn liquibase:rollback \
  -Dliquibase.rollbackTag=release-2026-07-13-case-service
```

Liquibase `rollback` berbasis tag mengembalikan perubahan sampai tag yang ditentukan; dokumentasi juga menekankan tag perlu ada sebelum rollback berbasis tag bisa dipakai. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2-1/init-update-and-rollback-commands/rollback?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 23. `updateTestingRollback`

Untuk non-prod, gunakan rollback testing saat changeset mengklaim punya rollback.

```bash id="3ymr7w"
mvn liquibase:updateTestingRollback
```

Dokumentasi Liquibase menyebut goal Maven `updateTestingRollback` biasanya dipakai untuk menguji fungsi rollback saat changeset dideploy secara berurutan. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/oss/integration-guide-4-33/updatetestingrollback?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Tapi Tetap Hati-hati

`updateTestingRollback` membantu memvalidasi rollback mechanics, bukan membuktikan rollback production aman secara bisnis.

---

## 24. Command yang Perlu Dikuasai

| Command | Fungsi Praktis |
|---|---|
| `liquibase:validate` | Validasi changelog sebelum update. |
| `liquibase:update` | Menjalankan pending changesets. |
| `liquibase:status` | Melihat pending changesets. |
| `liquibase:updateSQL` | Generate SQL tanpa mengeksekusi, untuk review. |
| `liquibase:rollback` | Rollback ke tag/date/count sesuai konfigurasi. |
| `liquibase:updateTestingRollback` | Test deploy → rollback → deploy ulang di non-prod. |
| `liquibase:clearCheckSums` | Reset checksum metadata; hanya untuk recovery terkontrol. |

Liquibase Maven `validate` digunakan untuk mendeteksi masalah pada changelog sebelum `update`, termasuk struktur XML/YAML/JSON/formatted SQL, file yang direferensikan, dan attribute yang required/prohibited untuk database. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/integration-guide-5-2/maven-validate?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 25. Local Workflow

Start PostgreSQL:

```bash id="9t1k1c"
docker compose up -d postgres
```

Validate:

```bash id="ewtqys"
mvn liquibase:validate
```

Generate SQL preview:

```bash id="6cq6vp"
mvn liquibase:updateSQL
```

Apply migration:

```bash id="e0ufya"
mvn liquibase:update
```

Check status:

```bash id="k1m1dr"
mvn liquibase:status
```

Test rollback di non-prod/local:

```bash id="gn4mxy"
mvn liquibase:updateTestingRollback
```

---

## 26. CI/CD Pattern

```text id="n3rmjd"
Pull Request
  ├─ Validate changelog
  ├─ Generate updateSQL artifact
  ├─ Apply migration to ephemeral PostgreSQL
  ├─ Run MyBatis integration test
  ├─ Run Jersey API integration test
  ├─ Run rollback test if claimed reversible
  └─ Require approval for destructive migration

Staging
  ├─ Run Liquibase once
  ├─ Deploy app
  ├─ Smoke test
  └─ Verify logs/metrics

Production
  ├─ Backup / restore point
  ├─ Run Liquibase once via pipeline or Kubernetes Job
  ├─ Deploy app rollout
  ├─ Monitor
  └─ Archive migration evidence
```

### GitHub Actions Shape

```yaml id="m2ukcd"
name: liquibase-migration-check

on:
  pull_request:
    paths:
      - 'src/main/resources/db/changelog/**'
      - 'src/main/resources/mybatis/**'
      - 'src/main/java/**'
      - 'pom.xml'

jobs:
  migration-check:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:16
        env:
          POSTGRES_DB: case_service
          POSTGRES_USER: postgres
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U postgres -d case_service"
          --health-interval 5s
          --health-timeout 3s
          --health-retries 10

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: '17'
          cache: maven

      - name: Validate Liquibase changelog
        run: mvn liquibase:validate

      - name: Generate SQL preview
        run: mvn liquibase:updateSQL

      - name: Apply Liquibase migration
        run: mvn liquibase:update

      - name: Run tests
        run: mvn test
```

Production credential harus dipasok dari secret manager / CI secret, bukan hardcoded di repository.

---

## 27. Kubernetes Execution Model

Recommended:

```text id="zgfwg8"
Release Pipeline
  ├─ Build app image
  ├─ Build migration artifact/image
  ├─ Run Liquibase once
  │    └─ Kubernetes Job / CI runner / migration container
  ├─ Deploy Jersey application pods
  └─ Verify
```

Jangan:

```text id="66y3y8"
Setiap pod aplikasi Jersey menjalankan liquibase.update() saat startup production.
```

Liquibase memang memakai `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK` untuk mencegah multiple instance berjalan bersamaan, tetapi itu bukan alasan untuk menjadikan setiap app pod sebagai migration runner. Lock table membantu concurrency safety, tetapi deployment architecture tetap lebih bersih jika migration dijalankan **sekali per release**. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-1/what-is-the-database-changelog-lock-table?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 28. MyBatis Compatibility

Jika view berubah:

```sql id="7qmbqh"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status,
    priority
FROM case_app.enforcement_case;
```

Mapper yang bergantung pada view harus ikut diuji:

```xml id="kwfr46"
<select id="findCaseSummary" resultType="CaseSummaryDto">
    SELECT
        id,
        case_number,
        status,
        priority
    FROM case_app.v_case_summary
    WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

### Contract Rule

```text id="36fosf"
View/function/procedure yang dipakai MyBatis adalah internal database contract.
Perubahan column name, return type, function signature, atau semantic meaning
harus dianggap sebagai breaking change.
```

---

## 29. Liquibase vs PostgreSQL Object Strategy

| PostgreSQL Object | Recommended Liquibase Strategy |
|---|---|
| Schema | Modeled YAML or raw SQL. |
| Table | Modeled YAML/XML changeset. |
| Column | Modeled changeset. |
| FK/check/unique | Modeled changeset jika simple; SQL jika PostgreSQL-specific. |
| Index biasa | Modeled or SQL. |
| Concurrent index | SQL + `runInTransaction: false`. |
| View | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true`. |
| Materialized view | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange`, plus separate versioned index/refresh strategy. |
| Function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange`; new changeset jika signature berubah. |
| Procedure | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange`; new changeset jika signature berubah. |
| Trigger function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange`. |
| Trigger binding | Versioned changeset. |
| Event trigger | Versioned changeset + approval khusus. |
| Seed data | SQL changeset dengan context/label dan idempotency. |
| Large backfill | Prefer external job atau chunked SQL with runbook. |

---

## 30. Anti-Pattern Liquibase

| Anti-Pattern | Masalah | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Semua hal ditulis modeled YAML meski SQL kompleks | PL/pgSQL jadi sulit dibaca. | Pakai `sqlFile` untuk function/procedure/view/trigger. |
| `runOnChange` dipakai untuk table/column migration | Bisa membuat structural migration rerun tidak terduga. | Pakai ordinary versioned changeset. |
| Seed dev/test tanpa context | Dummy data bisa masuk staging/prod. | Wajib context/label. |
| Rollback dianggap selalu aman | Data destructive tetap berbahaya. | Roll-forward plan untuk production. |
| `clearCheckSums` dipakai sembarangan | Menutupi perubahan changeset yang seharusnya dicurigai. | Gunakan hanya dalam recovery SOP. |
| `runInTransaction=false` berisi banyak statement | Partial failure bisa membuat state kacau. | Satu statement utama per changeset. |
| App runtime pakai migration credential | Blast radius tinggi. | Pisahkan runtime role dan migration role. |
| Changeset lama diedit setelah deploy | Checksum mismatch atau audit ambiguity. | Buat changeset baru kecuali `runOnChange` memang disengaja. |

---

## 31. Practical Review Checklist

Gunakan checklist ini untuk setiap PR Liquibase:

| Pertanyaan | Wajib Dicek |
|---|---|
| Apakah changeset ID unik dan deskriptif? | Ya |
| Apakah `author` mencerminkan team/service ownership? | Ya |
| Apakah changelog include order benar? | Ya |
| Apakah changeset destructive? | Kalau ya, butuh approval. |
| Apakah ada context/label yang benar? | Terutama seed/test data. |
| Apakah precondition dibutuhkan? | Untuk legacy/conditional state. |
| Apakah rollback realistis? | Jangan hanya formalitas. |
| Apakah MyBatis mapper terdampak? | Wajib test. |
| Apakah Jersey API contract terdampak? | Wajib review. |
| Apakah Camunda process instance terdampak? | Wajib review jika domain data dipakai process. |
| Apakah PostgreSQL object dependency aman? | View/function/trigger/materialized view. |
| Apakah ada `runInTransaction=false`? | Wajib review khusus. |
| Apakah ada `runOnChange=true`? | Wajib review dependency. |

---

## 32. Minimal Production Standard

Sebelum Liquibase dipakai production, minimal harus ada:

```text id="1s2xnj"
1. Master changelog deterministic.
2. Folder convention untuk table/index/seed/view/function/trigger.
3. Role migration terpisah dari role runtime.
4. liquibase.properties tanpa production secret di repo.
5. CI validate.
6. CI updateSQL artifact.
7. CI migration test ke ephemeral PostgreSQL.
8. MyBatis integration test.
9. Context/label policy untuk seed.
10. Precondition policy untuk risky changeset.
11. Rollback / roll-forward policy.
12. Failure SOP untuk lock, checksum mismatch, partial migration, dan rollback.
13. Kubernetes execution model one-time per release.
```

---

## 33. Ringkasan Seri 4

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| Liquibase core | Changelog berisi ordered changesets. |
| Tracking | Liquibase memakai `DATABASECHANGELOG`; lock memakai `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK`. |
| Modeled changeset | Cocok untuk table, column, FK, constraint sederhana. |
| `sqlFile` | Cocok untuk PostgreSQL-heavy object: view, materialized view, function, procedure, trigger function. |
| `runOnChange` | Cocok untuk definitional object yang perlu rerun saat file berubah. |
| Context/label | Penting untuk environment-specific migration dan seed data. |
| Preconditions | Guard agar migration hanya jalan saat state DB valid. |
| Rollback | Berguna, tetapi production safety tetap perlu roll-forward thinking. |
| `runInTransaction` | Perlu untuk DDL tertentu seperti concurrent index, tetapi harus sangat hati-hati. |
| Kubernetes | Jalankan Liquibase sekali per release, bukan dari semua pod. |
| MyBatis | View/function/procedure adalah contract internal yang harus dites bersama mapper. |

7/13/2026, 7:02:56 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 5 dari 18**: struktur project migration untuk microservices. Seri ini akan lebih banyak membahas governance praktis: folder, naming, ownership, ordering, konflik branch, dan review PR.
