# Seri 5 - Migration Project Structure for Microservices

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana menyusun struktur database migration di repository microservice supaya:

- mudah direview di GitHub PR;
- tidak sering konflik antar branch;
- jelas ownership-nya;
- aman untuk Flyway atau Liquibase;
- cocok untuk PostgreSQL object kompleks seperti **view, materialized view, PL/pgSQL function, procedure, trigger, event trigger**;
- kompatibel dengan **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + Camunda**;
- siap dipakai dalam CI/CD dan Kubernetes deployment.

Intinya: migration structure bukan urusan kosmetik folder. Struktur yang buruk akan menyebabkan:

```text id="n0fh7p"
- migration version conflict
- checksum mismatch
- object dependency rusak
- seed data bocor ke environment salah
- repeatable object berubah tanpa test
- app deploy dan DB deploy tidak sinkron
- incident recovery sulit diaudit
```

Flyway menyimpan migration history, checksum, dan status eksekusi di schema history table sebagai audit trail. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-schema-history-table-273973417.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Liquibase changeset juga punya identitas berdasarkan `id`, `author`, dan path changelog; perubahan path bisa memengaruhi identitas changeset sehingga `logicalFilePath` diperlukan saat memindahkan atau mengganti nama changelog. ([docs.liquibase.com](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-0/changelog-attributes/logicalfilepath?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Core Principle

```text id="0lrc2i"
Database migration repository harus menjawab 5 pertanyaan:

1. Perubahan ini milik service/domain mana?
2. Perubahan ini harus jalan sebelum atau sesudah apa?
3. Perubahan ini structural, definitional, seed, atau operational?
4. Perubahan ini aman untuk rolling deployment?
5. Perubahan ini bisa diaudit dan direcover jika gagal?
```

Kalau struktur migration tidak menjawab lima hal itu, struktur tersebut belum production-ready.

---

## 3. Recommended High-Level Repository Shape

Untuk satu microservice:

```text id="1qr54z"
case-service/
├─ src/main/java/
├─ src/main/resources/
│  ├─ db/
│  │  ├─ migration/                 # Flyway style
│  │  │  ├─ versioned/
│  │  │  ├─ repeatable/
│  │  │  └─ callback/
│  │  │
│  │  └─ changelog/                 # Liquibase style
│  │     ├─ db.changelog-master.yaml
│  │     ├─ tables/
│  │     ├─ indexes/
│  │     ├─ constraints/
│  │     ├─ seed/
│  │     ├─ views/
│  │     ├─ materialized-views/
│  │     ├─ functions/
│  │     ├─ procedures/
│  │     ├─ triggers/
│  │     └─ governance/
│  │
│  └─ mybatis/
│     └─ mapper/
│
├─ src/test/
├─ docker-compose.yml
├─ pom.xml
└─ docs/
   └─ database/
      ├─ migration-runbook.md
      ├─ migration-review-policy.md
      └─ rollback-rollforward-policy.md
```

Dalam praktik, biasanya pilih salah satu: **Flyway** atau **Liquibase**, bukan keduanya dalam satu service. Struktur di atas menunjukkan dua alternatif.

---

# Part A - Struktur untuk Flyway

## 4. Flyway Structure Option 1 - Simple Default

Untuk service kecil sampai menengah:

```text id="f7qlw6"
src/main/resources/db/migration/
├─ V001__create_case_schema.sql
├─ V002__create_case_status_reference.sql
├─ V003__create_enforcement_case_table.sql
├─ V004__add_enforcement_case_indexes.sql
├─ V005__seed_case_status_reference.sql
├─ R__view_case_summary.sql
├─ R__fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
├─ R__proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
└─ R__trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
```

### Kelebihan

| Kelebihan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Sederhana | Developer baru cepat paham. |
| Convention mengikuti Flyway | File langsung terlihat sebagai migration. |
| PR review mudah | Semua perubahan DB ada di satu folder. |

### Kekurangan

| Kekurangan | Dampak |
|---|---|
| Folder cepat penuh | Sulit navigasi jika ratusan migration. |
| Object type campur | Table, view, function, trigger tercampur. |
| Repeatable dependency kurang jelas | Urutan dependency perlu dijaga lewat nama file. |

Flyway repeatable migration selalu dijalankan setelah pending versioned migrations, dan repeatable migrations dijalankan berdasarkan urutan description secara alfabetis dalam satu migration run. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repeatable-migrations-273973335.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5. Flyway Structure Option 2 - Separated by Migration Type

Untuk service yang mulai kompleks:

```text id="a2t60h"
src/main/resources/db/migration/
├─ versioned/
│  ├─ V001__create_case_schema.sql
│  ├─ V002__create_case_status_reference.sql
│  ├─ V003__create_enforcement_case_table.sql
│  ├─ V004__add_case_constraints.sql
│  ├─ V005__add_case_indexes.sql
│  └─ V006__seed_case_status_reference.sql
│
└─ repeatable/
   ├─ R__001_view_case_summary.sql
   ├─ R__010_fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
   ├─ R__020_proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
   └─ R__030_trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
```

Konfigurasi Flyway perlu menyertakan kedua lokasi:

```properties id="1gdef7"
flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable
```

### Kapan Cocok?

| Kondisi | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Banyak table migration | Ya |
| Banyak PL/pgSQL function | Ya |
| Banyak view/reporting object | Ya |
| Banyak seed/reference data | Ya |
| Team kecil dan service sederhana | Mungkin overkill |

---

## 6. Flyway Structure Option 3 - Domain-Oriented

Untuk service besar dengan beberapa bounded context:

```text id="jg5vsn"
src/main/resources/db/migration/
├─ case/
│  ├─ versioned/
│  │  ├─ V001001__case_create_tables.sql
│  │  ├─ V001002__case_add_indexes.sql
│  │  └─ V001003__case_seed_reference.sql
│  └─ repeatable/
│     ├─ R__case_view_summary.sql
│     └─ R__case_fn_calculate_sla.sql
│
├─ assignment/
│  ├─ versioned/
│  │  ├─ V002001__assignment_create_tables.sql
│  │  └─ V002002__assignment_add_indexes.sql
│  └─ repeatable/
│     └─ R__assignment_view_workload.sql
│
└─ audit/
   ├─ versioned/
   │  ├─ V003001__audit_create_tables.sql
   │  └─ V003002__audit_create_triggers.sql
   └─ repeatable/
      └─ R__audit_trg_fn_capture_change.sql
```

### Version Number Range per Domain

| Domain | Version Range |
|---|---:|
| Case | `V001001`-`V001999` |
| Assignment | `V002001`-`V002999` |
| Audit | `V003001`-`V003999` |
| Reporting | `V004001`-`V004999` |
| Integration | `V005001`-`V005999` |

### Trade-off

Domain-oriented structure mengurangi konflik naming, tetapi tetap harus memastikan **urutan global migration benar**. Flyway tetap mengeksekusi versioned migration berdasarkan version ordering, bukan urutan folder semata.

---

## 7. Flyway Naming Convention

### Recommended

```text id="5tt5ja"
V<major><minor><patch>__<domain>_<object>_<action>.sql
R__<domain>_<object_type>_<object_name>.sql
```

Contoh:

```text id="2z5f8g"
V001001__case_schema_create.sql
V001002__case_status_reference_create_table.sql
V001003__case_enforcement_case_create_table.sql
V001004__case_enforcement_case_add_status_fk.sql
V001005__case_status_reference_seed.sql

R__case_view_case_summary.sql
R__case_fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
R__case_proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
R__case_trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
```

### Naming Rule

| Rule | Reason |
|---|---|
| Nama harus menjelaskan domain | Mudah cari ownership. |
| Nama harus menjelaskan object | Review cepat. |
| Nama harus menjelaskan action | Intent jelas. |
| Jangan pakai `fix`, `update`, `misc` | Tidak audit-friendly. |
| Repeatable file harus stabil | Rename repeatable bisa dianggap object lifecycle berubah. |
| Versioned file immutable setelah merge ke shared env | Mencegah checksum mismatch. |

Flyway migration naming menggunakan prefix untuk menentukan tipe file, seperti `V` untuk versioned dan `R` untuk repeatable; naming pattern yang benar penting agar Flyway mengenali file. ([red-gate.com](https://www.red-gate.com/blog/database-devops/flyway-naming-patterns-matter/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Flyway Object Type Mapping

| Object Type | Folder | Prefix | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| Schema | `versioned` | `V` | `V001001__case_schema_create.sql` |
| Table | `versioned` | `V` | `V001003__case_enforcement_case_create_table.sql` |
| Column | `versioned` | `V` | `V001010__case_enforcement_case_add_risk_score.sql` |
| Constraint | `versioned` | `V` | `V001011__case_enforcement_case_add_risk_score_check.sql` |
| Index | `versioned` | `V` | `V001012__case_enforcement_case_add_created_at_index.sql` |
| Seed once | `versioned` | `V` | `V001020__case_status_seed.sql` |
| View | `repeatable` | `R` | `R__case_view_case_summary.sql` |
| Materialized view definition | `repeatable` | `R` | `R__case_matview_daily_metrics.sql` |
| Materialized view index | `versioned` | `V` | `V001030__case_daily_metrics_add_unique_index.sql` |
| Function | `repeatable` | `R` | `R__case_fn_calculate_sla.sql` |
| Procedure | `repeatable` | `R` | `R__case_proc_refresh_metrics.sql` |
| Trigger function | `repeatable` | `R` | `R__case_trg_fn_audit_change.sql` |
| Trigger binding | `versioned` | `V` | `V001040__case_create_audit_trigger.sql` |
| Event trigger | `versioned` | `V` | `V009001__governance_create_ddl_audit_event_trigger.sql` |

---

# Part B - Struktur untuk Liquibase

## 9. Liquibase Recommended Structure

```text id="vftqjb"
src/main/resources/db/changelog/
├─ db.changelog-master.yaml
│
├─ 000-bootstrap/
│  ├─ 001-create-schemas.yaml
│  └─ 002-create-extensions.yaml
│
├─ 100-tables/
│  ├─ 101-case-status-reference.yaml
│  ├─ 102-enforcement-case.yaml
│  └─ 103-case-assignment.yaml
│
├─ 200-indexes/
│  ├─ 201-enforcement-case-indexes.yaml
│  └─ 202-case-assignment-indexes.yaml
│
├─ 300-constraints/
│  ├─ 301-enforcement-case-fk.yaml
│  └─ 302-case-assignment-fk.yaml
│
├─ 400-seed/
│  ├─ 401-case-status-reference.yaml
│  └─ 402-assignment-role-reference.yaml
│
├─ 500-views/
│  ├─ 501-v-case-summary.yaml
│  └─ sql/
│     └─ v_case_summary.sql
│
├─ 600-materialized-views/
│  ├─ 601-mv-case-daily-metrics.yaml
│  └─ sql/
│     └─ mv_case_daily_metrics.sql
│
├─ 700-functions/
│  ├─ 701-fn-calculate-case-sla.yaml
│  └─ sql/
│     └─ fn_calculate_case_sla.sql
│
├─ 800-procedures/
│  ├─ 801-proc-refresh-case-metrics.yaml
│  └─ sql/
│     └─ proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
│
├─ 900-triggers/
│  ├─ 901-trg-fn-case-audit.yaml
│  ├─ 902-create-case-audit-trigger.yaml
│  └─ sql/
│     └─ trg_fn_case_audit.sql
│
└─ 990-governance/
   ├─ 991-event-trigger-ddl-audit.yaml
   └─ sql/
      └─ event_trigger_ddl_audit.sql
```

### Kenapa Ada Prefix Angka Folder?

Karena Liquibase mengeksekusi changeset sesuai urutan changelog include dan urutan changeset di dalam changelog. Prefix folder membantu manusia membaca urutan dependency.

---

## 10. Liquibase Master Changelog

```yaml id="sr3yuj"
databaseChangeLog:
  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/000-bootstrap/001-create-schemas.yaml

  - include:
      file: src/main/resources/db/changelog/000-bootstrap/002-create-extensions.yaml

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/100-tables

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/200-indexes

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/300-constraints

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/400-seed

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/500-views

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/600-materialized-views

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/700-functions

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/800-procedures

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/900-triggers

  - includeAll:
      path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/990-governance
```

### Caution: `includeAll`

`includeAll` nyaman, tetapi harus hati-hati. Untuk production-grade system, saya lebih suka:

```text id="ujusfr"
Explicit include untuk dependency-sensitive object.
includeAll hanya untuk folder yang ordering-nya benar-benar aman.
```

Contoh dependency-sensitive:

- trigger binding butuh trigger function sudah ada;
- view butuh table/function sudah ada;
- materialized view index butuh materialized view sudah ada;
- FK butuh parent table sudah ada;
- event trigger function butuh function sudah ada.

---

## 11. Liquibase Changeset Identity and File Movement

Liquibase changeset identity bergantung pada `id`, `author`, dan changelog file path. Dokumentasi Liquibase menjelaskan `logicalFilePath` dipakai untuk override file name/path saat membuat unique identifier changeset; ini diperlukan ketika changelog dipindahkan atau di-rename supaya changeset tidak dianggap baru dan dideploy ulang. ([docs.liquibase.com](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-0/changelog-attributes/logicalfilepath?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Practical Rule

```text id="m8x4lr"
Setelah changeset masuk shared environment:
  - jangan rename file changelog sembarangan;
  - jangan pindah folder sembarangan;
  - jika harus pindah, gunakan logicalFilePath;
  - dokumentasikan alasan refactor.
```

Contoh:

```yaml id="z1vd03"
databaseChangeLog:
  - logicalFilePath: db/changelog/legacy/101-enforcement-case.yaml

  - changeSet:
      id: 101-case-enforcement-case-create-table
      author: case-service-team
      changes:
        - createTable:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
            columns:
              - column:
                  name: id
                  type: uuid
```

---

## 12. Liquibase Object Type Mapping

| Object Type | Recommended Folder | Changeset Style |
|---|---|---|
| Schema | `000-bootstrap` | YAML or SQL |
| Extension | `000-bootstrap` | SQL with precondition |
| Table | `100-tables` | YAML modeled changeset |
| Index | `200-indexes` | YAML or SQL |
| Concurrent index | `200-indexes` | SQL + `runInTransaction: false` |
| Constraint | `300-constraints` | YAML modeled or SQL |
| Seed | `400-seed` | SQL with context/label |
| View | `500-views` | YAML wrapper + `sqlFile` + `runOnChange` |
| Materialized view | `600-materialized-views` | YAML wrapper + `sqlFile` + runbook |
| Function | `700-functions` | YAML wrapper + `sqlFile` + `runOnChange` |
| Procedure | `800-procedures` | YAML wrapper + `sqlFile` + `runOnChange` |
| Trigger function | `900-triggers` | YAML wrapper + `sqlFile` + `runOnChange` |
| Trigger binding | `900-triggers` | Versioned YAML/SQL changeset |
| Event trigger | `990-governance` | Versioned YAML/SQL + approval |

---

# Part C - Ownership Model

## 13. Ownership per Bounded Context

Database object harus punya owner.

```text id="vyhjjv"
case-service database ownership
────────────────────────────────

case domain
  ├─ enforcement_case
  ├─ case_status_reference
  ├─ v_case_summary
  └─ fn_calculate_case_sla

assignment domain
  ├─ case_assignment
  ├─ assignment_role_reference
  └─ v_assignment_workload

audit domain
  ├─ case_audit_log
  ├─ trg_fn_case_audit
  └─ trg_case_audit
```

### Ownership Table

| Object | Owner | Reviewer |
|---|---|---|
| Domain table | Owning feature team | Senior engineer / domain owner |
| Reference data | Domain owner + business owner | Tech lead |
| View used by API | API owner + DB owner | Senior engineer |
| View used by reporting | Reporting owner | Data/DB reviewer |
| Function/procedure | DB logic owner | Senior engineer |
| Trigger | Domain owner + DB owner | Tech lead |
| Event trigger | Platform/DB governance owner | Architecture review |

### Rule

```text id="k9ch3j"
No orphan database object.
Every table/view/function/procedure/trigger must have a known owner.
```

---

## 14. Ownership Metadata in Migration

### Flyway

Flyway SQL file bisa punya header comment:

```sql id="r7etsn"
-- Migration: V001003__case_enforcement_case_create_table.sql
-- Owner: case-service-team
-- Reviewer: case-domain-owner
-- Risk: medium
-- Compatibility: backward-compatible
-- Related: CaseMapper.xml, CaseResource.java

CREATE TABLE case_app.enforcement_case (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    case_number TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Liquibase

Liquibase bisa memakai `author`, `labels`, dan `comments`:

```yaml id="pqyxms"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 101-case-enforcement-case-create-table
      author: case-service-team
      labels: domain-case,table,api-contract
      comments: >
        Creates primary case table used by CaseMapper and CaseResource.
      changes:
        - createTable:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
```

---

# Part D - Ordering Strategy

## 15. Dependency Ordering

Urutan migration harus mengikuti dependency object.

```text id="rd8o0z"
Recommended Order
────────────────────────────────

1. Schema / extension
2. Reference tables
3. Domain tables
4. Columns
5. Seed base reference data
6. Constraints / FK
7. Indexes
8. Functions
9. Views
10. Materialized views
11. Materialized view indexes
12. Trigger functions
13. Trigger bindings
14. Procedures / jobs
15. Permission grants
16. Release tags / audit marker
```

### Kenapa Function Sebelum View?

Karena view bisa memanggil function.

```sql id="36mdig"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    c.id,
    case_app.fn_calculate_case_age_days(c.created_at) AS age_days
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c;
```

Jika function belum ada, view creation gagal.

### Kenapa Trigger Function Sebelum Trigger Binding?

Karena trigger binding butuh function sudah ada:

```sql id="b9hdog"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON case_app.enforcement_case
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at();
```

---

## 16. Versioned vs Repeatable Ordering

Untuk Flyway:

```text id="3c0fdi"
Versioned migration:
  berjalan berdasarkan urutan version.

Repeatable migration:
  berjalan setelah pending versioned migrations.
```

Dokumentasi Flyway menyebut repeatable migration dijalankan setelah semua pending versioned migration, lalu urut berdasarkan description secara alfabetis. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repeatable-migrations-273973335.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Implikasi

Kalau view repeatable bergantung pada function repeatable, nama file harus membuat urutan benar:

```text id="n6ze4g"
R__010_fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
R__020_view_case_summary.sql
```

Jangan:

```text id="sqh3v6"
R__view_case_summary.sql
R__fn_calculate_case_age_days.sql
```

Karena urutan alfabetis bisa membuat view dieksekusi sebelum function, tergantung deskripsi/naming.

---

## 17. Dependency Graph Example

```text id="w0igcq"
case_status_reference
        │
        ▼
enforcement_case
        │
        ├──────────────┐
        ▼              ▼
fn_calculate_sla     trg_fn_case_audit
        │              │
        ▼              ▼
v_case_summary      trg_case_audit
        │
        ▼
mv_case_daily_metrics
        │
        ▼
proc_refresh_case_metrics
```

### Mapping ke File

```text id="jojc93"
V001001__case_status_reference_create_table.sql
V001002__enforcement_case_create_table.sql
V001003__enforcement_case_add_status_fk.sql
V001004__case_audit_log_create_table.sql

R__010_fn_calculate_sla.sql
R__020_trg_fn_case_audit.sql
V001005__case_create_audit_trigger.sql
R__030_view_case_summary.sql
R__040_matview_case_daily_metrics.sql
V001006__case_daily_metrics_add_unique_index.sql
R__050_proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
```

---

# Part E - Branch Conflict Strategy

## 18. Problem: Version Collision

Dua engineer membuat migration bersamaan:

```text id="sxt8cv"
PR A:
  V001010__case_add_risk_score.sql

PR B:
  V001010__case_add_priority_reason.sql
```

Keduanya valid di branch masing-masing, tapi conflict saat merge.

---

## 19. Strategy 1 - Sequential Version Rebase

Policy:

```text id="ctscwo"
Sebelum merge:
  - rebase ke main
  - cek migration version terakhir
  - rename migration jika version collision
  - rerun migration test dari fresh DB
```

Kelebihan:

- sederhana;
- cocok untuk tim kecil/menengah;
- history linear.

Kekurangan:

- PR sering perlu rename migration;
- checksum bisa berubah sebelum migration masuk shared environment;
- butuh disiplin.

---

## 20. Strategy 2 - Timestamp Versioning

Contoh:

```text id="qf1rnz"
V202607130930__case_add_risk_score.sql
V202607131015__case_add_priority_reason.sql
```

Kelebihan:

- collision lebih jarang;
- cocok untuk banyak engineer.

Kekurangan:

- dependency ordering bisa tidak selalu intuitif;
- timestamp dari local timezone bisa membingungkan;
- PR lama bisa punya version lebih kecil dari PR baru tapi merge belakangan.

### Rule Jika Pakai Timestamp

```text id="bdtv5g"
Use UTC timestamp:
  VYYYYMMDDHHMM__description.sql

Jangan pakai timezone lokal developer.
```

Karena user berada di Asia/Jakarta, tetapi CI/CD dan engineer lain mungkin tidak. Untuk file migration, gunakan UTC atau standard perusahaan, bukan timezone personal.

---

## 21. Strategy 3 - Domain Range Versioning

Contoh:

```text id="t9uusu"
Case domain:
  V001001 - V001999

Assignment domain:
  V002001 - V002999

Audit domain:
  V003001 - V003999
```

Kelebihan:

- collision antar domain berkurang;
- ownership jelas.

Kekurangan:

- dependency lintas domain perlu dijaga;
- range bisa habis;
- ordering global bisa membingungkan.

Cocok jika service besar dan domain ownership sudah stabil.

---

## 22. Strategy 4 - Migration Registry

Buat file registry:

```text id="0msqnv"
docs/database/migration-registry.md
```

Isi:

```md id="dey47n"
# Migration Registry

| Version | Owner | PR | Status | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| V001010 | case-team | #312 | merged | add risk score |
| V001011 | case-team | #318 | reserved | add priority reason |
| V001012 | audit-team | #320 | in review | create audit trigger |
```

Kelebihan:

- controlled;
- bagus untuk regulated system;
- menghindari collision.

Kekurangan:

- overhead;
- bisa menjadi bottleneck jika terlalu manual.

---

# Part F - Repeatable Object Governance

## 23. Repeatable Migration Is Not “Free Edit”

Repeatable object seperti view/function/procedure memang bisa dijalankan ulang saat checksum berubah. Tetapi itu tetap bisa breaking.

Flyway repeatable migration akan dijalankan ulang jika checksum berubah, dan developer bertanggung jawab memastikan repeatable migration bisa diterapkan berkali-kali, biasanya memakai `CREATE OR REPLACE`. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repeatable-migrations-273973335.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Liquibase `runOnChange` juga membandingkan checksum changeset dengan checksum di `DATABASECHANGELOG` untuk menentukan apakah changeset berubah. ([docs.liquibase.com](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-1-1/changelog-attributes/runonchange?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Risk Matrix

| Repeatable Object | Risk | Required Review |
|---|---|---|
| View | Mapper/reporting bisa rusak. | MyBatis mapper test + API contract check. |
| Materialized view | Data stale / refresh cost / index dependency. | Performance + refresh strategy review. |
| Function | Return type/semantic change bisa breaking. | Caller search + integration test. |
| Procedure | Operational side effect bisa berubah. | Runbook + privilege review. |
| Trigger function | Silent data mutation bisa berubah. | Data integrity review. |
| Reference data repeatable | Bisa overwrite operational config. | Business owner approval. |

---

## 24. Repeatable Naming with Dependency Order

Untuk Flyway:

```text id="l4hc9r"
R__010_fn_calculate_case_sla.sql
R__020_fn_format_case_status.sql
R__030_view_case_summary.sql
R__040_matview_case_daily_metrics.sql
R__050_proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
R__060_trg_fn_case_audit.sql
```

Untuk Liquibase:

```text id="v4ho4k"
700-functions/
  701-fn-calculate-case-sla.yaml
  702-fn-format-case-status.yaml

500-views/
  501-v-case-summary.yaml

600-materialized-views/
  601-mv-case-daily-metrics.yaml

800-procedures/
  801-proc-refresh-case-metrics.yaml
```

### Rule

```text id="w1zgg9"
Repeatable object names should encode dependency order.
Not because database needs pretty names,
but because migration runner needs deterministic execution.
```

---

# Part G - Seed Data Structure

## 25. Seed Data Categories

Tidak semua seed data sama.

| Seed Type | Example | Folder | Risk |
|---|---|---|---|
| Reference data | status, priority, type | `seed/reference` | Medium |
| Permission data | roles, permissions, ACL defaults | `seed/security` | High |
| App config | feature config, SLA config | `seed/config` | High |
| Test fixture | dummy user/case | `seed/test` | High if leaked |
| Demo data | demo case/workflow | `seed/demo` | High if leaked |
| Migration support seed | temp mapping table | `seed/migration-support` | Medium |

---

## 26. Recommended Seed Structure

### Flyway

```text id="4gx1fn"
src/main/resources/db/migration/versioned/
├─ V001020__seed_reference_case_status.sql
├─ V001021__seed_reference_case_priority.sql
├─ V001022__seed_security_case_permissions.sql
└─ V001023__seed_config_case_sla.sql

src/test/resources/db/seed/
├─ test_case_fixture.sql
└─ test_user_fixture.sql
```

### Liquibase

```text id="x0qe90"
400-seed/
├─ reference/
│  ├─ 401-case-status.yaml
│  └─ 402-case-priority.yaml
├─ security/
│  └─ 421-case-permissions.yaml
├─ config/
│  └─ 431-case-sla-config.yaml
└─ test/
   └─ 491-test-fixtures.yaml
```

### Rule

```text id="05kkqv"
Production seed and test seed must never live in the same uncontrolled execution path.
```

---

## 27. Idempotent Seed Pattern

PostgreSQL:

```sql id="2yhpce"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
VALUES
    ('OPEN', 'Open', 10, true),
    ('CLOSED', 'Closed', 20, true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

### Warning

`ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` bagus untuk reference data, tetapi berbahaya jika dipakai ke data operasional.

| Data Type | `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` |
|---|---|
| Static reference data | Cocok |
| Permission catalog | Cocok, dengan review |
| App config | Hati-hati |
| User data | Tidak cocok sebagai seed |
| Case data production | Tidak cocok |
| Audit data | Tidak cocok |

---

# Part H - Review and CI Structure

## 28. PR Folder Impact Map

Saat PR menyentuh folder tertentu, reviewer harus tahu area yang terdampak.

| Changed Path | Required Checks |
|---|---|
| `db/migration/**V*.sql` | Migration order, destructive change, rollback/roll-forward. |
| `db/migration/**R*.sql` | Dependency, caller impact, repeatability. |
| `db/changelog/tables/**` | MyBatis mapper and API compatibility. |
| `db/changelog/indexes/**` | Query plan, lock, concurrent index need. |
| `db/changelog/seed/**` | Environment targeting and business approval. |
| `db/changelog/views/**` | Mapper/reporting contract test. |
| `db/changelog/functions/**` | Caller search and PL/pgSQL test. |
| `db/changelog/procedures/**` | Operational runbook. |
| `db/changelog/triggers/**` | Data mutation/integrity review. |
| `db/changelog/materialized-views/**` | Refresh and stale data review. |
| `mybatis/mapper/**` | Schema compatibility test. |
| `api/**` or `resource/**` | API contract compatibility. |

---

## 29. GitHub PR Template

```md id="kwqbzs"
## Database Migration PR

### Migration Type
- [ ] Schema / table
- [ ] Column
- [ ] Constraint / FK
- [ ] Index
- [ ] Seed / reference data
- [ ] View
- [ ] Materialized view
- [ ] Function / procedure
- [ ] Trigger / event trigger
- [ ] Backfill / data repair
- [ ] Permission / grant

### Tool
- [ ] Flyway
- [ ] Liquibase

### Compatibility
- [ ] Backward-compatible with currently deployed app
- [ ] Requires migration before app deploy
- [ ] Requires app deploy before migration
- [ ] Requires expand/contract rollout
- [ ] Breaking change

### Impacted Code
- [ ] MyBatis mapper
- [ ] Jersey/JAX-RS resource
- [ ] Service layer
- [ ] Camunda worker/process
- [ ] Reporting/query consumers
- [ ] External API clients

### Data Risk
- [ ] Destructive DDL
- [ ] Large table
- [ ] Long-running query
- [ ] Lock risk
- [ ] Backfill
- [ ] Seed overwrite
- [ ] Trigger side effect

### Verification
Describe:
- local migration test
- CI migration test
- mapper/API test
- production smoke test

### Rollback / Roll-forward Plan
Describe the recovery path.

### Reviewer Notes
Mention required reviewer:
- DB owner
- domain owner
- security owner
- platform owner
```

---

## 30. CI Workflow by Structure

```text id="o950p6"
CI Pipeline
────────────────────────────────

1. Detect changed files
2. Validate migration naming
3. Validate migration tool metadata
4. Start ephemeral PostgreSQL
5. Run migration from empty DB
6. Run migration validate/info/status
7. Run MyBatis integration tests
8. Run Jersey resource/service tests
9. Run PL/pgSQL object tests
10. Run destructive-change policy check
11. Publish migration SQL/log artifact
```

### Additional Policy Checks

| Rule | Automation |
|---|---|
| No `DROP TABLE` without approval label | grep/lint + PR label |
| No `TRUNCATE` in production migration | grep/lint |
| No `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` inside transaction | migration-specific config check |
| No test seed in prod path | path/context validation |
| No editing applied migration | compare against main/deployed baseline |
| No repeatable object without test | path-based required test check |
| No app startup migration in prod | config scan |

---

# Part I - Documentation Structure

## 31. Required Docs

```text id="ntmueu"
docs/database/
├─ migration-runbook.md
├─ migration-review-policy.md
├─ rollback-rollforward-policy.md
├─ seed-data-policy.md
├─ object-ownership.md
├─ materialized-view-refresh-policy.md
└─ migration-incident-sop.md
```

### `migration-runbook.md`

Should answer:

```text id="8uzaxz"
- How to run migration locally
- How to run migration in CI
- How production migration is executed
- Who approves destructive migration
- How to inspect migration history
- How to respond to failed migration
```

### `object-ownership.md`

Should list:

```text id="whai9z"
- Tables
- Views
- Materialized views
- Functions
- Procedures
- Triggers
- Seed data
- Owners and reviewers
```

---

## 32. Minimum Object Ownership Table

```md id="kvdsrn"
| Object | Type | Owner | Reviewer | Consumer | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| case_app.enforcement_case | table | case-team | tech-lead | CaseMapper | Core domain table |
| case_app.v_case_summary | view | case-team | db-reviewer | CaseMapper, reporting | Internal read contract |
| case_app.fn_calculate_case_sla | function | case-team | db-reviewer | v_case_summary | Used in query projection |
| case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics | materialized view | reporting-team | db-reviewer | dashboard | Refresh every 15 min |
| case_app.trg_case_audit | trigger | audit-team | tech-lead | audit log | Captures UPDATE changes |
```

---

# Part J - Anti-Patterns

## 33. Structural Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Buruk | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Semua migration di satu folder tanpa convention | Sulit navigasi dan review. | Prefix + type/domain folder. |
| Nama file `fix.sql`, `update.sql`, `misc.sql` | Tidak audit-friendly. | Nama menjelaskan domain/object/action. |
| Rename applied migration file | Bisa memicu checksum/path identity issue. | Buat migration baru; untuk Liquibase gunakan `logicalFilePath` jika refactor wajib. |
| Repeatable view/function tanpa order prefix | Dependency bisa salah urut. | `R__010_fn...`, `R__020_view...`. |
| Test seed di folder production migration | Data dummy bisa bocor. | Pisahkan test seed path/context. |
| Trigger function dan trigger binding dalam satu file besar | Sulit rollback dan dependency kabur. | Function repeatable, binding versioned. |
| Materialized view definition, index, refresh campur satu file | Recovery sulit. | Definition, index, refresh strategy dipisah. |
| Migration folder tidak punya README | Developer baru menebak-nebak. | Tambahkan runbook singkat. |

---

## 34. Process Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Migration review hanya oleh feature author | Blind spot operasional. | DB/domain reviewer. |
| Tidak ada changed-path based test | View/function bisa berubah tanpa test. | CI path-based required tests. |
| Manual hotfix DDL tidak dibackfill ke repo | Environment drift. | Buat corrective migration segera. |
| Tidak ada migration registry | Version conflict sering. | Registry atau timestamp/domain range. |
| Mengandalkan rollback tool | False confidence. | Roll-forward-first policy. |
| App pod menjalankan migration | Race dan privilege issue. | One-time migration job. |
| Tidak ada owner object | Incident lambat. | Object ownership registry. |

---

# Part K - Recommended Standard untuk Stack Ini

## 35. Jika Memakai Flyway

Saya rekomendasikan:

```text id="jjm80x"
src/main/resources/db/migration/
├─ versioned/
│  ├─ V001001__case_schema_create.sql
│  ├─ V001002__case_status_reference_create_table.sql
│  ├─ V001003__case_enforcement_case_create_table.sql
│  ├─ V001004__case_enforcement_case_add_constraints.sql
│  ├─ V001005__case_enforcement_case_add_indexes.sql
│  └─ V001006__case_status_reference_seed.sql
│
└─ repeatable/
   ├─ R__010_case_fn_calculate_case_sla.sql
   ├─ R__020_case_view_case_summary.sql
   ├─ R__030_case_matview_daily_metrics.sql
   ├─ R__040_case_proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
   └─ R__050_case_trg_fn_case_audit.sql
```

Config:

```properties id="9jc70m"
flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable
```

---

## 36. Jika Memakai Liquibase

Saya rekomendasikan:

```text id="5k83ru"
src/main/resources/db/changelog/
├─ db.changelog-master.yaml
├─ 000-bootstrap/
├─ 100-tables/
├─ 200-indexes/
├─ 300-constraints/
├─ 400-seed/
├─ 500-views/
├─ 600-materialized-views/
├─ 700-functions/
├─ 800-procedures/
├─ 900-triggers/
└─ 990-governance/
```

Guideline:

```text id="m1rjfw"
- Modeled YAML untuk table/column/constraint sederhana.
- sqlFile untuk view/materialized view/function/procedure/trigger function.
- runOnChange hanya untuk definitional object.
- runInTransaction=false hanya untuk DDL khusus seperti concurrent index.
- logicalFilePath saat changelog lama harus dipindah.
```

---

## 37. Practical Decision

Untuk microservice **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL**, standar yang paling balanced:

```text id="y0m831"
Primary:
  Flyway with separated versioned/repeatable folders

Add:
  PR template
  object ownership registry
  migration runbook
  CI ephemeral PostgreSQL migration test
  MyBatis integration test
  path-based review rules

Use Liquibase:
  jika organisasi butuh context/label/precondition/rollback governance formal.
```

---

## 38. Ringkasan Seri 5

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| Struktur migration | Harus mencerminkan ownership, dependency, dan risk profile. |
| Flyway simple structure | Cocok untuk service kecil. |
| Flyway separated structure | Cocok untuk service yang punya banyak view/function/procedure. |
| Flyway domain range | Cocok untuk service besar dengan bounded context jelas. |
| Liquibase structure | Gunakan folder berdasarkan object lifecycle dan dependency. |
| Versioned migration | Untuk perubahan structural dan historis. |
| Repeatable migration | Untuk definitional object, tetapi tetap wajib test. |
| Seed data | Pisahkan reference, config, security, test, dan demo seed. |
| Branch conflict | Atur lewat rebase, timestamp, domain range, atau registry. |
| Object ownership | Wajib untuk table/view/function/procedure/trigger. |
| CI/CD | Harus path-aware dan menjalankan migration ke ephemeral DB. |
| Documentation | Minimal punya runbook, review policy, rollback policy, object ownership. |

---

**Seri 5 dari 18 selesai.**

Rekomendasi materi selanjutnya:

**Seri 6 - PostgreSQL DDL Migration Safety**

Fokusnya: table, column, constraint, index, lock behavior, transactional DDL, `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`, `NOT NULL`, FK, CHECK, UNIQUE, default value, dan bagaimana membuat perubahan schema aman untuk production traffic.

# Seri 6 - PostgreSQL DDL Migration Safety

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini fokus pada **safety saat mengubah struktur PostgreSQL di production**, terutama untuk microservice berbasis:

```text
Java 17+
Jersey / JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Flyway / Liquibase
Docker / Kubernetes
CI/CD
```

Inti seri ini:

> DDL migration yang “benar secara SQL” belum tentu aman secara production.

Contoh SQL ini valid:

```sql
ALTER TABLE enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER NOT NULL;
```

Tetapi bisa gagal atau mengganggu production jika tabel sudah berisi data, aplikasi lama masih berjalan, atau deploy dilakukan secara rolling.

PostgreSQL menyediakan banyak mekanisme aman seperti `NOT VALID`, `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`, `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`, dan fast default column behavior, tetapi setiap mekanisme punya batasan yang harus dipahami. Misalnya, `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak bisa dijalankan di dalam transaction block, dan failed concurrent index bisa meninggalkan index invalid yang perlu dibersihkan. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

---

## 2. Mental Model: DDL Safety

```text
PostgreSQL DDL Migration Safety
────────────────────────────────────────────

DDL Change
  │
  ├─ Structural Risk
  │    ├─ table rewrite
  │    ├─ index rebuild
  │    ├─ constraint validation
  │    └─ dependency break
  │
  ├─ Concurrency Risk
  │    ├─ lock level
  │    ├─ blocking reads/writes
  │    ├─ long transaction
  │    └─ deadlock / timeout
  │
  ├─ Application Risk
  │    ├─ MyBatis mapper mismatch
  │    ├─ Jersey API contract break
  │    ├─ old pod/new pod compatibility
  │    └─ Camunda running process impact
  │
  └─ Recovery Risk
       ├─ rollback destructive
       ├─ partial migration
       ├─ invalid index
       └─ data inconsistency
```

---

## 3. DDL Risk Classification

| Risk Level | Example | Why Risky | Safer Pattern |
|---|---|---|---|
| Low | Add nullable column | Usually backward-compatible. | Add column first, app reads later. |
| Low-medium | Add constant default | Fast in modern PostgreSQL for constant default, but still review version/behavior. | Add default separately if unsure. |
| Medium | Add index | Can take time and block writes if not concurrent. | Use `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` for large live tables. |
| Medium | Add FK/check constraint | Existing rows must be validated. | Add `NOT VALID`, then `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`. |
| Medium-high | Add `NOT NULL` | Requires checking existing rows unless using supported staged pattern. | Backfill first, validate, then enforce. |
| High | Rename column/table | Breaks old app version and MyBatis mapper. | Expand/contract with compatibility period. |
| High | Change column type | May rewrite table and break dependent objects. | Add new column, backfill, switch app, drop old later. |
| High | Drop column/table | Destructive and breaks old app/query/view/function. | Contract phase only after consumer cleanup. |
| High | Backfill large table | Long transaction, lock pressure, WAL growth. | Chunked/resumable backfill job. |
| High | Trigger behavior change | Silent mutation/change in business invariant. | Test and deploy as contract change. |

---

## 4. Core Rule: Separate Structural Change from Data Change

Jangan campur semua dalam satu migration besar.

Buruk:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;

UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score = expensive_function(id);

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score CHECK (risk_score BETWEEN 0 AND 100);
```

Lebih aman:

```text
Release A
  └─ Add nullable column

Release B
  └─ App writes new column

Release C
  └─ Backfill in chunks

Release D
  └─ Add constraint / NOT NULL

Release E
  └─ Remove old compatibility path
```

Prinsipnya:

```text
DDL kecil, data movement terukur, constraint enforcement bertahap.
```

---

## 5. Adding Column Safely

### 5.1 Safe Basic Add Column

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

Ini biasanya paling aman karena:

- field nullable;
- app lama tidak peduli;
- app baru bisa mulai membaca/menulis;
- backfill bisa dilakukan belakangan.

PostgreSQL documentation menyebut kolom baru awalnya diisi dengan default value yang diberikan, atau `NULL` jika tidak ada default. Untuk constant default, PostgreSQL modern tidak perlu memperbarui setiap row saat `ALTER TABLE` dijalankan; nilai default akan dikembalikan saat row diakses dan diterapkan saat table rewrite berikutnya. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html))

---

### 5.2 Add Column with Constant Default

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN source_system TEXT DEFAULT 'INTERNAL';
```

Ini bisa relatif cepat di PostgreSQL modern jika default adalah constant expression.

Tetapi tetap perlu review:

| Concern | Why |
|---|---|
| PostgreSQL version | Behavior lama bisa berbeda. |
| Default expression | Constant default aman relatif; volatile default tidak. |
| Table size | Lock tetap ada walau singkat. |
| App compatibility | App lama harus tetap jalan. |

PostgreSQL documentation membedakan constant default dengan volatile default seperti `clock_timestamp()`; volatile default mengharuskan setiap row dihitung saat `ALTER TABLE`, sehingga bisa menjadi operasi panjang. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html))

---

### 5.3 Avoid Volatile Default on Large Table

Hindari:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN migration_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT clock_timestamp();
```

Lebih aman:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN migration_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ;
```

Lalu isi dengan backfill terkontrol:

```sql
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET migration_timestamp = now()
WHERE migration_timestamp IS NULL
  AND id IN (
      SELECT id
      FROM case_app.enforcement_case
      WHERE migration_timestamp IS NULL
      ORDER BY id
      LIMIT 1000
  );
```

---

## 6. Adding NOT NULL Safely

### 6.1 Naive Pattern

Buruk untuk tabel existing:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER NOT NULL;
```

Masalah:

- existing rows tidak punya value;
- migration gagal;
- atau butuh default/backfill;
- bisa mengunci table lebih lama dari yang diharapkan.

---

### 6.2 Safe Pattern

```text
1. Add nullable column
2. Deploy app that writes value
3. Backfill old rows
4. Add/validate constraint
5. Set NOT NULL
```

SQL:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

Backfill:

```sql
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score = 0
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

Set not null:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ALTER COLUMN risk_score SET NOT NULL;
```

PostgreSQL `SET NOT NULL` hanya bisa diterapkan jika tidak ada row yang masih `NULL`; secara umum PostgreSQL memeriksa ini dengan scan seluruh table, kecuali memakai mekanisme valid constraint yang bisa membuktikan tidak ada `NULL` atau fitur `NOT VALID` yang tersedia di PostgreSQL saat ini. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

### 6.3 PostgreSQL Version Awareness

PostgreSQL current documentation menyebut `NOT VALID` dapat digunakan untuk foreign key, `CHECK`, dan not-null constraints. Ini penting, tetapi jangan diasumsikan tersedia di semua versi PostgreSQL yang dipakai perusahaan. Validasi versi target di on-prem/AWS/Azure sebelum menetapkan migration standard. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

Untuk organisasi yang masih di PostgreSQL lama, pola umum yang sering dipakai:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_not_null
CHECK (risk_score IS NOT NULL)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_not_null;

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ALTER COLUMN risk_score SET NOT NULL;
```

PostgreSQL current docs menyebut `SET NOT NULL` dapat melewati table scan jika ada valid `CHECK` constraint yang membuktikan tidak ada `NULL`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 7. Adding CHECK Constraint Safely

### Naive

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_range
CHECK (risk_score >= 0 AND risk_score <= 100);
```

Masalah:

- PostgreSQL harus memeriksa existing rows;
- scan table besar bisa lama;
- concurrent update bisa terdampak.

### Safer

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_range
CHECK (risk_score >= 0 AND risk_score <= 100)
NOT VALID;
```

Lalu validate saat traffic lebih aman:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_range;
```

`NOT VALID` membuat scan existing data dilewati saat constraint ditambahkan, tetapi constraint tetap berlaku untuk insert/update berikutnya. Constraint baru dianggap benar untuk seluruh table setelah `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` selesai. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 8. Adding Foreign Key Safely

### Naive

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_enforcement_case_status
FOREIGN KEY (status)
REFERENCES case_app.case_status_reference (code);
```

### Safer

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_enforcement_case_status
FOREIGN KEY (status)
REFERENCES case_app.case_status_reference (code)
NOT VALID;
```

Lalu:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_enforcement_case_status;
```

PostgreSQL documentation memberikan contoh yang sama untuk menambahkan foreign key dengan dampak lebih kecil: `ADD CONSTRAINT ... NOT VALID` lalu `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`. Dokumentasi juga menjelaskan `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` memakai lock `SHARE UPDATE EXCLUSIVE`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

### Operational Checklist

Sebelum validate FK:

```sql
SELECT c.status, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
LEFT JOIN case_app.case_status_reference r
    ON r.code = c.status
WHERE r.code IS NULL
GROUP BY c.status;
```

Jika query menghasilkan row, data harus dibersihkan dulu.

---

## 9. Adding UNIQUE Constraint Safely

### Problem

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT uq_enforcement_case_case_number
UNIQUE (case_number);
```

Pada table besar, membuat unique constraint langsung bisa mahal.

### Safer Pattern

Buat unique index secara concurrent:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY uqidx_enforcement_case_case_number
ON case_app.enforcement_case (case_number);
```

Lalu convert menjadi constraint:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT uq_enforcement_case_case_number
UNIQUE USING INDEX uqidx_enforcement_case_case_number;
```

PostgreSQL documentation menyebut menambahkan constraint memakai existing index bisa membantu saat constraint baru perlu ditambahkan tanpa blocking update terlalu lama; caranya adalah membuat index dengan `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`, lalu mengubahnya menjadi constraint. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

### Precheck Duplicate

```sql
SELECT case_number, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
GROUP BY case_number
HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;
```

Jangan membuat unique index sebelum duplikasi diselesaikan.

---

## 10. Index Migration Safety

### 10.1 Normal Index

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_enforcement_case_status
ON case_app.enforcement_case (status);
```

Untuk table kecil atau maintenance window, ini mungkin cukup.

Tetapi pada table besar dengan traffic aktif, gunakan:

```sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_enforcement_case_status
ON case_app.enforcement_case (status);
```

PostgreSQL menyatakan regular `CREATE INDEX` dapat berjalan dalam transaction block, tetapi `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak bisa. Concurrent index build juga bisa meninggalkan invalid index jika gagal, dan recovery yang direkomendasikan adalah drop invalid index lalu coba lagi, atau rebuild dengan `REINDEX INDEX CONCURRENTLY`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

---

### 10.2 Flyway Pattern for Concurrent Index

Flyway migration khusus:

```sql
-- V001030__case_enforcement_case_status_index_concurrently.sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_enforcement_case_status
ON case_app.enforcement_case (status);
```

Konfigurasi harus memastikan migration ini **tidak dijalankan dalam transaction**.

Rule:

```text
One concurrent index per migration file.
No mixed statements.
No unrelated DDL in the same migration.
```

---

### 10.3 Liquibase Pattern for Concurrent Index

```yaml
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 203-case-enforcement-case-status-index-concurrently
      author: case-service-team
      runInTransaction: false
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_enforcement_case_status
              ON case_app.enforcement_case (status);
      rollback:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS case_app.idx_enforcement_case_status;
```

Karena `runInTransaction: false` memperbesar risiko partial state, jangan isi changeset ini dengan banyak statement.

---

## 11. Dropping Index Safely

```sql
DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS case_app.idx_enforcement_case_status;
```

Gunakan saat:

- index tidak dipakai;
- index redundant;
- write overhead terlalu tinggi;
- query plan sudah diverifikasi.

Checklist sebelum drop index:

```sql
-- cek definisi index
SELECT schemaname, tablename, indexname, indexdef
FROM pg_indexes
WHERE schemaname = 'case_app'
  AND tablename = 'enforcement_case';
```

Pastikan:

- tidak mendukung unique/primary key constraint;
- tidak dipakai critical query;
- tidak menjadi dependency query performance;
- tidak dibutuhkan reporting/batch;
- query plan sudah diuji.

---

## 12. Rename Column / Table Safety

### Jangan Langsung Rename

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
RENAME COLUMN case_number TO reference_number;
```

Masalah:

- MyBatis mapper lama rusak;
- view lama rusak;
- function/procedure bisa gagal;
- pod lama di rolling deployment masih query kolom lama;
- report/batch/Camunda worker bisa rusak.

### Safer Expand/Contract

```text
Release 1
  Add new column reference_number

Release 2
  App dual-write case_number and reference_number

Release 3
  Backfill reference_number from case_number

Release 4
  App read reference_number only

Release 5
  Drop old column case_number
```

SQL:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN reference_number TEXT;
```

Backfill:

```sql
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET reference_number = case_number
WHERE reference_number IS NULL;
```

Contract later:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
DROP COLUMN case_number;
```

---

## 13. Drop Column Safety

PostgreSQL documentation jelas: saat column di-drop, data di column tersebut hilang, dan constraints yang melibatkan column ikut di-drop; dependency eksternal seperti FK/view perlu `CASCADE` jika mau ikut dihapus. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-alter.html))

### Dangerous

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
DROP COLUMN case_number CASCADE;
```

`CASCADE` bisa menghapus dependent object seperti view secara tidak sengaja.

### Safer Checklist

Sebelum drop column:

```sql
-- cari dependency object terhadap table
SELECT
    dependent_ns.nspname AS dependent_schema,
    dependent_view.relname AS dependent_object,
    source_ns.nspname AS source_schema,
    source_table.relname AS source_table
FROM pg_depend d
JOIN pg_rewrite r
    ON r.oid = d.objid
JOIN pg_class dependent_view
    ON dependent_view.oid = r.ev_class
JOIN pg_class source_table
    ON source_table.oid = d.refobjid
JOIN pg_namespace dependent_ns
    ON dependent_ns.oid = dependent_view.relnamespace
JOIN pg_namespace source_ns
    ON source_ns.oid = source_table.relnamespace
WHERE source_ns.nspname = 'case_app'
  AND source_table.relname = 'enforcement_case';
```

Dan secara aplikasi:

```text
- grep MyBatis mapper
- grep Java repository/service
- grep view/function/procedure SQL
- grep reporting query
- check Camunda worker/process variables
- check dashboards and batch jobs
```

---

## 14. Changing Column Type Safety

### Risky

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ALTER COLUMN risk_score TYPE NUMERIC(5,2);
```

PostgreSQL `SET DATA TYPE` bisa membutuhkan conversion dan kadang perlu `USING`; statistik kolom juga dihapus, sehingga `ANALYZE` direkomendasikan setelah perubahan tipe. PostgreSQL docs juga mencatat beberapa bentuk `ALTER TABLE` yang melakukan rewrite bisa memerlukan waktu signifikan dan temporary disk space hingga sekitar dua kali ukuran table/index yang di-rewrite. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

### Safer Pattern

```text
1. Add new column
2. Backfill with conversion
3. App reads new column
4. Stop writing old column
5. Drop old column later
```

SQL:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score_decimal NUMERIC(5,2);
```

```sql
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score_decimal = risk_score::NUMERIC(5,2)
WHERE risk_score_decimal IS NULL;
```

---

## 15. Table Rewrite Awareness

Beberapa operasi bisa menyebabkan table rewrite:

| Operation | Risk |
|---|---|
| Change column type with rewrite | Large table rewrite, lock, disk usage. |
| Add volatile default column | Existing rows must be computed. |
| Add stored generated column | Table rewrite. |
| Certain storage changes | May require rewrite later. |
| Vacuum full / cluster | Full rewrite. |

PostgreSQL current documentation menyebut adding column dengan volatile default, stored generated column, identity column, atau domain type dengan constraints dapat menyebabkan seluruh table dan index di-rewrite. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 16. Constraint Validation Pattern

### General Pattern

```text
1. Add constraint as NOT VALID
2. Ensure new writes obey constraint
3. Clean old invalid rows
4. Validate constraint
```

Example:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_priority_valid
CHECK (priority IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL'))
NOT VALID;
```

Fix invalid historical data:

```sql
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET priority = 'MEDIUM'
WHERE priority IS NULL
   OR priority NOT IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL');
```

Validate:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_priority_valid;
```

`NOT VALID` mengurangi dampak saat menambahkan constraint karena tidak melakukan scan table saat add constraint; validation dilakukan terpisah, dan PostgreSQL docs menyatakan scanning table besar untuk constraint baru bisa lama dan update ke table bisa terkunci sampai `ADD CONSTRAINT` commit jika tidak memakai `NOT VALID`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 17. Backfill Safety

### Bad Backfill

```sql
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score = 0
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

Pada table besar, ini bisa:

- membuat transaksi lama;
- menghasilkan WAL besar;
- menahan lock lebih lama;
- mengganggu autovacuum;
- memicu replication lag;
- sulit di-resume jika gagal.

### Safer Chunked Backfill

```sql
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id;
```

Jalankan berulang dari job dengan:

```text
- batch size configurable
- sleep between batches
- max runtime per execution
- progress logging
- retry safe
- stop condition clear
```

---

## 18. Backfill Job Design

Untuk Java microservice, jangan selalu memasukkan backfill besar ke migration SQL.

Lebih aman:

```text
Migration:
  Add nullable column

Application / Batch Job:
  Backfill gradually

Migration:
  Add constraint / NOT NULL after completion
```

### Backfill Control Table

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_app.migration_backfill_progress (
    migration_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    last_processed_id UUID,
    processed_count BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);
```

### Why Useful?

| Benefit | Explanation |
|---|---|
| Resumable | Job bisa lanjut dari progress terakhir. |
| Observable | Bisa tahu berapa row sudah diproses. |
| Auditable | Cocok untuk regulated system. |
| Safer retry | Tidak mengulang semua dari nol. |

---

## 19. Lock Timeout and Statement Timeout

Untuk migration production, gunakan guard timeout.

```sql
SET lock_timeout = '5s';
SET statement_timeout = '15min';
```

Contoh:

```sql
SET lock_timeout = '5s';

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

Manfaat:

- migration gagal cepat jika lock tidak tersedia;
- menghindari deploy menggantung;
- lebih baik gagal sebelum merusak availability.

### Caution

Jangan sembarang set timeout terlalu pendek untuk migration yang memang perlu waktu. Bedakan:

| Timeout | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `lock_timeout` | Berapa lama menunggu lock. |
| `statement_timeout` | Berapa lama statement boleh berjalan. |
| job timeout | Berapa lama pipeline/job boleh hidup. |

---

## 20. Migration Transaction Strategy

### Transactional Migration

Cocok untuk:

```text
- add nullable column
- create table
- add simple constraint
- small schema changes
```

Benefit:

- atomic;
- failure rollback otomatis;
- schema history lebih bersih.

### Non-Transactional Migration

Diperlukan untuk:

```text
- CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY
- DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY
- beberapa operasi concurrent/maintenance tertentu
```

`CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` tidak boleh berjalan dalam transaction block. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

### Rule

```text
Default: transactional migration.
Exception: non-transactional only when PostgreSQL requires it.
```

---

## 21. Migration Tool Implication

### Flyway

```text
V001030__create_index_concurrently.sql
```

Isi:

```sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_enforcement_case_created_at
ON case_app.enforcement_case (created_at);
```

Pastikan file ini dikonfigurasi non-transactional sesuai tool/version/policy perusahaan.

### Liquibase

```yaml
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 203-create-index-concurrently
      author: case-service-team
      runInTransaction: false
      changes:
        - sql:
            sql: |
              CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_enforcement_case_created_at
              ON case_app.enforcement_case (created_at);
```

---

## 22. Migration Compatibility with Rolling Deployment

Di Kubernetes, rolling deployment berarti ada window di mana:

```text
old pod + old code
new pod + new code
same database schema
```

Jadi migration harus compatible dengan kedua versi aplikasi.

### Safe Change

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

Old app masih jalan karena tidak peduli kolom baru.

### Unsafe Change

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
DROP COLUMN status;
```

Old app rusak karena mapper masih query `status`.

### Compatibility Matrix

| DB Change | Old App Safe? | New App Safe? | Pattern |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Add nullable column | Yes | Yes | Safe expand. |
| Add table unused by old app | Yes | Yes | Safe expand. |
| Add NOT NULL column without default | No / migration fail | Maybe | Avoid. |
| Rename column | No | Yes only after app update | Expand/contract. |
| Drop column | No | Yes only after cleanup | Contract later. |
| Tighten constraint | Maybe | Maybe | Validate data first. |
| Change view columns | Maybe no | Maybe | Treat view as contract. |
| Change function signature | Maybe no | Maybe no | Version function or expand. |

---

## 23. MyBatis-Specific Safety

MyBatis makes schema compatibility extra important because mapper SQL is explicit.

Example mapper:

```xml
<select id="findByStatus" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
    SELECT
        id,
        case_number,
        status,
        priority,
        created_at
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE status = #{status}
</select>
```

Migration that breaks it:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
DROP COLUMN priority;
```

### Required Test

Every migration PR touching schema should run:

```text
- mapper XML parse/test
- integration test against migrated PostgreSQL
- resultMap compatibility test
- query execution test for changed tables/views
```

### Query Contract Rule

```text
If MyBatis references it, migration must treat it as a contract.
```

This includes:

- table column;
- view column;
- function parameter;
- procedure call;
- enum/reference values;
- trigger side effect used by app.

---

## 24. View / Function / Trigger Dependency Safety

DDL migration can break database objects silently if dependency is not mapped.

### Example

View:

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status
FROM case_app.enforcement_case;
```

Migration:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
DROP COLUMN status;
```

This can fail if view depends on column, or worse, someone uses `CASCADE` and drops the view.

### Rule

```text
Never use CASCADE in production migration unless dependency impact is explicitly reviewed.
```

---

## 25. Materialized View Safety

Materialized view is not just DDL; it also has data freshness concern.

Risky migration:

```sql
DROP MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics;

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics AS
SELECT ...
```

Problems:

- readers may fail during gap;
- indexes disappear;
- refresh schedule may break;
- data stale after re-create;
- `CONCURRENTLY` refresh may require unique index.

Safer thinking:

```text
- Is this API-critical?
- Can readers tolerate stale or missing data?
- Is refresh part of migration or scheduled job?
- Are indexes recreated?
- Is concurrent refresh possible?
- Is fallback query available?
```

---

## 26. Trigger Safety

Trigger changes are high-risk because they alter behavior behind the application’s back.

Example trigger function:

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at := now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

Risk:

- changes all updates;
- MyBatis update statements may behave differently;
- audit fields change silently;
- bulk backfill can fire trigger unexpectedly;
- trigger can slow writes.

Checklist:

```text
- Does trigger fire on backfill?
- Should trigger be disabled for migration?
- Does trigger mutate user-visible fields?
- Does trigger write audit logs?
- Is behavior deterministic?
- Is there an integration test?
```

---

## 27. Safe DDL Patterns

### Pattern A - Add Optional Field

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN external_reference TEXT;
```

Then app uses it optionally.

---

### Pattern B - Add Required Field

```text
1. Add nullable column
2. App writes new value
3. Backfill old rows
4. Add CHECK/NOT NULL
```

---

### Pattern C - Add FK

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_case_status
FOREIGN KEY (status)
REFERENCES case_app.case_status_reference(code)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_case_status;
```

---

### Pattern D - Add Unique Constraint

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX CONCURRENTLY uqidx_case_number
ON case_app.enforcement_case(case_number);

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT uq_case_number
UNIQUE USING INDEX uqidx_case_number;
```

---

### Pattern E - Rename Column

```text
1. Add new column
2. Dual-write
3. Backfill
4. Switch read
5. Drop old column later
```

---

### Pattern F - Change Column Type

```text
1. Add new typed column
2. Backfill converted values
3. Dual-write / switch read
4. Drop old column later
```

---

## 28. Unsafe DDL Patterns

| Unsafe Pattern | Why Dangerous |
|---|---|
| `DROP COLUMN ... CASCADE` | Can remove dependent views/functions unexpectedly. |
| Large `UPDATE` in one transaction | Lock/WAL/replication lag risk. |
| `CREATE INDEX` normal on huge live table | Can block writes. |
| `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` inside transaction | PostgreSQL rejects it. |
| Rename column used by MyBatis | Old pod breaks. |
| Add FK without prechecking data | Migration may fail or block long. |
| Add `NOT NULL` before backfill | Fails or scans large table. |
| Change function signature directly | Callers break. |
| Trigger change without tests | Silent behavior change. |
| Recreate materialized view without reader strategy | Reader failures / stale data. |

---

## 29. Production DDL Review Checklist

Use this before approving a migration PR.

| Question | Required Answer |
|---|---|
| Is table large? | Row count and size estimate. |
| Is DDL transactional? | Yes/no and why. |
| Does it require table rewrite? | Yes/no/unknown. |
| What lock can it take? | Known or tested. |
| Does it affect old app version? | Must be backward-compatible. |
| Does it affect MyBatis mapper? | Tests required. |
| Does it affect Jersey API response? | Contract review required. |
| Does it affect Camunda running process? | Process compatibility review. |
| Is there data backfill? | Batch/resume strategy. |
| Is there constraint validation? | `NOT VALID`/validate plan. |
| Is there index build? | Concurrent or maintenance window. |
| Is there rollback/roll-forward plan? | Explicit. |
| Is there monitoring? | Migration logs and DB metrics. |

---

## 30. CI/CD Safety Gates

```text
Pull Request
  ├─ SQL lint
  ├─ Migration naming check
  ├─ Detect dangerous DDL
  ├─ Run migration on fresh PostgreSQL
  ├─ Run MyBatis tests
  ├─ Run Jersey integration tests
  ├─ Generate migration report
  └─ Require approval for high-risk DDL

Staging
  ├─ Run on realistic data volume if possible
  ├─ Capture duration
  ├─ Capture lock wait
  ├─ Capture failed statement
  └─ Verify app compatibility

Production
  ├─ Backup / restore point
  ├─ Set lock timeout
  ├─ Run migration once
  ├─ Deploy app
  ├─ Monitor locks/errors/latency
  └─ Record audit evidence
```

---

## 31. Dangerous DDL Detector

Minimal grep-based gate:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

changed_files="${1:-src/main/resources/db}"

if grep -RniE \
  "DROP TABLE|DROP COLUMN|TRUNCATE|CASCADE|ALTER COLUMN .* TYPE|CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY|ALTER TABLE .* SET NOT NULL" \
  "$changed_files"; then
  echo "High-risk DDL detected. Require DB migration review."
  exit 1
fi
```

Ini bukan pengganti review manusia, tetapi cukup untuk mencegah perubahan berisiko lewat tanpa perhatian.

---

## 32. Observability During Migration

Pantau query dan lock:

```sql
SELECT
    pid,
    usename,
    state,
    wait_event_type,
    wait_event,
    query_start,
    now() - query_start AS duration,
    query
FROM pg_stat_activity
WHERE state <> 'idle'
ORDER BY query_start;
```

Pantau blocking:

```sql
SELECT
    blocked.pid AS blocked_pid,
    blocked.query AS blocked_query,
    blocking.pid AS blocking_pid,
    blocking.query AS blocking_query
FROM pg_stat_activity blocked
JOIN pg_locks blocked_locks
    ON blocked_locks.pid = blocked.pid
JOIN pg_locks blocking_locks
    ON blocking_locks.locktype = blocked_locks.locktype
   AND blocking_locks.database IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.database
   AND blocking_locks.relation IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.relation
   AND blocking_locks.page IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.page
   AND blocking_locks.tuple IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.tuple
   AND blocking_locks.virtualxid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.virtualxid
   AND blocking_locks.transactionid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.transactionid
   AND blocking_locks.classid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.classid
   AND blocking_locks.objid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objid
   AND blocking_locks.objsubid IS NOT DISTINCT FROM blocked_locks.objsubid
   AND blocking_locks.pid <> blocked_locks.pid
JOIN pg_stat_activity blocking
    ON blocking.pid = blocking_locks.pid
WHERE NOT blocked_locks.granted
  AND blocking_locks.granted;
```

---

## 33. Practical Example: Add `risk_score` Safely

### Goal

Tambahkan `risk_score` required dengan range 0-100.

### Migration 1 - Expand

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

### App Release

App mulai menulis `risk_score` untuk case baru.

### Backfill

```sql
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id;
```

Run until:

```sql
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

### Migration 2 - Add Constraint Not Valid

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_enforcement_case_risk_score_range
CHECK (risk_score >= 0 AND risk_score <= 100)
NOT VALID;
```

### Migration 3 - Validate

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_enforcement_case_risk_score_range;
```

### Migration 4 - Enforce Not Null

Untuk PostgreSQL yang mendukung staged not-null validation sesuai versi target:

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ALTER COLUMN risk_score SET NOT NULL;
```

Jika belum tersedia/masih perlu scan, gunakan valid `CHECK (risk_score IS NOT NULL)` sebagai bukti sebelum `SET NOT NULL`, sesuai versi dan testing di environment target.

---

## 34. Practical Example: Add FK Safely

### Existing Table

```sql
case_app.enforcement_case(status)
case_app.case_status_reference(code)
```

### Precheck

```sql
SELECT c.status, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
LEFT JOIN case_app.case_status_reference r
    ON r.code = c.status
WHERE r.code IS NULL
GROUP BY c.status;
```

### Add FK

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_enforcement_case_status
FOREIGN KEY (status)
REFERENCES case_app.case_status_reference(code)
NOT VALID;
```

### Validate

```sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_enforcement_case_status;
```

---

## 35. Practical Example: Add Index Safely

### Query Problem

```sql
SELECT *
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE status = 'OPEN'
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

### Candidate Index

```sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_case_status_created_at
ON case_app.enforcement_case (status, created_at DESC);
```

### Verify

```sql
EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)
SELECT *
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE status = 'OPEN'
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

### Cleanup If Failed

If invalid index remains:

```sql
DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS case_app.idx_case_status_created_at;
```

Then retry after root cause is resolved.

---

## 36. Ringkasan Seri 6

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| DDL safety | Valid SQL belum tentu aman untuk production. |
| Add column | Add nullable first is safest. |
| Default value | Constant default is optimized in modern PostgreSQL; volatile default can rewrite/update rows. |
| NOT NULL | Backfill first; validate before enforcing. |
| CHECK/FK | Use `NOT VALID` then `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`. |
| Unique constraint | Consider concurrent unique index then attach as constraint. |
| Index | Use `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` for large live tables. |
| Transaction | Concurrent index cannot run inside transaction block. |
| Rename/drop | Use expand/contract, not direct change. |
| Type change | Prefer new column + backfill for large/critical tables. |
| Backfill | Use chunked/resumable jobs. |
| MyBatis | Mapper SQL is a schema contract. |
| Triggers | Treat trigger changes as behavior changes, not minor DDL. |
| Materialized views | Manage definition, index, refresh, and reader fallback separately. |
| CI/CD | Detect dangerous DDL and require migration review. |

---

# Status Seri

**Seri 6 dari 18 selesai.**

## Berikutnya

Rekomendasi materi selanjutnya:

**Seri 7 - Zero-Downtime Schema Evolution**

Fokusnya: expand/contract migration, backward-compatible schema change, dual-read/dual-write, rolling deployment compatibility, feature flag, safe removal, dan strategi untuk menghindari MyBatis/Jersey/Camunda break saat deploy bertahap.

---

**Sources:**

- [PostgreSQL: Documentation: 18: CREATE INDEX](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html)

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:11:11 PM

Kita lanjut ke **Seri 7 dari 18**.

# Seri 7 - Zero-Downtime Schema Evolution

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas cara mengubah schema PostgreSQL **tanpa memutus service** saat aplikasi di-deploy secara bertahap, terutama pada stack:

```text id="t0r3a9"
Jersey / JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Camunda
Docker / Kubernetes
Flyway / Liquibase
```

Zero-downtime schema evolution bukan berarti “tidak ada risiko sama sekali”. Artinya: perubahan schema dirancang agar **versi lama aplikasi dan versi baru aplikasi bisa berjalan bersamaan** selama window deployment.

Ini penting karena Kubernetes Deployment dengan strategi rolling update akan mengganti Pod secara bertahap: old ReplicaSet diturunkan dan new ReplicaSet dinaikkan secara gradual, dengan kontrol seperti `maxUnavailable` dan `maxSurge`. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/deployment/))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="4bsujc"
Zero-Downtime Schema Evolution
────────────────────────────────────────────

During rolling deployment:

       old app pod ─────┐
                        ├── same database schema
       new app pod ─────┘

Therefore:

       schema must be compatible with both app versions
```

Kalau database migration hanya kompatibel dengan versi baru aplikasi, maka old pod bisa gagal selama rolling update.

Kalau aplikasi baru butuh schema yang belum ada, maka new pod bisa gagal saat naik.

Maka aturan dasarnya:

```text id="twhpo8"
Database schema must support:
  - old application version
  - new application version
  - transitional data state
```

---

## 3. Core Pattern: Expand / Migrate / Contract

```text id="wb22gl"
Expand
  └─ Tambahkan struktur baru tanpa merusak struktur lama

Migrate
  └─ Isi / sinkronkan / pindahkan data secara aman

Switch
  └─ Aplikasi mulai memakai struktur baru

Contract
  └─ Hapus struktur lama setelah tidak ada consumer lama
```

Dalam praktik, sering menjadi:

```text id="hhqtgu"
Release 1: Expand schema
Release 2: App dual-read / dual-write
Release 3: Backfill data
Release 4: App read new only
Release 5: Contract / remove old schema
```

Contract tidak boleh dilakukan sebelum yakin semua consumer lama sudah berhenti.

---

## 4. Compatibility Matrix

| Change Type | Old App Safe? | New App Safe? | Zero-Downtime Strategy |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Add nullable column | Ya | Ya | Expand only. |
| Add table unused by old app | Ya | Ya | Expand only. |
| Add index | Ya | Ya | Use concurrent index for large live table. |
| Add reference data | Biasanya ya | Ya | Idempotent seed. |
| Add optional API field | Ya | Ya | Backward-compatible DTO. |
| Add NOT NULL column directly | Tidak / migration fail | Mungkin | Add nullable → backfill → enforce. |
| Rename column directly | Tidak | Ya | Add new column → dual-write → switch → drop old. |
| Drop column | Tidak | Ya setelah cleanup | Contract phase only. |
| Change column type directly | Mungkin tidak | Mungkin | Add new typed column → backfill → switch. |
| Change enum/status semantic | Berisiko | Berisiko | Dual value support / mapping layer. |
| Change view output columns | Berisiko | Berisiko | Versioned view or additive change first. |
| Change function signature | Tidak | Mungkin | Create new function version. |
| Change trigger behavior | Berisiko | Berisiko | Feature flag / staged trigger logic. |

---

## 5. Why MyBatis Makes This More Important

Dengan MyBatis, SQL biasanya eksplisit:

```xml id="9t2irs"
<select id="findCaseById" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
    SELECT
        id,
        case_number,
        status,
        priority
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

Kalau migration langsung menghapus `priority`:

```sql id="dzpcjt"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
DROP COLUMN priority;
```

Aplikasi lama akan gagal saat query dijalankan.

Karena itu, untuk stack MyBatis:

```text id="z0cnhe"
Every column, view output, function signature, and reference value
used by mapper is an internal contract.
```

---

## 6. Pattern 1 - Add Optional Column

### Scenario

Tambahkan `external_reference` ke `enforcement_case`.

### Migration

```sql id="h28c5a"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN external_reference TEXT;
```

### App Change

Mapper baru boleh membaca field ini:

```xml id="8mnybc"
<result property="externalReference" column="external_reference"/>
```

### Why Safe

| Consumer | Status |
|---|---|
| Old app | Tidak peduli kolom baru. |
| New app | Bisa membaca/menulis kolom baru. |
| Existing data | `NULL` valid. |
| Migration rollback need | Rendah. |

Ini adalah contoh expand-only migration.

---

## 7. Pattern 2 - Add Required Column

### Bad Pattern

```sql id="r35gwc"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER NOT NULL;
```

Problem:

- existing row tidak punya value;
- migration bisa gagal;
- old app tidak menulis field ini;
- rolling deployment bisa menghasilkan row baru tanpa `risk_score`.

### Safe Pattern

```text id="kpxg7p"
Release 1:
  Add nullable column

Release 2:
  App writes column for new rows

Release 3:
  Backfill old rows

Release 4:
  Enforce CHECK / NOT NULL

Release 5:
  Remove fallback logic
```

### Migration 1

```sql id="4o0962"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

### App Write

```java id="i2w009"
public void createCase(CreateCaseRequest request) {
    CaseEntity entity = new CaseEntity();
    entity.setCaseNumber(request.caseNumber());
    entity.setStatus("OPEN");
    entity.setRiskScore(calculateInitialRiskScore(request));
    caseMapper.insert(entity);
}
```

### Backfill

```sql id="zm1o3x"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id;
```

### Constraint

```sql id="c10o5e"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_enforcement_case_risk_score_range
CHECK (risk_score >= 0 AND risk_score <= 100)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_enforcement_case_risk_score_range;
```

PostgreSQL `NOT VALID` memungkinkan constraint ditambahkan tanpa langsung memvalidasi seluruh row existing; constraint tetap berlaku untuk insert/update baru, lalu validasi existing rows dilakukan terpisah dengan `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT`. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html))

---

## 8. Pattern 3 - Rename Column Without Downtime

### Requirement

Rename:

```text id="df4aau"
case_number → reference_number
```

### Jangan Langsung

```sql id="m0knaw"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
RENAME COLUMN case_number TO reference_number;
```

Ini breaking untuk MyBatis mapper lama.

### Safe Multi-Release Plan

```text id="glqmgv"
Release 1 - Expand
  Add reference_number nullable

Release 2 - Dual-write
  App writes both case_number and reference_number

Release 3 - Backfill
  Fill reference_number for old rows

Release 4 - Switch read
  App reads reference_number as primary field

Release 5 - Contract
  Drop case_number after all consumers migrated
```

### Release 1 Migration

```sql id="wimrmu"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN reference_number TEXT;
```

### Release 2 Mapper Insert

```xml id="bxkhik"
<insert id="insertCase">
```
