```xml id="bxkhik"
    INSERT INTO case_app.enforcement_case (
        id,
        case_number,
        reference_number,
        status,
        created_at
    )
    VALUES (
        #{id},
        #{referenceNumber},
        #{referenceNumber},
        #{status},
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

### Release 3 Backfill

```sql id="06gkms"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET reference_number = case_number
WHERE reference_number IS NULL;
```

Untuk table besar, gunakan chunked backfill, bukan satu `UPDATE` besar.

### Release 4 Read New Column

```xml id="z0d8ur"
<select id="findCaseById" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
    SELECT
        id,
        reference_number,
        status,
        created_at
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE id = #{id}
</select>
```

### Release 5 Contract

```sql id="cibffb"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
DROP COLUMN case_number;
```

Contract hanya aman setelah:

- old app version sudah tidak berjalan;
- mapper lama sudah tidak ada;
- view/function/procedure/report tidak pakai kolom lama;
- Camunda worker/process lama tidak bergantung pada field lama;
- downstream consumers sudah dimigrasikan.

---

## 9. Pattern 4 - Change Column Type Safely

### Requirement

Change:

```text id="azqqnx"
risk_score INTEGER → NUMERIC(5,2)
```

### Risky Direct Change

```sql id="opdptj"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ALTER COLUMN risk_score TYPE NUMERIC(5,2);
```

Ini bisa menyebabkan table rewrite, conversion issue, lock, dan mapper type mismatch.

### Safe Pattern

```text id="ixpdz4"
1. Add risk_score_decimal
2. App dual-write integer + decimal
3. Backfill decimal from integer
4. Switch read to decimal
5. Drop old integer column later
```

### Migration

```sql id="lqlm1m"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score_decimal NUMERIC(5,2);
```

### Backfill

```sql id="qspv5c"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score_decimal = risk_score::NUMERIC(5,2)
WHERE risk_score_decimal IS NULL;
```

### Mapper Transition

During transition, support both:

```xml id="llxv76"
SELECT
    id,
    COALESCE(risk_score_decimal, risk_score::NUMERIC(5,2)) AS risk_score_decimal
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE id = #{id}
```

Ini cocok sebagai transitional query, bukan final state.

---

## 10. Pattern 5 - Split One Column into Two

### Requirement

```text id="44mak8"
customer_name → first_name + last_name
```

### Expand

```sql id="rm191w"
ALTER TABLE case_app.customer
ADD COLUMN first_name TEXT,
ADD COLUMN last_name TEXT;
```

### Dual-Write

Aplikasi baru menulis:

```text id="6xzo5i"
customer_name = original input / display name
first_name    = parsed first name
last_name     = parsed last name
```

### Backfill

```sql id="ykojf1"
UPDATE case_app.customer
SET
    first_name = split_part(customer_name, ' ', 1),
    last_name = nullif(
        substr(customer_name, length(split_part(customer_name, ' ', 1)) + 2),
        ''
    )
WHERE first_name IS NULL;
```

### Switch

API baru bisa expose:

```json id="55zntf"
{
  "firstName": "Ana",
  "lastName": "Wijaya"
}
```

Tetapi pertahankan field lama jika client lama masih butuh:

```json id="suw4ez"
{
  "customerName": "Ana Wijaya",
  "firstName": "Ana",
  "lastName": "Wijaya"
}
```

### Contract

Drop `customer_name` hanya setelah client lama aman.

---

## 11. Pattern 6 - Merge Columns

### Requirement

```text id="obubka"
first_name + last_name → display_name
```

### Expand

```sql id="qm1r6z"
ALTER TABLE case_app.customer
ADD COLUMN display_name TEXT;
```

### Backfill

```sql id="syx741"
UPDATE case_app.customer
SET display_name = trim(concat_ws(' ', first_name, last_name))
WHERE display_name IS NULL;
```

### Dual-Write

App menulis semua field selama transition.

### Switch

App membaca `display_name`.

### Contract

Drop `first_name`, `last_name` setelah semua consumer tidak memakainya.

---

## 12. Pattern 7 - Change Status / Enum Semantics

PostgreSQL sering memakai reference table, enum-like text, atau actual enum.

Contoh status lama:

```text id="h0ohud"
OPEN
IN_PROGRESS
CLOSED
```

Status baru:

```text id="lnu5n1"
DRAFT
OPEN
UNDER_REVIEW
RESOLVED
CLOSED
```

### Risk

Status value sering dipakai oleh:

- MyBatis queries;
- business rules;
- Camunda gateway conditions;
- reporting;
- permission checks;
- dashboard filters;
- external API clients.

### Safe Strategy

```text id="koxeq4"
1. Add new status values to reference table
2. App supports old and new statuses
3. Introduce mapping layer
4. Migrate existing rows gradually
5. Update Camunda conditions / workers safely
6. Remove old statuses only after no active usage
```

### Seed

```sql id="80l9iu"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
VALUES
    ('UNDER_REVIEW', 'Under Review', 30, true),
    ('RESOLVED', 'Resolved', 40, true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

### Transitional Query

```sql id="e82u2z"
SELECT *
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE status IN ('IN_PROGRESS', 'UNDER_REVIEW');
```

### Contract

Only deactivate old value after:

```sql id="dbgy76"
SELECT status, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
GROUP BY status;
```

shows no old active usage, and process/reporting checks are clean.

---

## 13. Pattern 8 - Change View Contract

View is not “just internal SQL” if MyBatis reads from it.

### Existing View

```sql id="w4nxtf"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    status
FROM case_app.enforcement_case;
```

### Breaking Change

```sql id="ncttzf"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    id,
    reference_number,
    status
FROM case_app.enforcement_case;
```

Old mapper expecting `case_number` breaks.

### Safe Additive View Change

```sql id="tizifq"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    id,
    case_number,
    reference_number,
    status
FROM case_app.enforcement_case;
```

Then app migrates to `reference_number`.

### Better for Major Changes: Versioned View

```sql id="fvkf4i"
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary_v2 AS
SELECT
    id,
    reference_number,
    status
FROM case_app.enforcement_case;
```

Then:

```text id="dj972w"
old mapper → v_case_summary
new mapper → v_case_summary_v2
```

Drop old view later.

---

## 14. Pattern 9 - Change Function Signature

### Existing Function

```sql id="egupaj"
case_app.fn_calculate_case_sla(case_id UUID)
```

### Bad Change

```sql id="27tqdh"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.fn_calculate_case_sla(
    case_id UUID,
    timezone TEXT
)
RETURNS INTEGER
...
```

If callers still use one argument, they may fail depending on old function existence and overload behavior.

### Safe Pattern: Add New Function

```sql id="1c0f9j"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.fn_calculate_case_sla_v2(
    p_case_id UUID,
    p_timezone TEXT
)
RETURNS INTEGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
STABLE
AS $$
BEGIN
    -- new logic
    RETURN 0;
END;
$$;
```

Migration flow:

```text id="0c6kj7"
1. Add fn_calculate_case_sla_v2
2. Update view/mapper/procedure to call v2
3. Verify no callers use v1
4. Drop v1 later
```

---

## 15. Pattern 10 - Trigger Evolution

Trigger changes can silently change write behavior.

### Existing Trigger

```sql id="hmbt5x"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_case_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON case_app.enforcement_case
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at();
```

### Risky Change

Changing trigger function to also mutate `status`:

```sql id="to2sil"
NEW.status := 'UNDER_REVIEW';
```

This is not a minor DB change. It changes business behavior.

### Safe Strategy

```text id="hviqoq"
1. Add explicit app-level behavior first if possible
2. If trigger is required, make trigger logic additive and observable
3. Guard with conditions
4. Add audit/log table if side effect matters
5. Test inserts/updates/backfills
6. Avoid firing trigger during backfill if not intended
```

### Guarded Trigger Function

```sql id="ksxzvi"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW IS DISTINCT FROM OLD THEN
        NEW.updated_at := now();
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

---

## 16. Pattern 11 - Materialized View Evolution

Materialized view has two contracts:

```text id="ldiu7g"
1. Definition contract
2. Freshness contract
```

### Additive Change

```sql id="jw7rvq"
DROP MATERIALIZED VIEW IF EXISTS case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics;

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics AS
SELECT
    status,
    COUNT(*) AS case_count,
    max(updated_at) AS latest_update_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
GROUP BY status
WITH NO DATA;
```

Risk:

- drop/recreate creates reader gap;
- index lost;
- data unavailable until refresh;
- API/report could fail.

### Safer Strategy

Create new materialized view:

```sql id="1s65vo"
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics_v2 AS
SELECT
    status,
    priority,
    COUNT(*) AS case_count,
    max(updated_at) AS latest_update_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
GROUP BY status, priority
WITH NO DATA;
```

Index:

```sql id="zhux1g"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_mv_case_daily_metrics_v2
ON case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics_v2 (status, priority);
```

Refresh:

```sql id="2qnqkb"
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW case_app.mv_case_daily_metrics_v2;
```

Switch consumer to v2, then drop v1 later.

PostgreSQL materialized views store query results physically and are refreshed explicitly; this makes freshness and refresh strategy part of the migration design, not an implementation detail. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/rules-materializedviews.html))

---

## 17. Zero-Downtime with Index Changes

Adding an index usually does not require application compatibility changes, but can affect availability if done wrong.

For large live tables:

```sql id="dy6kzh"
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_case_status_created_at
ON case_app.enforcement_case (status, created_at DESC);
```

PostgreSQL allows ordinary `CREATE INDEX` inside a transaction block, but `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` cannot run inside one. ([postgresql.org](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createindex.html))

### Tool Implication

| Tool | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Flyway | Non-transactional migration file/config for concurrent index. |
| Liquibase | `runInTransaction: false` changeset. |
| CI/CD | Detect `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` and verify config. |

---

## 18. Zero-Downtime with Constraint Tightening

Constraint tightening should usually be staged.

### Requirement

Only allow valid priority:

```text id="qhz3i8"
LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, CRITICAL
```

### Stage 1 - Add Constraint Not Valid

```sql id="um8qc6"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_case_priority_valid
CHECK (priority IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL'))
NOT VALID;
```

### Stage 2 - Clean Existing Data

```sql id="rjzyi6"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET priority = 'MEDIUM'
WHERE priority IS NULL
   OR priority NOT IN ('LOW', 'MEDIUM', 'HIGH', 'CRITICAL');
```

### Stage 3 - Validate

```sql id="z1aj5w"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_case_priority_valid;
```

This separates enforcement for new writes from validation of historical data.

---

## 19. Zero-Downtime with Permission / ACL Seed

Permission seed changes can be breaking even if schema does not change.

### Example

```sql id="63j966"
INSERT INTO case_app.permission_reference (code, label)
VALUES
    ('CASE_REASSIGN', 'Reassign case')
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET label = EXCLUDED.label;
```

### Risk

If new app checks `CASE_REASSIGN` but seed did not run first, users may get denied incorrectly.

### Safe Ordering

```text id="ycb4e1"
1. Seed new permission
2. Deploy app that can check/use permission
3. Assign permission to roles
4. Enable feature
```

### Feature Flag Pattern

```text id="73bdth"
permission exists
  + role assignment exists
  + feature flag enabled
  = feature active
```

---

## 20. Camunda-Specific Compatibility

Camunda process instances can outlive application releases. A running process instance may still rely on:

- old status values;
- old business key format;
- old domain fields;
- old worker behavior;
- old form data;
- old correlation keys.

Camunda 7 has a process instance migration capability where a migration plan is generated and validated before execution; this exists because changing process definitions while instances are running has explicit compatibility concerns. ([docs.camunda.org](https://docs.camunda.org/manual/7.5/user-guide/process-engine/process-instance-migration/))

### Rule

```text id="3thrzp"
Do not assume app release completion means process compatibility is complete.
```

### Migration Review Questions

| Question | Why |
|---|---|
| Do existing process instances reference old status values? | Gateway conditions may break. |
| Do workers query renamed columns? | Job execution may fail. |
| Are business keys changing? | Message correlation may fail. |
| Are task forms relying on old reference data? | User task rendering may break. |
| Are old BPMN versions still active? | New DB schema must support old workflow path. |

---

## 21. Deployment Ordering Patterns

## Pattern A - Migration Before App

Use when migration is backward-compatible.

```text id="fdyj5u"
1. Run DB migration
2. Deploy app
3. Verify
```

Examples:

- add nullable column;
- add table;
- add index;
- add seed data;
- add optional view column.

---

## Pattern B - App Before Migration

Rare. Use when app is backward-compatible with absence of new schema and migration depends on app behavior.

```text id="e7jhx5"
1. Deploy app that tolerates old/new schema
2. Run migration
3. Enable feature
```

This is harder with MyBatis because SQL referencing missing column fails. Avoid unless app uses explicit compatibility probing or feature flags.

---

## Pattern C - Multi-Release Expand/Contract

Use for breaking changes.

```text id="trmj6b"
Release 1: Expand DB
Release 2: Deploy dual-compatible app
Release 3: Backfill / migrate data
Release 4: Switch consumers
Release 5: Contract DB
```

Examples:

- rename column;
- split column;
- merge columns;
- change type;
- change function signature;
- replace materialized view;
- remove reference values;
- drop table/column.

---

## 22. Feature Flag Integration

Feature flags help separate deployment from activation.

```text id="r6m05r"
Code deployed ≠ feature enabled
Schema available ≠ behavior active
```

### Example

```text id="83in2h"
Migration:
  Add risk_score column

App:
  Writes risk_score only if feature flag enabled

Backfill:
  Populate historical risk_score

Feature:
  Enable risk_score display after data is complete
```

### Caution

Feature flags do not solve all schema problems.

If mapper SQL references a column that does not exist, feature flag in Java code may not help if the query is still executed during startup/test/path.

---

## 23. Dual-Read and Dual-Write

### Dual-Write

During transition, write both old and new fields:

```text id="snart8"
case_number      = REF-001
reference_number = REF-001
```

### Dual-Read

Read new field if available, fallback to old:

```sql id="puybd5"
SELECT
    id,
    COALESCE(reference_number, case_number) AS reference_number
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE id = #{id};
```

### When Useful

| Pattern | Useful For |
|---|---|
| Dual-write | Rename/split/merge fields. |
| Dual-read | Backfill not complete yet. |
| Fallback read | Existing historical records. |
| Data consistency check | Compare old/new values before contract. |

### Risk

Dual-write can drift if one write succeeds and another fails. Keep writes in same transaction when possible.

---

## 24. Data Consistency Verification

Before contract phase, prove old and new data match.

### Rename Column Check

```sql id="xmezrb"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE reference_number IS DISTINCT FROM case_number;
```

Expected:

```text id="e738z8"
0
```

### Split Column Check

```sql id="1dq91h"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.customer
WHERE trim(concat_ws(' ', first_name, last_name)) IS DISTINCT FROM customer_name;
```

May not be exactly zero if parsing rules are lossy. If so, document exceptions.

### Status Mapping Check

```sql id="6az04m"
SELECT old_status, new_status, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.case_status_migration_audit
GROUP BY old_status, new_status;
```

---

## 25. Contract Phase Checklist

Do not contract until all are true.

```text id="y1jfs4"
Application:
  - no old pods running
  - no mapper query references old field
  - no API clients require old contract
  - no batch/reporting job references old field

Database:
  - no view/function/procedure depends on old field
  - no trigger depends on old field
  - data consistency verified
  - backup/restore point available

Workflow:
  - no active Camunda process needs old value
  - old BPMN version path is handled
  - correlation keys/status values are compatible

Operations:
  - rollback/roll-forward plan ready
  - monitoring ready
  - contract migration approved
```

---

## 26. Detecting Database Dependencies

Find views depending on a table:

```sql id="pp28i3"
SELECT
    dependent_ns.nspname AS dependent_schema,
    dependent_view.relname AS dependent_object,
    source_ns.nspname AS source_schema,
    source_table.relname AS source_table
FROM pg_depend d
JOIN pg_rewrite r
    ON r.oid = d.objid
JOIN pg_class dependent_view
    ON dependent_view.oid = r.ev_class
JOIN pg_class source_table
    ON source_table.oid = d.refobjid
JOIN pg_namespace dependent_ns
    ON dependent_ns.oid = dependent_view.relnamespace
JOIN pg_namespace source_ns
    ON source_ns.oid = source_table.relnamespace
WHERE source_ns.nspname = 'case_app'
  AND source_table.relname = 'enforcement_case';
```

Find functions by text search:

```sql id="u6bbhd"
SELECT
    n.nspname AS schema_name,
    p.proname AS function_name,
    pg_get_functiondef(p.oid) AS function_definition
FROM pg_proc p
JOIN pg_namespace n
    ON n.oid = p.pronamespace
WHERE n.nspname = 'case_app'
  AND pg_get_functiondef(p.oid) ILIKE '%case_number%';
```

This is not perfect dependency analysis, but useful for review.

---

## 27. Application Dependency Search

Before contract, search repository:

```bash id="aaq8id"
grep -R "case_number" \
  src/main/java \
  src/main/resources/mybatis \
  src/main/resources/db \
  docs \
  --line-number
```

Also search:

```text id="m3mps4"
- BPMN files
- DMN files
- form definitions
- OpenAPI specs
- reporting SQL
- dashboard configs
- data export jobs
- integration tests
```

---

## 28. Release Timeline Example

### Requirement

Rename `case_number` to `reference_number`.

```text id="3h3od7"
T0 - Current State
  DB: case_number
  App: reads/writes case_number

T1 - Expand
  DB: add reference_number nullable
  App: still reads/writes case_number

T2 - Dual-write
  DB: case_number + reference_number
  App: writes both, reads case_number

T3 - Backfill
  DB: all reference_number populated
  App: writes both, can read fallback

T4 - Switch
  DB: case_number + reference_number
  App: reads reference_number, writes both

T5 - Stop old write
  DB: case_number + reference_number
  App: reads/writes reference_number only

T6 - Contract
  DB: drop case_number
  App: no reference to case_number
```

---

## 29. Rollback vs Roll-Forward in Zero-Downtime

### During Expand

Rollback is usually easy:

```text id="til5gi"
Add nullable column → can ignore or drop later
Add index → can drop index
Add table unused → can ignore or drop later
```

### During Switch

Rollback is harder:

```text id="njhnrk"
App started writing new schema.
Data may have diverged.
```

Need fallback:

- dual-write still active;
- old field still populated;
- new and old values consistent;
- feature flag can turn off new behavior.

### During Contract

Rollback is hardest:

```text id="15p6y7"
Old schema is gone.
Old app can no longer run.
Historical data may be removed.
```

Contract should be delayed and treated as high-risk.

---

## 30. Common Zero-Downtime Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous | Better |
|---|---|---|
| Rename column directly | Old app breaks. | Add new column, dual-write, contract later. |
| Drop old column in same release as app change | Rolling old pods break. | Wait multiple releases. |
| Tighten constraint before app writes valid data | New/old writes fail. | App first, backfill, validate. |
| Change view output destructively | MyBatis mapper breaks. | Add column or create v2 view. |
| Change function signature in place | Callers fail. | Create v2 function. |
| Assume feature flag protects missing column | SQL still fails if executed. | Schema expand before code references. |
| Backfill in one giant migration | Lock/WAL/timeout risk. | Chunked/resumable job. |
| Contract without dependency search | Hidden consumer breaks. | DB + repo + workflow dependency check. |
| Use `CASCADE` for cleanup | Drops hidden dependencies. | Explicitly drop known objects. |
| Forget Camunda active instances | Running process may use old schema/status. | Process compatibility review. |

---

## 31. PR Template for Zero-Downtime Migration

```md id="6j0u8j"
## Zero-Downtime DB Change

### Change Category
- [ ] Additive schema
- [ ] Rename
- [ ] Type change
- [ ] Split / merge field
- [ ] Constraint tightening
- [ ] View/function contract change
- [ ] Trigger behavior change
- [ ] Materialized view replacement
- [ ] Contract / cleanup

### Rollout Phase
- [ ] Expand
- [ ] Dual-write / dual-read
- [ ] Backfill
- [ ] Switch
- [ ] Contract

### Compatibility
- [ ] Old app can run after this migration
- [ ] New app can run before/after this migration
- [ ] MyBatis mapper is compatible
- [ ] API contract is compatible
- [ ] Camunda active instances are compatible

### Data Safety
- [ ] Backfill not needed
- [ ] Backfill is chunked/resumable
- [ ] Consistency query included
- [ ] Constraint validation staged
- [ ] No destructive DDL

### Contract Safety
- [ ] Repository search completed
- [ ] DB dependency search completed
- [ ] Reporting/dashboard consumers checked
- [ ] BPMN/DMN/form consumers checked
- [ ] Contract delayed until old usage is zero
```

---

## 32. Zero-Downtime Decision Tree

```text id="hchhyr"
Is the change additive?
  ├─ Yes
  │   ├─ Does old app ignore it?
  │   │   ├─ Yes → migration before app is usually safe
  │   │   └─ No → review compatibility
  │   └─ Does new app require it?
  │       ├─ Yes → migrate before app
  │       └─ No → either order may work
  │
  └─ No
      ├─ Is it rename/type/split/merge/drop?
      │   └─ Use expand/contract
      │
      ├─ Is it constraint tightening?
      │   └─ Add as NOT VALID → clean data → validate
      │
      ├─ Is it view/function contract change?
      │   └─ Add v2 or additive change first
      │
      └─ Is it trigger behavior change?
          └─ Treat as business behavior release
```

---

## 33. Minimal Checklist for Production

Before production zero-downtime DB change:

| Area | Required |
|---|---|
| Rollout phase | Clearly labeled: expand/migrate/switch/contract. |
| Old/new app compatibility | Proved by integration test. |
| MyBatis mapper | Tested against migrated DB. |
| Jersey API | Contract compatibility reviewed. |
| Camunda | Active process compatibility reviewed. |
| Backfill | Chunked or explicitly safe. |
| Constraints | Staged with `NOT VALID` where appropriate. |
| Index | Concurrent for large live table. |
| Feature flag | Used for activation when relevant. |
| Contract | Delayed until old usage is zero. |
| Dependency search | DB + repository + workflow search. |
| Recovery | Roll-forward/rollback plan documented. |

---

## 34. Ringkasan Seri 7

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| Zero-downtime migration | Harus kompatibel dengan old app dan new app selama rolling deployment. |
| Core pattern | Expand → migrate → switch → contract. |
| Add nullable column | Biasanya aman sebagai expand migration. |
| Required column | Add nullable → app writes → backfill → constraint. |
| Rename column | Jangan rename langsung; gunakan dual-write/switch/contract. |
| Type change | Tambah kolom baru, backfill, switch, drop lama. |
| Status change | Treat as workflow/API/business contract. |
| View change | Additive first or create v2 view. |
| Function change | Create v2 function if signature/semantic changes. |
| Trigger change | Treat as business behavior change. |
| Materialized view | Manage definition, freshness, index, refresh, and consumer switch. |
| Constraint tightening | Use `NOT VALID` then `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` when suitable. |
| MyBatis | Mapper SQL is a hard contract. |
| Camunda | Running process instances may outlive release windows. |
| Contract phase | Highest risk; delay until dependency usage is zero. |

---

# Status Seri

**Seri 7 dari 18 selesai.**

## Berikutnya

Rekomendasi materi selanjutnya:

**Seri 8 - Data Seeding & Reference Data**

Fokusnya: seed master/reference data, permission/role seed, config seed, environment-specific seed, idempotency, `ON CONFLICT`, seed ownership, auditability, dan cara mencegah test/dummy data bocor ke staging atau production.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 7:13:40 PM

Kita lanjut ke **Seri 8 dari 18**.

# Seri 8 - Data Seeding & Reference Data

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana mengelola **seed data** dan **reference data** secara aman dalam database migration untuk microservice berbasis:

```text id="65v98u"
Jersey / JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Flyway / Liquibase
Camunda
Docker / Kubernetes
CI/CD
```

Seed data sering terlihat sederhana:

```sql id="190cm4"
INSERT INTO case_status_reference ...
```

Tetapi di sistem production, seed data bisa menentukan:

- status lifecycle case;
- role dan permission;
- default configuration;
- SLA rule;
- routing rule;
- lookup value untuk API;
- value yang dipakai Camunda gateway;
- authorization policy;
- MyBatis query behavior.

Jadi seed data bukan “data dummy”. Seed data adalah bagian dari **application contract**.

PostgreSQL menyediakan `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT` untuk menentukan aksi alternatif saat terjadi unique/exclusion constraint violation, misalnya `DO NOTHING` atau `DO UPDATE`. Ini adalah fondasi penting untuk membuat seed idempotent. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="b5she4"
Data Seeding Mental Model
────────────────────────────────────────────

Seed Data
  │
  ├─ Reference Data
  │    ├─ status
  │    ├─ priority
  │    ├─ type
  │    └─ category
  │
  ├─ Security Data
  │    ├─ roles
  │    ├─ permissions
  │    ├─ ACL templates
  │    └─ policy mapping
  │
  ├─ Configuration Data
  │    ├─ SLA thresholds
  │    ├─ routing rules
  │    ├─ feature config
  │    └─ notification templates
  │
  ├─ Workflow Data
  │    ├─ Camunda process config
  │    ├─ task category
  │    ├─ escalation rule
  │    └─ status transition metadata
  │
  ├─ Local/Test/Demo Data
  │    ├─ test fixtures
  │    ├─ dummy users
  │    └─ demo cases
  │
  └─ Migration Support Data
       ├─ mapping table
       ├─ backfill staging table
       └─ one-time repair metadata
```

Core rule:

```text id="4yk7pd"
Production seed data must be intentional, owned, idempotent, reviewable, and auditable.
```

---

## 3. Seed Data vs Reference Data vs Fixture

| Type | Meaning | Production? | Example | Migration Strategy |
|---|---|---:|---|---|
| **Reference data** | Data stabil yang menjadi bagian domain model. | Ya | `OPEN`, `CLOSED`, `HIGH`, `LOW` | Versioned migration atau controlled repeatable. |
| **Security seed** | Role, permission, ACL default. | Ya | `CASE_READ`, `CASE_REASSIGN` | Versioned, strict approval. |
| **Config seed** | Runtime config yang punya default. | Ya, hati-hati | SLA threshold, escalation day count | Versioned atau config-management path. |
| **Workflow seed** | Data yang dipakai proses bisnis/workflow. | Ya | task type, escalation code | Versioned, Camunda-aware. |
| **Test fixture** | Data untuk automated test. | Tidak | dummy case/user | Test-only path. |
| **Demo data** | Data untuk demo/training. | Tidak untuk prod | sample case | Demo-only context/path. |
| **Migration support data** | Data sementara untuk backfill/repair. | Kadang | old-to-new status mapping | Versioned, temporary, documented. |

---

## 4. Why Seed Data Is Dangerous

Seed data bisa menyebabkan incident meski tidak mengubah schema.

Contoh:

```sql id="5zmpw1"
INSERT INTO case_app.permission_reference (code, label)
VALUES ('CASE_DELETE', 'Delete case');
```

Kalau permission ini langsung diberikan ke role yang salah, user bisa mendapat akses destructive.

Contoh lain:

```sql id="yfazna"
UPDATE case_app.case_status_reference
SET is_active = false
WHERE code = 'UNDER_REVIEW';
```

Jika status ini masih dipakai oleh Camunda process atau query MyBatis, proses bisa gagal.

### Risk Map

| Seed Change | Possible Impact |
|---|---|
| Add status | API/client harus tahu value baru. |
| Rename status code | Breaking untuk mapper, workflow, report. |
| Deactivate status | Existing rows/process bisa invalid. |
| Add permission | Bisa membuka akses baru. |
| Remove permission | Bisa memblokir user/job. |
| Change SLA config | Bisa mengubah escalation behavior. |
| Change routing config | Bisa mengirim case ke team salah. |
| Change lookup sort order | Bisa mengubah UI/report behavior. |
| Test seed leaked to prod | Data integrity/compliance issue. |

---

## 5. Idempotency Principle

Seed script harus aman bila dijalankan ulang.

Buruk:

```sql id="otsq4s"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order)
VALUES ('OPEN', 'Open', 10);
```

Jika dijalankan ulang, bisa gagal karena duplicate primary key.

Lebih baik:

```sql id="vcovqm"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
VALUES ('OPEN', 'Open', 10, true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

PostgreSQL `ON CONFLICT` memungkinkan `INSERT` mengambil aksi alternatif ketika row yang diusulkan melanggar unique atau exclusion constraint; ini umum dipakai untuk pola upsert seed/reference data. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 6. `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` vs `DO UPDATE`

### `DO NOTHING`

```sql id="kmctdr"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order)
VALUES ('OPEN', 'Open', 10)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;
```

Cocok jika:

- seed hanya membuat value awal;
- perubahan label/sort order tidak boleh overwrite manual config;
- data setelah dibuat dikelola oleh admin UI/config tool.

Risiko:

- jika label berubah di repository, DB tidak ikut berubah;
- drift antar environment bisa terjadi.

---

### `DO UPDATE`

```sql id="kq6o5s"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
VALUES ('OPEN', 'Open', 10, true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

Cocok jika:

- repository adalah source of truth;
- value harus konsisten antar environment;
- perubahan label/sort order memang bagian dari release.

Risiko:

- bisa overwrite perubahan manual;
- bisa mengubah behavior production secara diam-diam;
- harus ada owner dan review.

---

## 7. PostgreSQL `MERGE` for Seed

PostgreSQL juga punya `MERGE`, yang dapat melakukan `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, atau `DELETE` secara kondisional dalam satu statement berdasarkan source data. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-merge.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```sql id="7sm58o"
MERGE INTO case_app.case_status_reference AS target
USING (
    VALUES
        ('OPEN', 'Open', 10, true),
        ('CLOSED', 'Closed', 20, true)
) AS source(code, label, sort_order, is_active)
ON target.code = source.code
WHEN MATCHED THEN
    UPDATE SET
        label = source.label,
        sort_order = source.sort_order,
        is_active = source.is_active
WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
    INSERT (code, label, sort_order, is_active)
    VALUES (source.code, source.label, source.sort_order, source.is_active);
```

### Kapan Pakai `MERGE`?

| Use Case | `ON CONFLICT` | `MERGE` |
|---|---:|---:|
| Simple upsert by unique key | Sangat cocok | Bisa, tapi lebih verbose. |
| Multiple conditional branches | Kurang cocok | Lebih cocok. |
| Conditional delete/deactivate | Kurang langsung | Lebih ekspresif. |
| Bulk sync dari staging/source table | Bisa | Cocok. |

Untuk seed sederhana, `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT` biasanya lebih ringkas. Untuk sinkronisasi reference data kompleks, `MERGE` bisa lebih jelas.

---

## 8. Versioned Seed vs Repeatable Seed

## 8.1 Versioned Seed

Contoh Flyway:

```text id="vkl7kk"
V001020__seed_case_status_reference.sql
V001021__seed_case_priority_reference.sql
V001022__seed_case_permissions.sql
```

Cocok untuk:

- initial reference data;
- permission baru;
- config baru;
- one-time mapping;
- data correction spesifik.

### Strength

| Strength | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Audit jelas | Seed tertentu terjadi di versi tertentu. |
| Aman secara historis | Tidak berubah diam-diam. |
| Cocok untuk regulated system | Bisa ditelusuri dari PR/release. |

### Weakness

| Weakness | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Banyak file | Setiap perubahan kecil jadi migration baru. |
| State akhir tersebar | Untuk tahu current reference data, harus baca banyak file. |
| Tidak ideal untuk full catalog sync | Jika catalog besar dan sering berubah. |

---

## 8.2 Repeatable Seed

Contoh Flyway:

```text id="q3p95x"
R__reference_case_status.sql
R__reference_case_priority.sql
R__security_permissions.sql
```

Flyway repeatable migration dijalankan ulang ketika checksum berubah dan lazim dipakai untuk bulk reference data reinserts selain view/procedure/function. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repeatable-migrations-273973335.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Cocok untuk:

- full catalog sync;
- reference data kecil yang repository menjadi source of truth;
- permission catalog yang ingin selalu konsisten;
- lookup table yang tidak diedit manual.

### Risk

Repeatable seed bisa overwrite data production setiap kali file berubah. Maka harus jelas:

```text id="m36711"
Is repository the source of truth,
or is production/admin UI the source of truth?
```

---

## 9. Decision: Versioned or Repeatable?

| Seed Type | Recommended |
|---|---|
| Status lifecycle core | Versioned, unless catalog fully repository-owned. |
| Priority/reference lookup | Versioned or repeatable depending ownership. |
| Permission catalog | Often repeatable, but role assignment should be versioned/approved. |
| Role-permission mapping | Versioned with approval. |
| SLA config | Versioned or config service; avoid silent repeatable overwrite. |
| Test fixture | Not production migration. |
| Demo data | Demo-only path/context. |
| One-time repair | Versioned. |
| Backfill mapping table | Versioned temporary. |
| Country/currency catalog | Repeatable if source-of-truth file is controlled. |

---

## 10. Seed Ownership

Setiap seed harus punya owner.

| Seed Object | Owner | Reviewer |
|---|---|---|
| Case status | Domain owner | Tech lead / business analyst |
| Priority | Domain owner | Product/business reviewer |
| Permission | Security/platform owner | Security reviewer |
| Role mapping | Business owner + security owner | Access governance |
| SLA config | Operations/domain owner | Tech lead |
| Escalation rule | Workflow owner | Camunda/process reviewer |
| Notification template | Product/ops owner | Content/business reviewer |
| Test fixture | QA/dev owner | Dev reviewer |

Core rule:

```text id="hxopb7"
No anonymous seed.
If seed affects behavior, it needs domain ownership.
```

---

## 11. Schema Design for Reference Data

Contoh table reference yang production-friendly:

```sql id="ja6t31"
CREATE TABLE case_app.case_status_reference (
    code            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    label           TEXT NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    sort_order      INTEGER NOT NULL,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    is_terminal     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_by      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'migration'
);
```

### Why These Columns?

| Column | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `code` | Stable machine-readable value. |
| `label` | Human-readable display. |
| `description` | Documentation. |
| `sort_order` | UI/report ordering. |
| `is_active` | Soft disable without deleting. |
| `is_terminal` | Business invariant. |
| `created_at/updated_at` | Audit. |
| `updated_by` | Source of change. |

---

## 12. Stable Code vs Mutable Label

### Good

```text id="8lmjw9"
code  = UNDER_REVIEW
label = Under Review
```

`code` stabil untuk system.

`label` bisa berubah untuk display.

### Bad

```text id="znpd5o"
code = Dalam Review
```

Karena jika label bahasa/display berubah, code ikut berubah dan semua consumer bisa rusak.

### Rule

```text id="jlg244"
Use stable machine code.
Do not use display text as system identity.
```

---

## 13. Do Not Delete Reference Data Too Early

Buruk:

```sql id="k7bj1t"
DELETE FROM case_app.case_status_reference
WHERE code = 'IN_PROGRESS';
```

Jika masih ada row:

```sql id="a6g51v"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE status = 'IN_PROGRESS';
```

Atau Camunda process masih memakai status itu, penghapusan bisa memicu error.

Lebih aman:

```sql id="a4i4s1"
UPDATE case_app.case_status_reference
SET is_active = false,
    updated_at = now(),
    updated_by = 'migration:V001080'
WHERE code = 'IN_PROGRESS';
```

Contract deletion dilakukan jauh belakangan, jika benar-benar perlu.

---

## 14. Idempotent Reference Seed Example

```sql id="i336hs"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (
    code,
    label,
    description,
    sort_order,
    is_active,
    is_terminal,
    updated_by
)
VALUES
    (
        'DRAFT',
        'Draft',
        'Case is being prepared and not yet submitted',
        10,
        true,
        false,
        'migration:V001020'
    ),
    (
        'OPEN',
        'Open',
        'Case is active and ready for processing',
        20,
        true,
        false,
        'migration:V001020'
    ),
    (
        'UNDER_REVIEW',
        'Under Review',
        'Case is under formal review',
        30,
        true,
        false,
        'migration:V001020'
    ),
    (
        'CLOSED',
        'Closed',
        'Case has reached a terminal closed state',
        90,
        true,
        true,
        'migration:V001020'
    )
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    description = EXCLUDED.description,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active,
    is_terminal = EXCLUDED.is_terminal,
    updated_at = now(),
    updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by;
```

---

## 15. Permission Seed

Permission seed lebih sensitif dari lookup biasa.

### Permission Catalog

```sql id="57czlv"
CREATE TABLE security.permission_reference (
    code          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    label         TEXT NOT NULL,
    description   TEXT,
    resource      TEXT NOT NULL,
    action        TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_active     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

### Seed Permission

```sql id="wokwhu"
INSERT INTO security.permission_reference (
    code,
    label,
    description,
    resource,
    action,
    is_active
)
VALUES
    (
        'CASE_READ',
        'Read case',
        'Allows reading case details',
        'CASE',
        'READ',
        true
    ),
    (
        'CASE_REASSIGN',
        'Reassign case',
        'Allows reassignment of case ownership',
        'CASE',
        'REASSIGN',
        true
    )
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    description = EXCLUDED.description,
    resource = EXCLUDED.resource,
    action = EXCLUDED.action,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active,
    updated_at = now();
```

---

## 16. Role-Permission Mapping

Role assignment is riskier than permission catalog.

```sql id="99yc5z"
INSERT INTO security.role_permission (
    role_code,
    permission_code,
    granted_by
)
VALUES
    ('CASE_SUPERVISOR', 'CASE_REASSIGN', 'migration:V001030')
ON CONFLICT (role_code, permission_code) DO NOTHING;
```

### Why `DO NOTHING`?

For role-permission assignment, overwriting may be dangerous. If access governance has removed a permission manually or through IAM sync, repeatable `DO UPDATE` may re-grant unintended access.

### Review Requirement

| Change | Reviewer |
|---|---|
| Add permission catalog | Security/platform reviewer. |
| Grant permission to role | Business owner + security reviewer. |
| Remove permission | Security reviewer + operational impact check. |
| Rename permission code | Treat as breaking change. |

---

## 17. ACL Template Seed

ACL template often controls default access to new domain object.

```sql id="32iv6z"
CREATE TABLE security.acl_template (
    template_code   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    resource_type   TEXT NOT NULL,
    principal_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
    principal_code  TEXT NOT NULL,
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);
```

Seed:

```sql id="n0x4h1"
INSERT INTO security.acl_template (
    template_code,
    resource_type,
    principal_type,
    principal_code,
    permission_code,
    is_active
)
VALUES
    (
        'CASE_OWNER_READ',
        'CASE',
        'RELATIONSHIP',
        'OWNER',
        'CASE_READ',
        true
    ),
    (
        'CASE_ASSIGNEE_UPDATE',
        'CASE',
        'RELATIONSHIP',
        'ASSIGNEE',
        'CASE_UPDATE',
        true
    )
ON CONFLICT (template_code) DO UPDATE
SET
    resource_type = EXCLUDED.resource_type,
    principal_type = EXCLUDED.principal_type,
    principal_code = EXCLUDED.principal_code,
    permission_code = EXCLUDED.permission_code,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

ACL seed harus direview karena memengaruhi default authorization untuk object baru.

---

## 18. Configuration Seed

Contoh SLA configuration:

```sql id="a1hf54"
CREATE TABLE case_app.case_sla_config (
    config_key      TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    config_value    JSONB NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    is_active       BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_by      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'migration'
);
```

Seed:

```sql id="au69gl"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_sla_config (
    config_key,
    config_value,
    description,
    is_active,
    updated_by
)
VALUES
    (
        'DEFAULT_REVIEW_SLA',
        '{"days": 5, "calendar": "BUSINESS_DAY"}'::jsonb,
        'Default SLA for case review',
        true,
        'migration:V001040'
    )
ON CONFLICT (config_key) DO UPDATE
SET
    config_value = EXCLUDED.config_value,
    description = EXCLUDED.description,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active,
    updated_at = now(),
    updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by;
```

### Warning

Config seed can change runtime behavior. Treat it like code.

---

## 19. Workflow / Camunda-Related Seed

Example task category:

```sql id="k2k53o"
CREATE TABLE case_app.workflow_task_category (
    code          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    label         TEXT NOT NULL,
    description   TEXT,
    is_active     BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);
```

Seed:

```sql id="l5cxt8"
INSERT INTO case_app.workflow_task_category (
    code,
    label,
    description,
    is_active
)
VALUES
    (
        'CASE_REVIEW',
        'Case Review',
        'User task for formal case review',
        true
    ),
    (
        'CASE_APPROVAL',
        'Case Approval',
        'User task for final approval',
        true
    )
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    description = EXCLUDED.description,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

### Camunda Risk

If BPMN/DMN expects a category/status/decision value, seed change can break running process instances.

Checklist:

```text id="asrb1h"
- Is this code used in BPMN condition?
- Is this code used in DMN table?
- Is this code stored as process variable?
- Is this code used by worker query?
- Are old process instances still active?
```

---

## 20. Environment-Specific Seed

### Liquibase Context

Liquibase contexts are tags that control whether commands like `update` run certain changesets; runtime context filters decide which context-tagged changesets execute. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/reference-guide/changelog-attributes/what-are-contexts?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Example:

```yaml id="qiy9u8"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 491-test-fixture-case-data
      author: case-service-team
      context: test
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/test/resources/db/seed/test-case-fixture.sql
```

Run only test:

```bash id="mov2wh"
mvn liquibase:update -Dliquibase.contextFilter=test
```

### Liquibase Label

Liquibase labels also control whether commands like `update` run certain changesets, and are added to changesets then filtered at runtime. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/reference-guide/changelog-attributes/what-are-labels?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Example:

```yaml id="5e38iu"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 421-security-permission-seed
      author: security-team
      labels: security,permission,production-approved
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/seed/security/permissions.sql
```

---

## 21. Flyway Environment-Specific Seed

Flyway does not use contexts/labels like Liquibase. Common patterns:

### Pattern A - Separate Locations

```text id="c49p0b"
src/main/resources/db/migration/
  versioned/
  repeatable/

src/test/resources/db/seed/
  test-fixtures.sql

src/demo/resources/db/seed/
  demo-data.sql
```

Local/test config:

```properties id="b4cs7c"
flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable,classpath:db/seed
```

Production config:

```properties id="ff37rb"
flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable
```

### Pattern B - Separate Tool Step

```text id="p9qwab"
Production:
  flyway migrate

Test:
  flyway migrate
  psql -f src/test/resources/db/seed/test-fixtures.sql
```

This keeps test fixture outside production migration path.

---

## 22. Seed Folder Structure

### Flyway

```text id="v7xq2o"
src/main/resources/db/migration/
├─ versioned/
│  ├─ V001020__seed_reference_case_status.sql
│  ├─ V001021__seed_reference_case_priority.sql
│  ├─ V001030__seed_security_permission_catalog.sql
│  ├─ V001031__seed_security_role_permission_mapping.sql
│  └─ V001040__seed_config_case_sla.sql
│
└─ repeatable/
   ├─ R__100_reference_case_status.sql
   ├─ R__110_reference_case_priority.sql
   └─ R__200_security_permission_catalog.sql

src/test/resources/db/seed/
├─ test-users.sql
└─ test-cases.sql

src/demo/resources/db/seed/
└─ demo-case-data.sql
```

### Liquibase

```text id="s3dkcf"
src/main/resources/db/changelog/400-seed/
├─ reference/
│  ├─ 401-case-status.yaml
│  └─ 402-case-priority.yaml
├─ security/
│  ├─ 421-permissions.yaml
│  └─ 422-role-permission.yaml
├─ config/
│  └─ 431-case-sla-config.yaml
├─ workflow/
│  └─ 441-workflow-task-category.yaml
└─ migration-support/
   └─ 481-status-mapping.yaml

src/test/resources/db/seed/
└─ test-fixtures.sql
```

---

## 23. Seed Data Source of Truth

Sebelum memilih strategy, tentukan source of truth.

| Source of Truth | Meaning | Migration Strategy |
|---|---|---|
| Repository | Seed file adalah canonical data. | Repeatable seed boleh, dengan review. |
| Admin UI | Production value dikelola manual/operator. | Initial versioned seed, then avoid overwrite. |
| External IAM | Role/permission mapping dari IAM. | Jangan overwrite dari DB seed kecuali bootstrap. |
| Config service | Runtime config dari config platform. | DB seed hanya fallback/bootstrap. |
| BPMN/DMN | Workflow value melekat pada process definition. | Seed harus release-aligned dengan BPMN/DMN. |
| External master data | Data dari upstream system. | Jangan seed sebagai static data; sync pipeline. |

---

## 24. Seed Conflict Policy

### Policy Option 1 - Repository Wins

```sql id="9mzaev"
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET label = EXCLUDED.label;
```

Use when:

- seed file is canonical;
- manual edit is forbidden;
- changes must go through PR.

### Policy Option 2 - Database Wins

```sql id="6bwfqi"
ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;
```

Use when:

- DB/admin UI may change value;
- migration only bootstraps missing rows;
- repository does not own later edits.

### Policy Option 3 - Hybrid

```sql id="tzvsq3"
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order
WHERE case_app.case_status_reference.updated_by LIKE 'migration:%';
```

Use when:

- migration can update only migration-owned rows;
- manually updated rows are protected.

This is useful, but only if `updated_by` policy is reliable.

---

## 25. Audit Table for Seed Changes

For regulated systems, seed change should be auditable.

```sql id="mbp5jf"
CREATE TABLE case_app.seed_change_audit (
    id              BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    seed_group      TEXT NOT NULL,
    seed_key        TEXT NOT NULL,
    action          TEXT NOT NULL,
    old_value       JSONB,
    new_value       JSONB,
    migration_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
    changed_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    changed_by      TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user
);
```

Example audit insert:

```sql id="4kg7e3"
INSERT INTO case_app.seed_change_audit (
    seed_group,
    seed_key,
    action,
    new_value,
    migration_id
)
VALUES (
    'case_status_reference',
    'UNDER_REVIEW',
    'UPSERT',
    jsonb_build_object(
        'code', 'UNDER_REVIEW',
        'label', 'Under Review',
        'sortOrder', 30
    ),
    'V001020'
);
```

---

## 26. Safer Upsert with Audit

Example with CTE:

```sql id="a78ug1"
WITH seed_data AS (
    SELECT *
    FROM (
        VALUES
            ('OPEN', 'Open', 20, true),
            ('UNDER_REVIEW', 'Under Review', 30, true),
            ('CLOSED', 'Closed', 90, true)
    ) AS v(code, label, sort_order, is_active)
),
upserted AS (
    INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (
        code,
        label,
        sort_order,
        is_active,
        updated_by
    )
    SELECT
        code,
        label,
        sort_order,
        is_active,
        'migration:V001020'
    FROM seed_data
    ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
    SET
        label = EXCLUDED.label,
        sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
        is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active,
        updated_at = now(),
        updated_by = EXCLUDED.updated_by
    RETURNING code, label, sort_order, is_active
)
INSERT INTO case_app.seed_change_audit (
    seed_group,
    seed_key,
    action,
    new_value,
    migration_id
)
SELECT
    'case_status_reference',
    code,
    'UPSERT',
    jsonb_build_object(
        'code', code,
        'label', label,
        'sortOrder', sort_order,
        'isActive', is_active
    ),
    'V001020'
FROM upserted;
```

---

## 27. Avoid Seed Deletion: Prefer Deactivation

Bad:

```sql id="q42e8g"
DELETE FROM case_app.case_priority_reference
WHERE code = 'URGENT';
```

Better:

```sql id="om8rj4"
UPDATE case_app.case_priority_reference
SET
    is_active = false,
    updated_at = now(),
    updated_by = 'migration:V001090'
WHERE code = 'URGENT';
```

Before deactivation:

```sql id="rya9jc"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE priority = 'URGENT';
```

If count > 0, define how existing rows behave.

---

## 28. Seed Dependency Ordering

Recommended order:

```text id="d9fyvv"
1. Reference categories
2. Reference values
3. Permission catalog
4. Role catalog
5. Role-permission mapping
6. ACL template
7. Workflow/task category
8. Config defaults
9. Feature activation seed
10. Test/demo fixtures only outside production path
```

Why?

- role-permission mapping needs permission and role exist;
- ACL template needs permission exist;
- workflow config may reference task/status codes;
- feature activation should happen only after dependent data exists.

---

## 29. Seed and Feature Activation

Do not activate behavior in the same step without guard.

Bad:

```text id="w2y802"
Migration:
  seed new permission
  assign permission
  enable feature
  deploy app
```

Safer:

```text id="610ipo"
Release 1:
  seed permission catalog

Release 2:
  deploy app that understands permission

Release 3:
  assign permission to selected role

Release 4:
  enable feature flag

Release 5:
  broaden rollout
```

---

## 30. Seed and API Contract

If API exposes reference values, seed becomes API contract.

Example response:

```json id="jtgzfb"
{
  "status": "UNDER_REVIEW"
}
```

Changing `UNDER_REVIEW` to `REVIEWING` is breaking.

Safer:

```text id="ulcdhu"
1. Add REVIEWING
2. App accepts both UNDER_REVIEW and REVIEWING
3. Migrate rows
4. API clients migrate
5. Deactivate UNDER_REVIEW
6. Remove later if truly safe
```

---

## 31. Seed and MyBatis

Example mapper:

```xml id="wi69bz"
<select id="findOpenCases" resultMap="CaseResultMap">
    SELECT *
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE status IN ('OPEN', 'UNDER_REVIEW')
</select>
```

If seed deactivates `UNDER_REVIEW`, query semantics change.

Better:

- query status reference table by category;
- centralize status constants;
- test mapper behavior after seed changes.

Example:

```sql id="2m94lv"
SELECT c.*
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
JOIN case_app.case_status_reference s
    ON s.code = c.status
WHERE s.is_active = true
  AND s.is_terminal = false;
```

But beware: this changes semantics and query plan, so test it.

---

## 32. Seed and Camunda

Seed values often appear in BPMN/DMN as string constants.

Example gateway condition:

```text id="n983l3"
${caseStatus == "UNDER_REVIEW"}
```

If seed changes status code, active process instances can fail or take wrong path.

Checklist:

```text id="9u9pmb"
Before changing workflow seed:
  - search BPMN
  - search DMN
  - search worker code
  - search mapper XML
  - search process variable usage
  - check active process instances
```

---

## 33. Seed Testing Strategy

### Test 1 - Migration from Empty DB

```text id="ysp6md"
Fresh PostgreSQL
  → run migrations
  → assert seed rows exist
```

Example:

```sql id="if35le"
SELECT code
FROM case_app.case_status_reference
ORDER BY sort_order;
```

Expected:

```text id="x9vhh9"
DRAFT
OPEN
UNDER_REVIEW
CLOSED
```

---

### Test 2 - Idempotency

```text id="cuuxch"
Run seed migration twice
  → no duplicate rows
  → expected values unchanged
```

For Flyway versioned migration, repeated exact migration does not rerun normally, but local seed scripts or repeatable seed should be tested for idempotency.

---

### Test 3 - Environment Leakage

Ensure test/demo data not present in production migration path.

```sql id="nzz2uu"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE case_number LIKE 'DEMO-%';
```

Expected in production:

```text id="qgn4c7"
0
```

---

### Test 4 - Permission Regression

```sql id="v6ssrm"
SELECT permission_code
FROM security.role_permission
WHERE role_code = 'CASE_SUPERVISOR'
ORDER BY permission_code;
```

Assert no unexpected permission grants.

---

### Test 5 - Workflow Compatibility

Run representative BPMN process tests with seeded values.

---

## 34. CI/CD Gates for Seed Data

```text id="wu15hu"
Pull Request
  ├─ Detect seed file changes
  ├─ Require owner/reviewer based on seed category
  ├─ Run migration on fresh DB
  ├─ Run seed integrity tests
  ├─ Run MyBatis tests
  ├─ Run authorization tests if permission seed changed
  ├─ Run workflow tests if workflow seed changed
  └─ Block test/demo seed in production path
```

### Path-Based Review

| Changed Path | Required Reviewer |
|---|---|
| `seed/reference/**` | Domain owner |
| `seed/security/**` | Security owner |
| `seed/config/**` | Domain + operations owner |
| `seed/workflow/**` | Workflow/Camunda owner |
| `seed/test/**` | QA/dev reviewer |
| `seed/demo/**` | Demo environment owner |

---

## 35. Dangerous Seed Detector

Simple CI script:

```bash id="e1iu0n"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="${1:-src/main/resources/db}"

echo "Checking for risky seed patterns..."

if grep -RniE "DELETE FROM .*_reference|TRUNCATE|DROP TABLE|DROP SCHEMA" "$ROOT"; then
  echo "Risky destructive seed/reference data operation detected."
  exit 1
fi

if grep -RniE "DEMO-|TEST-|dummy|fake|sample" "$ROOT"; then
  echo "Possible test/demo seed found in production migration path."
  exit 1
fi

echo "Seed check passed."
```

This is a rough guard, not a substitute for human review.

---

## 36. Flyway Seed Examples

### Versioned Reference Seed

```text id="2zjg23"
V001020__seed_reference_case_status.sql
```

```sql id="8ynt9f"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference (
    code,
    label,
    sort_order,
    is_active
)
VALUES
    ('DRAFT', 'Draft', 10, true),
    ('OPEN', 'Open', 20, true),
    ('UNDER_REVIEW', 'Under Review', 30, true),
    ('CLOSED', 'Closed', 90, true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    sort_order = EXCLUDED.sort_order,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

### Repeatable Permission Catalog

```text id="mxio9t"
R__200_security_permission_catalog.sql
```

```sql id="9ki02e"
INSERT INTO security.permission_reference (
    code,
    label,
    resource,
    action,
    is_active
)
VALUES
    ('CASE_READ', 'Read case', 'CASE', 'READ', true),
    ('CASE_UPDATE', 'Update case', 'CASE', 'UPDATE', true),
    ('CASE_REASSIGN', 'Reassign case', 'CASE', 'REASSIGN', true)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
SET
    label = EXCLUDED.label,
    resource = EXCLUDED.resource,
    action = EXCLUDED.action,
    is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active,
    updated_at = now();
```

Use repeatable only if repository owns full catalog.

---

## 37. Liquibase Seed Examples

### Reference Seed with Context and Label

```yaml id="2g889h"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 401-case-status-reference-seed
      author: case-service-team
      context: prod,staging,dev,test
      labels: reference-data,case-status
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/main/resources/db/changelog/400-seed/reference/case-status.sql
            splitStatements: false
            stripComments: false
```

### Test Fixture Only

```yaml id="l819ic"
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 491-test-case-fixture
      author: case-service-team
      context: test
      labels: test-fixture
      changes:
        - sqlFile:
            path: src/test/resources/db/seed/test-case-fixture.sql
            splitStatements: false
            stripComments: false
```

### Important

Production command should not run with `contextFilter=test`.

---

## 38. Seed Rollback / Roll-forward

### Rollback by Delete

```sql id="ws00zq"
DELETE FROM case_app.case_status_reference
WHERE code = 'UNDER_REVIEW';
```

Risky if rows already reference it.

### Safer Roll-forward

```sql id="zy43xa"
UPDATE case_app.case_status_reference
SET is_active = false,
    updated_by = 'migration:V001099-disable-under-review'
WHERE code = 'UNDER_REVIEW';
```

### Permission Rollback

Instead of deleting permission:

```sql id="4f6rvx"
UPDATE security.permission_reference
SET is_active = false
WHERE code = 'CASE_REASSIGN';
```

Then separately remove role mapping:

```sql id="iz7ic7"
DELETE FROM security.role_permission
WHERE permission_code = 'CASE_REASSIGN';
```

Access changes must be approved because rollback can remove legitimate access.

---

## 39. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous | Better |
|---|---|---|
| Test seed in production migration folder | Dummy data can leak to prod. | Separate test path/context. |
| Non-idempotent seed | Fails on rerun/recovery. | Use `ON CONFLICT`/`MERGE`. |
| Hard delete reference value | Existing rows/workflows break. | Deactivate first. |
| Rename code value | Breaks API, mapper, workflow. | Add new code, migrate, deactivate old. |
| Repeatable seed without ownership | Silent overwrite. | Define source of truth. |
| Permission seed without security review | Access control incident. | Security approval required. |
| Role-permission mapping with broad overwrite | Unintended privilege grant. | Use explicit grants, audit. |
| Config seed used as hidden feature flag | Behavior changes unexpectedly. | Separate config and activation. |
| Seed value duplicated in Java strings | Drift between DB and app. | Centralize constants/tests. |
| No seed verification test | Broken lookup appears at runtime. | Seed integrity tests. |

---

## 40. Production Seed Review Checklist

| Question | Required Answer |
|---|---|
| What seed category is this? | Reference/security/config/workflow/test/demo. |
| Who owns this seed? | Named team/role. |
| Is repository source of truth? | Yes/no. |
| Is script idempotent? | Must be yes. |
| Does it overwrite existing values? | Explicitly state. |
| Does it grant/revoke permission? | Security review. |
| Does it affect Camunda/BPMN/DMN? | Workflow review. |
| Does it affect API response values? | Contract review. |
| Does it affect MyBatis queries? | Mapper test. |
| Is rollback safe? | Usually prefer roll-forward/deactivation. |
| Could test/demo data leak? | Must be impossible by path/context. |
| Is there audit evidence? | PR + migration log + optional audit table. |

---

## 41. Practical Recommendation for Your Stack

Untuk microservice **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Camunda**, saya rekomendasikan:

```text id="gm16da"
Reference data:
  Versioned migration by default.
  Repeatable only if repository is explicit source of truth.

Permission catalog:
  Repeatable can work.
  Role-permission assignment should be versioned and security-reviewed.

Config seed:
  Versioned or managed outside DB.
  Avoid silent repeatable overwrite.

Workflow seed:
  Versioned and Camunda-aware.
  Search BPMN/DMN/process variables before change.

Test/demo data:
  Never in production migration path.
  Use test resources, contexts, or separate seed step.

Rollback:
  Prefer deactivate/roll-forward instead of hard delete.
```

---

## 42. Ringkasan Seri 8

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| Seed data | Bagian dari application contract, bukan data kecil biasa. |
| Idempotency | Wajib; gunakan `ON CONFLICT` atau `MERGE` sesuai kebutuhan. |
| Versioned seed | Cocok untuk audit dan perubahan historis. |
| Repeatable seed | Cocok jika repository adalah source of truth. |
| Reference data | Gunakan stable code, mutable label. |
| Delete value | Hindari; prefer deactivation. |
| Permission seed | Butuh security review. |
| Role-permission mapping | High-risk, jangan overwrite sembarangan. |
| Config seed | Bisa mengubah runtime behavior. |
| Workflow seed | Harus kompatibel dengan Camunda/BPMN/DMN. |
| Environment seed | Test/demo harus terpisah dari production path. |
| MyBatis | Seed value bisa memengaruhi query semantics. |
| API | Reference code bisa menjadi public contract. |
| Audit | Seed change perlu owner, PR, migration log, dan optional audit table. |

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 9 dari 18**: View Migration. Fokusnya view sebagai kontrak baca untuk MyBatis/API/reporting, bukan sekadar SQL shortcut.
