# Seri 15 - Large Data Backfill & Data Repair Migration

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana melakukan **large data backfill** dan **data repair migration** secara aman di PostgreSQL untuk stack:

```text id="o3mr6w"
Jersey / JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Flyway / Liquibase
Kubernetes Job / CronJob
Camunda
CI/CD
```

Backfill adalah proses mengisi atau memperbaiki data existing setelah schema berubah.

Contoh:

```text id="rl3yfh"
- mengisi kolom baru risk_score untuk 50 juta rows
- mengubah status lama ke status baru
- memperbaiki reference key invalid
- mengisi normalized search key
- menghitung ulang SLA
- membuat data audit/derived yang sebelumnya belum ada
```

Core rule:

```text id="cdqxwg"
Large backfill is not just migration SQL.
It is an operational data movement with risk, progress, retry, and observability.
```

PostgreSQL bahkan mendokumentasikan pola `UPDATE` batch menggunakan CTE dengan `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT`, dan opsi `SKIP LOCKED` untuk mengurangi lock contention dan membuat update diulang sampai tidak ada row tersisa. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="xnbvra"
Large Backfill Mental Model
────────────────────────────────────────────

Schema Expand
  │
  ├─ add nullable column
  ├─ add reference table
  ├─ add new status/code
  └─ add compatibility path
       │
       ▼
Backfill / Repair
  │
  ├─ identify target rows
  ├─ process in chunks
  ├─ commit per batch
  ├─ record progress
  ├─ retry safely
  ├─ throttle load
  └─ validate result
       │
       ▼
Constraint / Switch / Contract
  │
  ├─ validate data
  ├─ add constraint
  ├─ switch app read path
  ├─ remove fallback
  └─ drop old schema later
```

Backfill berada di tengah proses **expand/contract**. Jangan langsung lompat dari schema change ke constraint ketat tanpa memastikan data lama sudah benar.

---

## 3. Kapan Backfill Tidak Boleh Masuk Migration SQL?

Masukkan ke Flyway/Liquibase migration SQL hanya jika data kecil dan predictable.

| Kondisi | Backfill di Migration SQL? | Rekomendasi |
|---|---:|---|
| < ribuan row, quick update | Bisa | Versioned migration cukup. |
| Puluhan ribu row, query ringan | Mungkin | Test durasi di staging. |
| Jutaan row | Biasanya tidak | Kubernetes Job / batch worker. |
| Puluhan juta row | Tidak | Resumable job + monitoring. |
| Tabel hot/high traffic | Tidak | Chunked + throttle + lock timeout. |
| Ada trigger/audit besar | Tidak langsung | Review trigger impact. |
| Butuh external lookup/API | Tidak | App/batch job, bukan DB migration. |
| Butuh business approval per batch | Tidak | Operational workflow. |
| Bisa gagal di tengah dan perlu resume | Tidak | Progress table wajib. |

Core rule:

```text id="ucx92j"
DDL migration should be short and deterministic.
Large data movement should be observable and resumable.
```

---

## 4. Backfill Risk Map

| Risk | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Long transaction | Lock lebih lama, rollback mahal, connection/pipeline timeout. |
| WAL growth | Update besar menghasilkan WAL besar dan bisa memicu replication lag. |
| Table/index bloat | Update banyak row membuat dead tuples dan index bloat. |
| Autovacuum pressure | Vacuum perlu membersihkan row lama setelah update besar. |
| Lock contention | App write/read bisa menunggu row/table lock. |
| Deadlock | Update tanpa ordering konsisten bisa bertabrakan dengan transaksi lain. |
| Trigger explosion | Audit/notification trigger bisa menghasilkan data/event masif. |
| Constraint failure | Data invalid muncul di tengah proses. |
| Retry duplication | Job retry membuat data dobel jika tidak idempotent. |
| Partial state | Sebagian row sudah berubah, sebagian belum. |
| API inconsistency | App membaca data lama dan baru selama transisi. |
| Camunda retry/incident | Worker bisa mengeksekusi ulang operation yang tidak idempotent. |

---

## 5. Golden Pattern

```text id="9fakod"
1. Expand schema safely
2. Deploy app compatible with old + new data
3. Backfill in chunks
4. Validate completion
5. Add/validate constraints
6. Switch app read path
7. Contract old schema later
```

Contoh:

```text id="qul9p6"
Requirement:
  add required risk_score

Release 1:
  add nullable risk_score

Release 2:
  app writes risk_score for new rows

Operational job:
  backfill risk_score for old rows

Release 3:
  add CHECK/NOT NULL after validation
```

---

## 6. Bad Backfill Pattern

```sql id="6ofc91"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score = 0
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

Masalah:

```text id="ral1au"
- satu transaksi besar
- sulit tahu progress
- sulit pause/resume
- rollback mahal
- trigger bisa fire untuk semua row
- WAL besar
- replication lag
- bisa timeout
```

Backfill besar harus **chunked**.

---

## 7. Simple Chunked Backfill Pattern

```sql id="6doplr"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id;
```

Ulangi sampai:

```sql id="86vtd3"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

menghasilkan:

```text id="x1f21h"
0
```

PostgreSQL docs menyarankan pola batch update dengan `ORDER BY` agar prioritas dan urutan update konsisten; urutan yang konsisten juga bisa membantu mencegah deadlock bila operasi update lain memakai ordering yang sama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 8. Chunked Backfill dengan `SKIP LOCKED`

Jika ada multiple workers atau row bisa sedang dikunci transaksi aplikasi:

```sql id="ewzzij"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id;
```

`FOR UPDATE` mengunci row yang dipilih terhadap update concurrent, dan `SKIP LOCKED` dapat dipakai untuk melewati row yang sedang terkunci agar worker tidak menunggu lama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-select.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Kapan Cocok?

| Kondisi | Cocok? |
|---|---:|
| Banyak worker memproses table sama | Ya |
| App transaksi sering lock row | Ya |
| Urutan absolut tidak penting | Ya |
| Harus proses strictly ordered | Tidak |
| Setiap row harus diproses sekali persis tanpa audit tambahan | Perlu progress/idempotency |

---

## 9. Backfill dengan Primary Key Cursor

Jika ID sortable dan stabil:

```sql id="ghlnkw"
SELECT id
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE id > :lastProcessedId
  AND risk_score IS NULL
ORDER BY id
LIMIT :batchSize;
```

Lalu update:

```sql id="9vnsc5"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score = 0
WHERE id = ANY(:ids);
```

### Cocok untuk

- sequential numeric ID;
- time-sortable ID;
- batch worker yang menyimpan cursor;
- satu worker utama.

### Kurang Cocok untuk

- UUID random tanpa meaningful order;
- row baru disisipkan di bawah cursor;
- condition target berubah saat proses.

---

## 10. Backfill dengan `ctid`

PostgreSQL docs memberi contoh batch update menggunakan `ctid` dalam CTE dan menyatakan pendekatan `ctid` aman dalam pola update yang diulang karena query menghindari masalah `ctid` berubah setelah update. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```sql id="ngs7ct"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT ctid
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    LIMIT 1000
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.ctid = batch.ctid;
```

### Practical Caution

Saya tetap lebih suka primary key jika tersedia.

Gunakan `ctid` hanya jika:

```text id="4awvww"
- table punya primary key buruk/tidak ada
- batch langsung update row hasil CTE
- tidak menyimpan ctid sebagai progress jangka panjang
- sudah diuji di staging
```

---

## 11. Progress Table Pattern

Untuk backfill besar, buat progress table.

```sql id="u3p6r8"
CREATE TABLE case_app.migration_job_progress (
    job_name              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    status                TEXT NOT NULL,
    last_processed_key    TEXT,
    total_processed       BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_failed          BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    batch_size            INTEGER NOT NULL,
    started_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    last_heartbeat_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at          TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message         TEXT,
    updated_at            TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Insert initial state:

```sql id="8dr3e1"
INSERT INTO case_app.migration_job_progress (
    job_name,
    status,
    batch_size,
    last_heartbeat_at
)
VALUES (
    'backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score',
    'RUNNING',
    1000,
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (job_name) DO UPDATE
SET
    status = 'RUNNING',
    last_heartbeat_at = now(),
    updated_at = now();
```

---

## 12. Per-Batch Audit Table

Untuk repair yang risk-nya tinggi:

```sql id="61f24y"
CREATE TABLE case_app.migration_job_batch_log (
    id                  BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    job_name             TEXT NOT NULL,
    batch_no             BIGINT NOT NULL,
    status               TEXT NOT NULL,
    selected_count       INTEGER NOT NULL,
    updated_count        INTEGER NOT NULL,
    started_at           TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_ms          BIGINT,
    error_message        TEXT
);
```

Useful untuk:

```text id="r5j24d"
- audit
- resume
- performance tuning
- troubleshooting
- compliance evidence
```

---

## 13. Idempotency

Backfill harus aman bila dijalankan ulang.

Good:

```sql id="05lmxd"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score = 0
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

Karena hanya row yang belum diproses yang diubah.

Bad:

```sql id="sxeduf"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET retry_count = retry_count + 1
WHERE migration_flag = false;
```

Retry bisa menggandakan efek.

### Idempotency Rules

| Rule | Example |
|---|---|
| Update only missing/old state | `WHERE new_column IS NULL` |
| Use deterministic computation | `risk_score = fn(old fields)` |
| Use unique key for insert | `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING/UPDATE` |
| Store operation id | `migration_run_id` |
| Avoid additive increments unless intentional | Jangan `counter = counter + 1` pada retry |
| Validate before and after | Count mismatch harus terlihat |

---

## 14. Backfill Insert dengan `ON CONFLICT`

Contoh membuat audit seed untuk existing case:

```sql id="2882vb"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_audit_log (
    case_id,
    operation,
    new_row,
    changed_at,
    changed_by
)
SELECT
    c.id,
    'BACKFILL_INITIAL_AUDIT',
    to_jsonb(c),
    now(),
    'migration:backfill_initial_audit'
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
WHERE NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM case_app.case_audit_log a
    WHERE a.case_id = c.id
      AND a.operation = 'BACKFILL_INITIAL_AUDIT'
);
```

Better dengan unique constraint:

```sql id="swgrmh"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_case_audit_initial_backfill
ON case_app.case_audit_log (case_id, operation)
WHERE operation = 'BACKFILL_INITIAL_AUDIT';
```

Then:

```sql id="f2c8k6"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_audit_log (
    case_id,
    operation,
    new_row,
    changed_at,
    changed_by
)
SELECT
    c.id,
    'BACKFILL_INITIAL_AUDIT',
    to_jsonb(c),
    now(),
    'migration:backfill_initial_audit'
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;
```

---

## 15. Lock Timeout dan Statement Timeout

Gunakan timeout guard untuk mencegah job menggantung.

```sql id="rwtqb8"
SET lock_timeout = '2s';
SET statement_timeout = '30s';
```

`lock_timeout` hanya berlaku saat menunggu lock, sedangkan `statement_timeout` membatasi durasi statement; PostgreSQL docs menyebut tidak berguna menyetel `lock_timeout` sama atau lebih besar dari `statement_timeout` karena statement timeout akan terjadi dulu. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/runtime-config-client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Practical Setting

| Context | `lock_timeout` | `statement_timeout` |
|---|---:|---:|
| Small batch | 1-2s | 10-30s |
| Medium batch | 2-5s | 30-120s |
| Large controlled batch | 5-10s | 5-15min |
| DDL constraint validate | 5-30s | sesuai maintenance window |

Jangan copy angka ini mentah-mentah. Sesuaikan dengan latency, workload, dan SLO internal.

---

## 16. Batch Size Tuning

Mulai kecil, naik bertahap.

```text id="d5vx17"
batch_size = 500
sleep_ms = 100
```

Jika aman:

```text id="072d5i"
batch_size = 1000
sleep_ms = 50
```

Jika DB pressure tinggi:

```text id="qeg6ug"
batch_size = 100
sleep_ms = 500
```

### Metrics untuk Tuning

| Metric | Sinyal |
|---|---|
| Batch duration | Terlalu tinggi → kecilkan batch. |
| Lock wait | Ada contention → pakai SKIP LOCKED/throttle. |
| DB CPU | Tinggi → throttle. |
| WAL generation | Tinggi → kecilkan batch/schedule off-peak. |
| Replication lag | Naik → pause/throttle. |
| Autovacuum activity | Pressure tinggi → pause/coordinate. |
| App latency | Naik → throttle/stop. |
| Error rate | Naik → stop dan investigate. |

---

## 17. Java Worker Pattern

Untuk backfill besar, implementasi Java worker sering lebih aman daripada SQL migration besar.

```text id="5hulfd"
Kubernetes Job
  └─ Java Backfill Worker
       ├─ read batch IDs
       ├─ update batch
       ├─ commit
       ├─ record progress
       ├─ sleep/throttle
       ├─ retry transient error
       └─ stop when complete
```

### Pseudocode

```java id="usyxpd"
public final class RiskScoreBackfillJob {
    private final CaseBackfillMapper mapper;
    private final TransactionTemplate tx;
    private final int batchSize;
    private final Duration sleep;

    public void run() {
        while (true) {
            int updated = tx.execute(() -> {
                List<UUID> ids = mapper.findBatchForRiskScoreBackfill(batchSize);
                if (ids.isEmpty()) {
                    return 0;
                }

                int count = mapper.backfillRiskScore(ids);
                mapper.recordProgress("backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score", count);
                return count;
            });

            if (updated == 0) {
                mapper.markCompleted("backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score");
                return;
            }

            sleepQuietly(sleep);
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 18. MyBatis Mapper Example

### Select Batch

```xml id="gw1n8v"
<select id="findBatchForRiskScoreBackfill" resultType="java.util.UUID">
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT #{batchSize}
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
</select>
```

### Update Batch

```xml id="m0u2gs"
<update id="backfillRiskScore">
    UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
    SET risk_score = 0
    WHERE id = ANY(#{ids, typeHandler=com.example.UuidArrayTypeHandler})
      AND risk_score IS NULL
</update>
```

### Simpler Alternative

Use one SQL statement:

```xml id="gtyqmy"
<update id="backfillRiskScoreBatch">
    WITH batch AS (
        SELECT id
        FROM case_app.enforcement_case
        WHERE risk_score IS NULL
        ORDER BY id
        LIMIT #{batchSize}
        FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    )
    UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
    SET risk_score = 0
    FROM batch
    WHERE c.id = batch.id
</update>
```

---

## 19. Transaction Boundary

Recommended:

```text id="w589h8"
one batch = one transaction
```

Why:

| Benefit | Explanation |
|---|---|
| Short lock duration | Row locks released per batch. |
| Retry easier | Failed batch rerun only. |
| Progress meaningful | Commit and progress align. |
| Less rollback cost | No huge transaction rollback. |
| Operational pause | Job can stop between batches. |

Avoid:

```text id="x5n6zm"
one huge transaction for millions of rows
```

---

## 20. Progress Update Must Be in Same Transaction?

Depends.

### Same Transaction

```text id="3ijnel"
update rows
update progress
commit
```

Pros:

- progress only recorded if data commit succeeds.

Cons:

- if progress table hot, can add contention.

### Separate Transaction

```text id="adownb"
update rows commit
update progress commit
```

Pros:

- simpler in some worker models.

Cons:

- progress can be stale if progress update fails.

Recommended default:

```text id="ifsa9t"
Same transaction for per-batch count.
Separate heartbeat allowed.
```

---

## 21. Heartbeat

Long-running job should update heartbeat.

```sql id="xtly2f"
UPDATE case_app.migration_job_progress
SET
    last_heartbeat_at = now(),
    updated_at = now()
WHERE job_name = 'backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score';
```

Use heartbeat for:

```text id="ruy9uu"
- detecting stuck job
- dashboard/alerting
- safe manual intervention
- distinguishing slow vs dead
```

---

## 22. Pause / Resume / Stop Control

Add control table:

```sql id="xvcn7r"
CREATE TABLE case_app.migration_job_control (
    job_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    requested_state TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_by TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user
);
```

Values:

```text id="5f5lsd"
RUN
PAUSE
STOP
```

Worker checks before each batch:

```sql id="6vcfkb"
SELECT requested_state
FROM case_app.migration_job_control
WHERE job_name = 'backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score';
```

### Behavior

| State | Worker Behavior |
|---|---|
| `RUN` | Continue. |
| `PAUSE` | Sleep and heartbeat. |
| `STOP` | Exit gracefully. |

---

## 23. Concurrency Control for Multiple Workers

If using multiple workers, avoid duplicate work with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`.

Worker query:

```sql id="mcgox7"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id
RETURNING c.id;
```

PostgreSQL docs explicitly mention `SKIP LOCKED` can be added to the CTE when lock contention is a concern to prevent multiple commands from updating the same row. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-update.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

### Multiple Worker Rules

```text id="fxbdi4"
- same deterministic WHERE condition
- idempotent update
- commit per batch
- bounded worker count
- central progress aggregation
- stop if replication lag / app latency too high
```

---

## 24. Advisory Lock untuk Single Job Instance

Untuk memastikan hanya satu job utama berjalan:

```sql id="t1pzyi"
SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext('backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score'));
```

If false:

```text id="mi4okz"
another worker is already running
```

Release:

```sql id="h17ysa"
SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext('backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score'));
```

### Use Case

- one logical job instance;
- prevent accidental duplicate Kubernetes Job;
- simple coordination.

### Caution

- session-level advisory lock hilang saat connection close;
- transaction-level advisory lock bisa dipakai jika sesuai;
- jangan jadikan advisory lock satu-satunya correctness guarantee.

---

## 25. Backfill and Triggers

Sebelum backfill, list trigger:

```sql id="8v4c0u"
SELECT
    trigger_schema,
    event_object_schema,
    event_object_table,
    trigger_name,
    action_timing,
    event_manipulation
FROM information_schema.triggers
WHERE event_object_schema = 'case_app'
  AND event_object_table = 'enforcement_case'
ORDER BY trigger_name;
```

Review:

| Trigger | Backfill Concern |
|---|---|
| `updated_at` | Apakah semua row boleh terlihat updated sekarang? |
| audit trigger | Apakah audit log akan membengkak? |
| validation trigger | Apakah data lama valid? |
| notification trigger | Apakah akan emit event massal? |
| derived trigger | Apakah derived data perlu ikut berubah? |

### Rule

```text id="4cbsm9"
Do not disable triggers casually.
If trigger behavior should be skipped, document compensating validation.
```

---

## 26. Backfill and `updated_at`

Masalah umum:

```sql id="zwfn5g"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
SET risk_score = 0
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

`updated_at` trigger membuat semua row tampak baru diubah.

Options:

| Option | Use When |
|---|---|
| Accept `updated_at` change | Data repair memang dianggap update. |
| Preserve `updated_at` | Business semantics butuh original update time. |
| Add `backfilled_at` separately | Ingin audit migration tanpa mengubah business timestamp. |
| Temporarily adjust trigger | High-risk; review. |

### Preserve `updated_at` Pattern

If trigger always overrides `updated_at`, this won’t work unless trigger allows preservation.

Better trigger logic:

```sql id="8oaf8e"
IF current_setting('case_app.preserve_updated_at', true) = 'on' THEN
    RETURN NEW;
END IF;

NEW.updated_at := now();
RETURN NEW;
```

Use only with governance.

---

## 27. Data Repair Mapping Table

For status migration:

```sql id="owiz6r"
CREATE TABLE case_app.case_status_repair_mapping (
    old_status TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    new_status TEXT NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    approved_by TEXT NOT NULL,
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL
);
```

Seed mapping:

```sql id="iqy28u"
INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_repair_mapping (
    old_status,
    new_status,
    reason,
    approved_by,
    approved_at
)
VALUES
    ('IN_PROGRESS', 'UNDER_REVIEW', 'Status taxonomy migration', 'case-domain-owner', now())
ON CONFLICT (old_status) DO UPDATE
SET
    new_status = EXCLUDED.new_status,
    reason = EXCLUDED.reason,
    approved_by = EXCLUDED.approved_by,
    approved_at = EXCLUDED.approved_at;
```

Backfill:

```sql id="e3b2il"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT c.id, m.new_status
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
    JOIN case_app.case_status_repair_mapping m
        ON m.old_status = c.status
    WHERE c.status <> m.new_status
    ORDER BY c.id
    LIMIT 1000
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET status = batch.new_status
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id;
```

---

## 28. Data Repair Audit

For high-risk repair, record before/after.

```sql id="zjpk0p"
CREATE TABLE case_app.data_repair_audit (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    repair_job_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    table_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    row_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    old_value JSONB,
    new_value JSONB,
    repaired_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    repaired_by TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user
);
```

Backfill with audit:

```sql id="qy4shh"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT c.id, c.status AS old_status, m.new_status
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
    JOIN case_app.case_status_repair_mapping m
        ON m.old_status = c.status
    WHERE c.status <> m.new_status
    ORDER BY c.id
    LIMIT 1000
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
),
updated AS (
    UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
    SET status = batch.new_status
    FROM batch
    WHERE c.id = batch.id
    RETURNING c.id, batch.old_status, c.status AS new_status
)
INSERT INTO case_app.data_repair_audit (
    repair_job_name,
    table_name,
    row_id,
    old_value,
    new_value
)
SELECT
    'repair_case_status_taxonomy',
    'case_app.enforcement_case',
    id::text,
    jsonb_build_object('status', old_status),
    jsonb_build_object('status', new_status)
FROM updated;
```

---

## 29. Validation Queries

Backfill is not done until validation passes.

### Count Remaining

```sql id="ve0qu7"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

### Invalid Values

```sql id="mbu23o"
SELECT risk_score, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE risk_score < 0
   OR risk_score > 100
GROUP BY risk_score;
```

### Mapping Completeness

```sql id="ut53e8"
SELECT c.status, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
LEFT JOIN case_app.case_status_reference r
    ON r.code = c.status
WHERE r.code IS NULL
GROUP BY c.status;
```

### Consistency Between Old/New Columns

```sql id="4bzyel"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE reference_number IS DISTINCT FROM case_number;
```

---

## 30. Add Constraint After Backfill

After validation:

```sql id="6h7b8r"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_range
CHECK (risk_score >= 0 AND risk_score <= 100)
NOT VALID;
```

Then:

```sql id="w60gau"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_range;
```

PostgreSQL docs explain that `NOT VALID` skips the potentially lengthy initial check when adding a constraint, while new inserts/updates are still enforced; later `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` checks existing rows and uses only a `SHARE UPDATE EXCLUSIVE` lock on the table being altered. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-altertable.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 31. Backfill and Foreign Key

Example: add FK after repairing invalid status.

Precheck:

```sql id="ja0rei"
SELECT c.status, COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
LEFT JOIN case_app.case_status_reference r
    ON r.code = c.status
WHERE r.code IS NULL
GROUP BY c.status;
```

Add FK:

```sql id="yapvuw"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT fk_case_status
FOREIGN KEY (status)
REFERENCES case_app.case_status_reference(code)
NOT VALID;
```

Validate:

```sql id="qkdx1y"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT fk_case_status;
```

This pattern separates data repair from constraint enforcement.

---

## 32. Kubernetes Job Pattern

```yaml id="pu19w4"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: Job
metadata:
  name: backfill-enforcement-case-risk-score
spec:
  backoffLimit: 2
  template:
    spec:
      restartPolicy: Never
      containers:
        - name: backfill
          image: case-service-backfill:2026.07.13
          env:
            - name: JOB_NAME
              value: backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score
            - name: BATCH_SIZE
              value: "1000"
            - name: SLEEP_MS
              value: "100"
          envFrom:
            - secretRef:
                name: case-service-db
```

### Production Rule

```text id="h1q7zl"
Backfill image can share code with service,
but execution should be an explicit Job, not normal app pod startup.
```

---

## 33. Kubernetes CronJob for Incremental Repair

Use if repair is long-running or recurring.

```yaml id="fx086x"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: CronJob
metadata:
  name: backfill-risk-score-incremental
spec:
  schedule: "*/10 * * * *"
  concurrencyPolicy: Forbid
  jobTemplate:
    spec:
      template:
        spec:
          restartPolicy: OnFailure
          containers:
            - name: backfill
              image: case-service-backfill:2026.07.13
              env:
                - name: MAX_BATCHES
                  value: "20"
                - name: BATCH_SIZE
                  value: "1000"
```

`concurrencyPolicy: Forbid` helps avoid overlapping CronJob executions.

---

## 34. Feature Flag / Readiness

During backfill, app must handle mixed state.

### Example

Some rows have `risk_score`, some do not.

API response:

```json id="an1fcl"
{
  "caseId": "123",
  "riskScore": null,
  "riskScoreReady": false
}
```

Or service fallback:

```text id="e1z5p2"
if risk_score is null:
    calculate on the fly
else:
    use stored value
```

After completion and constraint:

```text id="pj2onx"
risk_score becomes required
fallback removed later
```

---

## 35. Backfill and Camunda

Camunda workers may retry failed jobs. If a worker triggers repair/backfill:

```text id="j62xhr"
- operation must be idempotent
- duplicate calls must not duplicate side effects
- progress must be persistent
- partial completion must be visible
- BPMN version compatibility must be reviewed
```

Avoid making a long DB backfill a synchronous service task.

Better:

```text id="db6fow"
Camunda starts/request operation
  → create operation record
  → Kubernetes Job / worker performs backfill
  → worker updates operation status
  → process polls/waits for completion
```

---

## 36. Backfill and Replication Lag

Large updates generate WAL. In replicated PostgreSQL environments, this can cause replicas to lag.

Operational checks:

```text id="1u7mkx"
- monitor replication lag
- reduce batch size if lag rises
- pause job if lag exceeds threshold
- avoid backfill during peak write traffic
- coordinate with DBA/platform team
```

### Generic Lag Query

Actual query depends on primary/replica setup, but commonly:

```sql id="9s1dgf"
SELECT
    application_name,
    state,
    sync_state,
    write_lag,
    flush_lag,
    replay_lag
FROM pg_stat_replication;
```

For managed services, also check cloud provider metrics.

---

## 37. Autovacuum and Bloat

Large updates create dead tuples. PostgreSQL progress reporting docs show that `pg_stat_progress_vacuum` reports active VACUUM progress, including autovacuum workers. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/progress-reporting.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Useful checks:

```sql id="dx9nar"
SELECT
    relname,
    n_live_tup,
    n_dead_tup,
    last_vacuum,
    last_autovacuum
FROM pg_stat_user_tables
WHERE schemaname = 'case_app'
  AND relname = 'enforcement_case';
```

Vacuum progress:

```sql id="3b2mpa"
SELECT *
FROM pg_stat_progress_vacuum;
```

### Practical Guidance

```text id="0cz0z4"
- expect dead tuples after big update
- coordinate with DBA before massive backfill
- avoid huge single transaction
- do not disable autovacuum casually
- consider VACUUM/ANALYZE plan after large repair
```

---

## 38. ANALYZE After Large Backfill

If data distribution changes significantly, query planner statistics may become stale.

Run:

```sql id="cvb1ol"
ANALYZE case_app.enforcement_case;
```

For very large changes, coordinate with DBA and monitor.

---

## 40. Rollback vs Roll-Forward

For backfill, rollback is often expensive.

### Rollback Possible

If you stored old value:

```sql id="53mdx4"
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET status = (a.old_value->>'status')
FROM case_app.data_repair_audit a
WHERE a.repair_job_name = 'repair_case_status_taxonomy'
  AND a.row_id = c.id::text;
```

### Roll-forward Preferred

If new value is wrong:

```text id="3aljpo"
create new repair mapping
run corrective repair job
validate
document
```

### Rule

```text id="953pfb"
For large backfill, design recovery before execution.
Do not assume transaction rollback is available after batches commit.
```

---

## 41. Backfill Runbook Template

```md id="rff36t"
# Runbook: Backfill enforcement_case.risk_score

## Goal
Populate risk_score for existing rows.

## Precondition
- Column risk_score exists and is nullable.
- App version writes risk_score for new rows.
- Trigger impact reviewed.
- Backup/restore point available.
- DBA approved batch size.

## Execution
Kubernetes Job:
backfill-enforcement-case-risk-score

Parameters:
- BATCH_SIZE=1000
- SLEEP_MS=100
- MAX_RUNTIME_MINUTES=60

## Monitoring
- migration_job_progress
- DB CPU
- app latency
- replication lag
- dead tuples
- error logs

## Pause
UPDATE case_app.migration_job_control
SET requested_state = 'PAUSE'
WHERE job_name = 'backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score';

## Resume
UPDATE case_app.migration_job_control
SET requested_state = 'RUN'
WHERE job_name = 'backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score';

## Stop
UPDATE case_app.migration_job_control
SET requested_state = 'STOP'
WHERE job_name = 'backfill_enforcement_case_risk_score';

## Validation
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM case_app.enforcement_case WHERE risk_score IS NULL;

Expected:
0

## Post-Execution
- ANALYZE table if needed.
- Add CHECK/NOT NULL constraint in later migration.
- Archive validation report.
```

## 42. CI/CD Gates for Backfill

```text id="z49wf8"
Pull Request
  ├─ Detect large UPDATE/DELETE/INSERT SELECT
  ├─ Require backfill plan if target table is large
  ├─ Require idempotency check
  ├─ Require trigger impact review
  ├─ Require validation queries
  ├─ Require progress/observability plan
  ├─ Require rollback/roll-forward plan
  ├─ Run on seeded PostgreSQL test data
  ├─ Run MyBatis worker test if Java job
  └─ Require DBA/domain owner approval
```

### Dangerous SQL Detector

```bash id="m11ylp"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="${1:-src/main/resources/db}"

if grep -RniE "UPDATE .* SET|DELETE FROM|INSERT INTO .* SELECT" "$ROOT"; then
  echo "Data movement SQL detected. Require backfill/data repair review."
  exit 1
fi

if grep -RniE "UPDATE .* SET" "$ROOT" | grep -vi "LIMIT"; then
  echo "Potential unbounded UPDATE detected."
  exit 1
fi
```

This is crude because SQL formatting varies, but useful as a first gate.

---

## 43. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous | Better |
|---|---|---|
| One huge `UPDATE` for millions of rows | Long transaction, WAL, lock, timeout. | Chunked batches. |
| Backfill inside app pod startup | Every pod may run it; startup unstable. | Explicit Kubernetes Job. |
| No progress table | Cannot resume or observe. | Persistent progress. |
| Non-idempotent repair | Retry corrupts data. | Idempotent `WHERE` / unique keys. |
| No trigger review | Audit/notification explosion. | List triggers first. |
| No validation query | “Done” is subjective. | Measurable completion criteria. |
| Enforce NOT NULL before backfill complete | Migration fails or blocks. | Backfill then validate. |
| Backfill changes `updated_at` accidentally | Business timestamp polluted. | Decide and document. |
| No throttling | App latency/replication lag spike. | Batch size + sleep + pause. |
| No rollback/roll-forward plan | Bad data hard to recover. | Audit old values or corrective plan. |
| No DBA coordination | Vacuum/replication/storage surprise. | Operational review. |
| Camunda synchronous long backfill | Process timeout/retry risk. | Async operation record + job. |

---

## 44. Practical Example: `risk_score` Backfill

### Migration 1 - Add Column

```sql id="so799y"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score INTEGER;
```

### App Release

New app writes `risk_score` for new rows.

### Backfill SQL Batch

```sql id="3dz9yc"
WITH batch AS (
    SELECT id
    FROM case_app.enforcement_case
    WHERE risk_score IS NULL
    ORDER BY id
    LIMIT 1000
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
)
UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case c
SET risk_score = 0
FROM batch
WHERE c.id = batch.id
RETURNING c.id;
```

### Validation

```sql id="bdyztp"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.enforcement_case
WHERE risk_score IS NULL;
```

### Constraint

```sql id="nopm4d"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_range
CHECK (risk_score >= 0 AND risk_score <= 100)
NOT VALID;

ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_risk_score_range;
```

### Later

```sql id="s1qhge"
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ALTER COLUMN risk_score SET NOT NULL;
```

---

## 45. Decision Tree

```text id="4clcz4"
Is the data change small and fast?
  ├─ Yes → versioned migration SQL may be okay
  └─ No
      │
      ├─ Is it millions of rows / hot table?
      │   └─ Use chunked resumable job
      │
      ├─ Can it be retried safely?
      │   ├─ Yes → idempotent batch job
      │   └─ No → add operation id / audit old values
      │
      ├─ Does trigger fire?
      │   ├─ Yes → review trigger/backfill interaction
      │   └─ No → proceed with normal plan
      │
      ├─ Does app need mixed-state compatibility?
      │   ├─ Yes → dual-read/fallback/feature flag
      │   └─ No → prove why
      │
      └─ Is constraint needed?
          └─ validate data first, then add/validate constraint
```

---

## 46. Production Review Checklist

| Area | Required |
|---|---|
| Scope | Target table, columns, estimated row count. |
| Ownership | Domain owner and DB owner. |
| Execution model | Migration SQL vs Kubernetes Job vs worker. |
| Idempotency | Safe retry proven. |
| Batch size | Initial value and tuning plan. |
| Timeout | `lock_timeout`, `statement_timeout`, job timeout. |
| Trigger impact | Listed and reviewed. |
| MyBatis/app compatibility | Mixed state supported. |
| Camunda compatibility | Retry/process impact reviewed. |
| Progress | Persistent progress/heartbeat. |
| Pause/resume | Operational control. |
| Validation | SQL queries with expected results. |
| Constraint follow-up | Planned after validation. |
| Roll-forward | Corrective path defined. |
| Observability | DB metrics, logs, replication lag, app latency. |
| Post-maintenance | ANALYZE/VACUUM coordination if needed. |

---

## 47. Ringkasan Seri 15

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| Backfill besar | Operational data movement, bukan sekadar SQL migration. |
| Migration SQL | Hanya cocok untuk data kecil dan cepat. |
| Chunking | Gunakan batch kecil, commit per batch. |
| `SKIP LOCKED` | Berguna untuk menghindari lock contention dan multiple workers. |
| Progress table | Wajib untuk job besar/resumable. |
| Idempotency | Retry harus aman. |
| Trigger impact | Harus dicek sebelum backfill. |
| Timeouts | Gunakan `lock_timeout` dan `statement_timeout`. |
| WAL/replication | Monitor dan throttle. |
| Autovacuum/bloat | Perhatikan dead tuples setelah update besar. |
| MyBatis/Jersey | App harus kompatibel dengan mixed state. |
| Camunda | Jangan jadikan long backfill synchronous task. |
| Constraint | Backfill dulu, validasi, baru enforce. |
| Recovery | Roll-forward/corrective repair lebih realistis dari rollback besar. |
| Runbook | Harus ada sebelum production execution. |

# Seri 16 - CI/CD and Kubernetes Execution Model

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana menjalankan **database migration** di pipeline CI/CD dan Kubernetes secara aman untuk stack:

```text id="q4vrq1"
Java 17+
Maven
GitHub Actions
Jersey / JAX-RS
MyBatis
PostgreSQL
Flyway / Liquibase
Docker
Kubernetes
On-Prem / AWS / Azure
```

Fokus utamanya:

```text id="ydk1rs"
Database migration harus berjalan sebagai one-time controlled operation,
bukan otomatis dari setiap application pod startup.
```

Kubernetes Job memang dirancang untuk menjalankan task sampai sejumlah completion berhasil; saat completion target tercapai, Job dianggap selesai. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/job/))

---

## 2. Mental Model

```text id="xifacl"
CI/CD + Kubernetes Migration Mental Model
────────────────────────────────────────────

Developer PR
  │
  ├─ migration file
  ├─ MyBatis mapper change
  ├─ Jersey API change
  └─ tests
       │
       ▼
CI Validation
  │
  ├─ migration naming check
  ├─ Flyway/Liquibase validate
  ├─ ephemeral PostgreSQL migrate
  ├─ MyBatis integration test
  ├─ API integration test
  └─ dangerous DDL detection
       │
       ▼
Release Pipeline
  │
  ├─ build app image
  ├─ build migration image / job
  ├─ run DB migration once
  ├─ verify migration state
  ├─ deploy app
  └─ smoke test
       │
       ▼
Kubernetes Runtime
  │
  ├─ application Deployment
  ├─ one-time migration Job
  ├─ optional backfill Job
  ├─ optional refresh CronJob
  └─ separated runtime/migration DB roles
```

---

## 3. Core Principle

```text id="cs2oez"
Migration execution is release orchestration,
not application runtime initialization.
```

Artinya:

| Salah | Benar |
|---|---|
| Setiap pod menjalankan Flyway/Liquibase saat startup | Pipeline/Kubernetes Job menjalankan migration sekali |
| Runtime DB user punya `ALTER`, `DROP`, `CREATE` | Runtime DB user hanya punya akses operasional minimum |
| Migration failure membuat semua pod restart loop | Migration failure stop rollout sebelum app deploy |
| Migration logic tersembunyi di app bootstrap | Migration punya log, artifact, approval, dan runbook |
| DB state tidak diverifikasi sebelum deploy | `info/status/validate` dicek sebelum app naik |

---

## 4. Kenapa Jangan Migration di App Startup?

Beberapa framework mendorong model “run migration on startup”. Untuk production microservice di Kubernetes, ini sering berbahaya.

```text id="rhgznt"
Deployment replicas = 5

Pod 1 starts → runs migration
Pod 2 starts → runs migration
Pod 3 starts → waits/locks/errors
Pod 4 starts → waits/locks/errors
Pod 5 starts → waits/locks/errors
```

Walaupun Flyway/Liquibase punya locking mechanism, startup migration tetap buruk karena:

| Risiko | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Multi-pod race | Banyak pod mencoba migration bersamaan. |
| Startup delay | Pod readiness tertunda oleh DDL. |
| Privilege leak | Runtime app butuh credential migration. |
| Failure blast radius | Migration gagal → app rollout gagal/restart loop. |
| Observability buruk | Migration log tercampur app startup log. |
| Rollback sulit | App rollback tidak selalu rollback DB. |
| Operational coupling | Scaling pod bisa tidak sengaja menyentuh DB migration path. |

---

## 5. Recommended Execution Model

```text id="wkpc3d"
Recommended Production Order
────────────────────────────────

1. Build and test artifact
2. Run migration validation
3. Deploy/run migration job once
4. Verify schema history / changelog state
5. Deploy application pods
6. Run smoke tests
7. Monitor app and DB
```

Untuk zero-downtime migration, order bisa multi-release:

```text id="k4w8zm"
Release A:
  DB expand migration
  app compatible with old/new schema

Release B:
  backfill job

Release C:
  constraint validation

Release D:
  app switch

Release E:
  contract migration
```

---

## 6. CI Pipeline: Pull Request Stage

PR stage harus membuktikan migration bisa jalan dari fresh DB.

```text id="xrx0d1"
PR Checks
────────────────────────────────

1. Compile Java
2. Run unit tests
3. Validate migration naming
4. Start ephemeral PostgreSQL
5. Run Flyway/Liquibase validation
6. Apply migrations
7. Run MyBatis integration tests
8. Run Jersey/API integration tests
9. Detect high-risk DDL
10. Publish migration report
```

### Why Ephemeral PostgreSQL?

Karena H2/mock DB tidak cukup untuk PostgreSQL-specific behavior:

```text id="h9a5r5"
- PL/pgSQL
- CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY
- materialized view
- trigger
- event trigger
- jsonb
- array
- generated column
- PostgreSQL lock behavior
```

---

## 7. GitHub Actions Example - Flyway

```yaml id="11rvr6"
name: pr-check

on:
  pull_request:

jobs:
  migration-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:17
        env:
          POSTGRES_DB: case_app
          POSTGRES_USER: case_app_migration
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: case_app_migration_password
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U case_app_migration -d case_app"
          --health-interval 5s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 20

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "17"
          cache: maven

      - name: Validate migration naming
        run: ./scripts/check-migration-naming.sh

      - name: Detect dangerous DDL
        run: ./scripts/check-dangerous-ddl.sh src/main/resources/db

      - name: Flyway validate
        run: >
          mvn -B flyway:validate
          -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
          -Dflyway.user=case_app_migration
          -Dflyway.password=case_app_migration_password

      - name: Flyway migrate
        run: >
          mvn -B flyway:migrate
          -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
          -Dflyway.user=case_app_migration
          -Dflyway.password=case_app_migration_password

      - name: Run integration tests
        run: mvn -B verify
```

Flyway Maven plugin menyediakan goal seperti `migrate`, `info`, `validate`, `baseline`, `repair`, dan `clean`; `validate` memvalidasi migration yang sudah applied terhadap migration yang tersedia, sedangkan `migrate` menjalankan migration database. ([documentation.red-gate.com](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/maven-goal-277579365.html))

---

## 8. GitHub Actions Example - Liquibase

```yaml id="evbyw4"
name: pr-check-liquibase

on:
  pull_request:

jobs:
  liquibase-migration-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:17
        env:
          POSTGRES_DB: case_app
          POSTGRES_USER: case_app_migration
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: case_app_migration_password
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U case_app_migration -d case_app"
          --health-interval 5s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 20

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "17"
          cache: maven

      - name: Liquibase validate
        run: >
          mvn -B liquibase:validate
          -Dliquibase.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
          -Dliquibase.username=case_app_migration
          -Dliquibase.password=case_app_migration_password

      - name: Liquibase updateSQL
        run: >
          mvn -B liquibase:updateSQL
          -Dliquibase.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
          -Dliquibase.username=case_app_migration
          -Dliquibase.password=case_app_migration_password

      - name: Liquibase update
        run: >
          mvn -B liquibase:update
          -Dliquibase.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
          -Dliquibase.username=case_app_migration
          -Dliquibase.password=case_app_migration_password

      - name: Run integration tests
        run: mvn -B verify
```

Tambahkan artifact upload untuk `updateSQL` supaya reviewer bisa melihat SQL yang akan dijalankan.

---

## 9. Dangerous DDL Gate

Minimal detector:

```bash id="1od4ux"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="${1:-src/main/resources/db}"

PATTERN="DROP TABLE|DROP COLUMN|TRUNCATE|CASCADE|ALTER COLUMN .* TYPE|CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY|ALTER TABLE .* SET NOT NULL|DISABLE TRIGGER|DROP TRIGGER|DROP FUNCTION|DROP PROCEDURE|DROP MATERIALIZED VIEW"

if grep -RniE "$PATTERN" "$ROOT"; then
  echo "High-risk database change detected."
  echo "Require DB migration review."
  exit 1
fi

echo "No high-risk DDL pattern detected."
```

Ini bukan pengganti review manusia. Tujuannya adalah memaksa PR high-risk mendapatkan perhatian.

---

## 10. Migration Report Artifact

Setiap PR yang menyentuh DB migration sebaiknya menghasilkan report:

```md id="b8s3z8"
# Migration PR Report

## Changed Files
- V001201__case_add_risk_score.sql
- R__020_case_view_case_summary.sql
- CaseMapper.xml

## Tool
Flyway

## Validation
- flyway validate: PASS
- flyway migrate fresh DB: PASS
- MyBatis integration test: PASS
- API integration test: PASS

## Risk Detected
- Add nullable column: low
- View changed: medium
- Mapper changed: medium

## Required Reviewers
- case-domain-owner
- db-reviewer

## Notes
No destructive DDL.
```

Manfaat:

- reviewer cepat paham;
- audit trail lebih bagus;
- release manager punya evidence.

---

## 11. Build Artifact Strategy

Ada dua model.

## Option A - App Image Contains Migration Files

```text id="v92nqp"
case-service image
  ├─ app jar
  └─ db/migration files
```

Migration Job memakai image yang sama, tetapi command berbeda:

```text id="x96rhk"
java -jar app.jar migrate
```

Kelebihan:

- app code dan migration files always same version;
- artifact lebih sederhana.

Kekurangan:

- app image berisi migration tooling;
- harus hati-hati agar app normal tidak menjalankan migration.

---

## Option B - Separate Migration Image

```text id="2lmxpi"
case-service-app:2026.07.13
case-service-migration:2026.07.13
```

Migration image berisi:

```text id="dxlmx8"
- migration files
- Flyway/Liquibase CLI/Maven wrapper
- PostgreSQL driver
- scripts
```

Kelebihan:

- separation of concern;
- app image lebih kecil/bersih;
- command migration eksplisit.

Kekurangan:

- perlu build/publish dua artifact;
- harus memastikan versi app dan migration match.

### Recommendation

Untuk organisasi mature:

```text id="7iy210"
Separate migration image is cleaner.
Same git commit / same release version ties app and migration together.
```

---

## 12. Maven Packaging Pattern

Pastikan migration files ada di artifact:

```text id="zg0g8a"
src/main/resources/db/migration
src/main/resources/db/changelog
```

Maven akan memasukkan resources ke classpath.

Untuk migration image, bisa memakai:

```text id="xgzkdq"
target/case-service.jar
```

dan jalankan mode migration:

```bash id="znmk5k"
java -cp case-service.jar org.flywaydb.commandline.Main migrate
```

Atau gunakan Maven plugin/CLI di image.

---

## 13. Kubernetes Job for Migration

Basic Job:

```yaml id="87lsfj"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: Job
metadata:
  name: case-service-db-migration-20260713-001
  labels:
    app: case-service
    component: db-migration
    release: "20260713-001"
spec:
  backoffLimit: 0
  ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 86400
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: case-service
        component: db-migration
    spec:
      restartPolicy: Never
      containers:
        - name: migrate
          image: registry.example.com/case-service-migration:20260713-001
          envFrom:
            - secretRef:
                name: case-service-db-migration
          command:
            - /bin/sh
            - -c
          args:
            - |
              ./migrate.sh
```

Kubernetes Job dapat dibersihkan otomatis setelah selesai memakai `.spec.ttlSecondsAfterFinished`; TTL controller berlaku untuk Job yang selesai, baik `Complete` maupun `Failed`. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/ttlafterfinished/))

---

## 14. Migration Job Naming

Jangan pakai nama statis:

```text id="10np7r"
case-service-db-migration
```

Karena Job immutable/riwayat bisa bentrok.

Gunakan release-specific name:

```text id="p1d4ib"
case-service-db-migration-20260713-001
case-service-db-migration-1-42-0
case-service-db-migration-gitsha-a1b2c3d
```

### Rule

```text id="ihalky"
One release = one migration job identity.
```

---

## 15. Job Retry Policy

Untuk migration schema:

```yaml id="m10wx7"
backoffLimit: 0
restartPolicy: Never
```

Kenapa?

```text id="8kgmze"
Schema migration failure should be investigated,
not blindly retried many times.
```

Untuk backfill idempotent:

```yaml id="ske15h"
backoffLimit: 2
restartPolicy: Never
```

Karena backfill idempotent bisa retry terbatas.

---

## 16. Init Container: Kenapa Biasanya Tidak Cocok untuk Migration?

Kubernetes init containers adalah container khusus yang berjalan sampai selesai sebelum app containers dalam Pod dimulai. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/init-containers/))

Init container tampak menggoda:

```text id="yipp1g"
Pod starts
  → init container runs migration
  → app container starts
```

Tetapi untuk database migration production:

| Masalah | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Runs per pod | Setiap pod punya init container. |
| Scaling triggers migration path | Scale up bisa memicu migration attempt. |
| Privilege coupling | Pod app membawa secret migration. |
| Failure blocks pod | App readiness tergantung migration. |
| Harder orchestration | Tidak jelas migration selesai sebelum Deployment rollout keseluruhan. |

### Recommendation

```text id="aig1xj"
Use Kubernetes Job for migration.
Use init container only for local/dev or non-DB setup tasks.
```

---

## 17. Helm Hook Pattern

Jika deploy memakai Helm, migration Job sering dijalankan sebagai hook:

```yaml id="ep7vvk"
apiVersion: batch/v1
kind: Job
metadata:
  name: "{{ .Release.Name }}-db-migration-{{ .Chart.AppVersion }}"
  annotations:
    "helm.sh/hook": pre-install,pre-upgrade
    "helm.sh/hook-weight": "-5"
    "helm.sh/hook-delete-policy": before-hook-creation,hook-succeeded
spec:
  backoffLimit: 0
  template:
    spec:
      restartPolicy: Never
      containers:
        - name: migrate
          image: "{{ .Values.migration.image }}"
          envFrom:
            - secretRef:
                name: "{{ .Values.migration.secretName }}"
```

### Pros

- migration runs before app upgrade;
- integrated with Helm release.

### Cons

- failed hook can complicate Helm release state;
- rollback Helm chart does not automatically rollback DB safely;
- long-running migration can exceed deploy expectations;
- hook logs/artifacts must be captured separately.

### Rule

```text id="pwsd4v"
Helm hook is orchestration glue, not rollback strategy.
```

---

## 18. Secret Management

Kubernetes Secret adalah object untuk menyimpan sejumlah kecil data sensitif seperti password, token, atau key, supaya data ini tidak perlu ditaruh langsung di Pod spec atau container image. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/))

Pisahkan secret:

```text id="t54j9h"
case-service-db-runtime
case-service-db-migration
case-service-db-readonly
case-service-db-refresh
```

Jangan satu credential untuk semua.

---

## 19. DB Role Separation

Recommended PostgreSQL roles:

```text id="4ze06r"
case_app_runtime
  SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE needed by app
  EXECUTE selected functions/procedures
  no DDL

case_app_migration
  CREATE/ALTER/DROP within app schema
  migrate schema
  create indexes/constraints/views/functions
  no ordinary app usage

case_app_readonly
  SELECT only on approved views/tables

case_app_refresh
  refresh materialized views / execute refresh procedure

case_app_owner
  owns schema objects, not used by app runtime
```

### Why?

| Reason | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Least privilege | App exploit tidak otomatis punya DDL. |
| Audit clarity | Migration action terlihat dari role. |
| Blast radius | Runtime bug tidak bisa drop table. |
| Governance | DDL hanya dari pipeline/job. |

---

## 20. Secret Injection

Example:

```yaml id="vs6yid"
env:
  - name: DB_HOST
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-db-migration
        key: host
  - name: DB_PORT
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-db-migration
        key: port
  - name: DB_NAME
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-db-migration
        key: database
  - name: DB_USER
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-db-migration
        key: username
  - name: DB_PASSWORD
    valueFrom:
      secretKeyRef:
        name: case-service-db-migration
        key: password
```

Kubernetes docs also provide task-level guidance for securely injecting credentials into Pods through Secrets. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/inject-data-application/distribute-credentials-secure/))

### Rule

```text id="6bfnw1"
Never bake database credentials into Docker image.
Never commit them into GitHub repository.
```

---

## 21. Flyway Migration Script

```bash id="iu6zcd"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

: "${DB_HOST:?DB_HOST is required}"
: "${DB_PORT:?DB_PORT is required}"
: "${DB_NAME:?DB_NAME is required}"
: "${DB_USER:?DB_USER is required}"
: "${DB_PASSWORD:?DB_PASSWORD is required}"

export FLYWAY_URL="jdbc:postgresql://${DB_HOST}:${DB_PORT}/${DB_NAME}"
export FLYWAY_USER="${DB_USER}"
export FLYWAY_PASSWORD="${DB_PASSWORD}"

echo "Running Flyway info..."
flyway info

echo "Running Flyway validate..."
flyway validate

echo "Running Flyway migrate..."
flyway migrate

echo "Running Flyway info after migrate..."
flyway info
```

Tambahkan:

```text id="tqmyc2"
- connection timeout
- lock retry policy sesuai tool
- output log capture
- application_name in JDBC URL
```

---

## 22. Liquibase Migration Script

```bash id="9n8rqy"
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

: "${DB_HOST:?DB_HOST is required}"
: "${DB_PORT:?DB_PORT is required}"
: "${DB_NAME:?DB_NAME is required}"
: "${DB_USER:?DB_USER is required}"
: "${DB_PASSWORD:?DB_PASSWORD is required}"

JDBC_URL="jdbc:postgresql://${DB_HOST}:${DB_PORT}/${DB_NAME}"

echo "Running Liquibase validate..."
liquibase \
  --url="${JDBC_URL}" \
  --username="${DB_USER}" \
  --password="${DB_PASSWORD}" \
  --changeLogFile=db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml \
  validate

echo "Generating SQL preview..."
liquibase \
  --url="${JDBC_URL}" \
  --username="${DB_USER}" \
  --password="${DB_PASSWORD}" \
  --changeLogFile=db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml \
  updateSQL

echo "Running Liquibase update..."
liquibase \
  --url="${JDBC_URL}" \
  --username="${DB_USER}" \
  --password="${DB_PASSWORD}" \
  --changeLogFile=db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml \
  update
```

---

## 23. Database Migration Locking

Flyway and Liquibase both maintain metadata/lock mechanisms, but do not use that as excuse to run migrations from many app pods.

Recommended policy:

```text id="f4psja"
Tool lock:
  protects metadata consistency

Deployment orchestration:
  ensures migration runs once intentionally
```

### Practical Gate

Before app deployment:

```text id="8187n4"
migration job status = Complete
and migration info/status = expected version
and smoke query passes
```

---

## 24. Deployment Choreography

## Pattern A - Backward-Compatible Migration First

```text id="y7xu2d"
1. Run migration job
2. Verify migration
3. Deploy app
4. Smoke test
```

Examples:

- add nullable column;
- add table;
- add index;
- add function/view backward-compatible;
- add seed required by new app.

---

## Pattern B - App First, Migration Later

Rare. Only if app can tolerate both schemas.

```text id="3xx4ql"
1. Deploy app with compatibility fallback
2. Run migration
3. Enable feature flag
```

Harder with MyBatis because SQL referencing missing column fails if executed.

---

## Pattern C - Expand / Backfill / Contract

```text id="4nqqog"
Release 1:
  migration expand
  app compatible

Release 2:
  backfill job

Release 3:
  constraint validation

Release 4:
  app switch

Release 5:
  contract migration
```

Use for:

- rename column;
- required column;
- type change;
- split/merge fields;
- status taxonomy change;
- materialized view replacement.

---

## 25. Kubernetes Deployment Dependency

Avoid making the app Deployment directly depend on migration Job through fragile sleep scripts.

Bad:

```bash id="cmx2kk"
sleep 60
kubectl apply -f deployment.yaml
```

Better:

```text id="aawfg8"
CI/CD orchestrator:
  kubectl apply migration job
  kubectl wait --for=condition=complete job/...
  verify migration status
  kubectl apply deployment
```

Example:

```bash id="9u505k"
kubectl apply -f k8s/migration-job.yaml

kubectl wait \
  --for=condition=complete \
  --timeout=10m \
  job/case-service-db-migration-20260713-001

kubectl apply -f k8s/deployment.yaml
```

If job fails:

```bash id="bw37ji"
kubectl logs job/case-service-db-migration-20260713-001
kubectl describe job/case-service-db-migration-20260713-001
exit 1
```

---

## 26. Kubernetes Job Completion and Cleanup

For completed Jobs, set TTL:

```yaml id="4mno4c"
spec:
  ttlSecondsAfterFinished: 86400
```

Kubernetes TTL-after-finished controller can delete completed or failed Jobs automatically after TTL expires. ([kubernetes.io](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/controllers/ttlafterfinished/))

### Practical Recommendation

| Environment | TTL |
|---|---:|
| dev | 1-6 hours |
| staging | 1-3 days |
| production | 7-30 days or log externalized |

For production, ensure logs are shipped before TTL cleanup.

---

## 27. Readiness and Migration

Application readiness should not run migration. It can check schema compatibility lightly.

Example readiness check:

```text id="j1itdh"
- database connection works
- required table/view exists
- app_migration_min_version <= DB version
```

Avoid:

```text id="a9552l"
readiness endpoint runs DDL
readiness endpoint runs Flyway repair
readiness endpoint does heavy schema validation
```

### Schema Version Check Table

Optional app-level compatibility table:

```sql id="frgmxj"
CREATE TABLE case_app.app_schema_compatibility (
    app_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    min_supported_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    current_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

But avoid duplicating Flyway/Liquibase metadata unless you have clear need.

---

## 28. Smoke Test After Migration

After migration job completes:

```sql id="d08cyw"
SELECT 1;
```

Not enough.

Better:

```sql id="q5v91p"
SELECT COUNT(*)
FROM case_app.case_status_reference;
```

For object-specific migration:

```sql id="gwsvl8"
SELECT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM information_schema.columns
    WHERE table_schema = 'case_app'
      AND table_name = 'enforcement_case'
      AND column_name = 'risk_score'
);
```

For view:

```sql id="256j2d"
SELECT *
FROM case_app.v_case_summary
LIMIT 1;
```

For function:

```sql id="vgoz3e"
SELECT case_app.fn_normalize_code(' open ');
```

For materialized view readiness:

```sql id="4jsqmr"
SELECT *
FROM case_app.read_model_refresh_state
WHERE read_model_name = 'mv_case_dashboard_metrics';
```

---

## 29. Production Approval Gates

Before running production migration:

```text id="8ur2s0"
- PR approved
- migration report reviewed
- staging migration successful
- rollback/roll-forward plan documented
- backup/restore point confirmed
- DB owner approval for high-risk DDL
- security owner approval for permission/SECURITY DEFINER changes
- operations approval for long migration/backfill
- deployment window confirmed if needed
```

---

## 30. Rollback vs Roll-Forward in Pipeline

Do not assume app rollback = database rollback.

```text id="h2mcah"
App rollback:
  deploy previous image

DB rollback:
  may be impossible/destructive after migration
```

### Policy

| Change Type | Recovery |
|---|---|
| Add nullable column | Usually roll-forward or ignore. |
| Add index | Drop index if needed. |
| Add table unused | Ignore/drop later. |
| Add seed | Deactivate or corrective seed. |
| Add constraint | Drop constraint if needed. |
| Rename/drop column | Roll-forward correction preferred; rollback hard. |
| Large backfill | Corrective repair job. |
| Function body bug | Replace function with corrected version. |
| View bug | Replace view definition. |

### Pipeline Rule

```text id="t4ert0"
For production DB changes, prefer roll-forward plan
unless rollback has been explicitly tested.
```

---

## 31. Flyway `repair` / Liquibase Recovery Warning

Tool recovery commands are not normal deployment steps.

Flyway `repair` repairs metadata table state, but using it without root-cause analysis can hide serious divergence. Liquibase checksum clearing or manual changelog table edits are similarly dangerous.

Policy:

```text id="k72uzo"
repair / clearCheckSums / manual metadata change:
  - incident path only
  - DB owner approval
  - evidence captured
  - root cause documented
```

---

## 32. Migration User vs Runtime User

Deployment should prove app cannot perform DDL.

### Runtime Smoke

Connect as runtime role and verify DDL fails:

```sql id="6y3bjo"
CREATE TABLE case_app.runtime_should_not_create_table (
    id BIGINT
);
```

Expected:

```text id="ak2px0"
permission denied
```

This is a useful security test in non-prod.

---

## 33. Network and Secret Boundary

Migration Job needs DB network access and migration secret.

App Deployment needs runtime DB network access and runtime secret.

```text id="r9t0id"
migration namespace/service account:
  can read migration secret
  can connect to DB migration endpoint

app service account:
  can read runtime secret
  cannot read migration secret
```

Use Kubernetes RBAC and secret scoping.

---

## 34. Service Account Separation

```yaml id="7pld9w"
apiVersion: v1
kind: ServiceAccount
metadata:
  name: case-service-migration
```

```yaml id="5zj02t"
apiVersion: v1
kind: ServiceAccount
metadata:
  name: case-service-runtime
```

Migration Job:

```yaml id="pj4yxe"
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: case-service-migration
```

Deployment:

```yaml id="b06910"
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: case-service-runtime
```

### Rule

```text id="ytms7i"
Kubernetes service account separation should mirror database role separation.
```

---

## 35. Config by Environment

Do not use same config for all envs.

```text id="ktw9ni"
dev:
  lower timeout
  optional clean allowed
  sample data allowed

test:
  fresh DB
  test fixture allowed

staging:
  prod-like migration
  prod-like data volume if possible
  no test fixture

production:
  no clean
  no test/demo seed
  strict approval
  backup/restore point
```

### Flyway Example

```properties id="6o3h0e"
# prod
flyway.cleanDisabled=true
flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable
```

Never allow `clean` in production.

---

## 36. Test Data and Seed Isolation

Production migration path:

```text id="tn0evx"
src/main/resources/db/migration
```

Test fixture path:

```text id="hb42xf"
src/test/resources/db/seed
```

Demo data path:

```text id="tma7w5"
src/demo/resources/db/seed
```

CI should block:

```text id="ru796o"
DEMO-
TEST-
dummy
sample
fake
```

from production migration folders.

---

## 37. Handling Concurrent Deployments

Problem:

```text id="s0qw4z"
Two release pipelines run at same time.
Both attempt migration.
```

Mitigations:

| Layer | Mechanism |
|---|---|
| CI/CD | environment lock / concurrency group |
| Kubernetes | unique Job per release |
| DB tool | Flyway/Liquibase metadata lock |
| DB | advisory lock if custom scripts |
| Release process | only one prod deploy per service at a time |

### GitHub Actions Concurrency

```yaml id="y3n0u4"
concurrency:
  group: production-case-service
  cancel-in-progress: false
```

---

## 38. Advisory Lock for Custom Migration Script

If you have extra SQL scripts outside Flyway/Liquibase:

```sql id="qn33c4"
SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext('case_service_migration'));
```

If false:

```text id="d6bgap"
another migration is already running
```

At end:

```sql id="1y77x8"
SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext('case_service_migration'));
```

Use this only as additional protection, not replacement for tool history.

---

## 39. Blue/Green and Canary Deployment

### DB Migration Constraint

Blue/green and canary require both versions compatible with one DB.

```text id="4cs2f4"
blue app old version
green app new version
same PostgreSQL database
```

Therefore:

```text id="ib0zo2"
No destructive DB migration before traffic fully shifts
and old version is no longer needed.
```

Use expand/contract:

```text id="8z22nu"
expand DB
deploy green
shift traffic
observe
remove blue
contract DB later
```

---

## 40. Multi-Tenant / Multi-Schema Migration

If each tenant has separate schema:

```text id="md3d8p"
tenant_001
tenant_002
tenant_003
```

Migration strategy must answer:

```text id="6jtwt3"
- migrate all tenants in one job?
- migrate tenant by tenant?
- what happens if tenant 57 fails?
- can app serve mixed tenant schema versions?
- how to resume?
- how to report progress?
```

Recommended for many tenants:

```text id="2nm8lr"
tenant migration progress table
per-tenant transaction
resume failed tenants
compatibility checks in app
```

Avoid one giant transaction across all tenants.

---

## 41. Migration Observability

Capture:

```text id="61luod"
- release version
- git SHA
- migration job name
- migration tool version
- database name
- user/role
- start/end time
- status
- applied migrations
- error message
- duration
```

Example audit table:

```sql id="qe10ol"
CREATE TABLE case_app.deployment_migration_audit (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    release_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    git_sha TEXT NOT NULL,
    migration_job_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    tool_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    tool_version TEXT,
    db_user TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT current_user,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT
);
```

---

## 42. Logs

Migration logs should be accessible after Job TTL cleanup.

Recommended:

```text id="l7x9rf"
- ship pod logs to centralized logging
- attach migration report to release
- store updateSQL / dry-run output for Liquibase
- store flyway info output
- store failed SQL/error stack
```

Do not rely only on Kubernetes Job pod logs if TTL deletes job quickly.

---

## 43. Production Migration Runbook

```md id="q601ey"
# Production Migration Runbook

## Service
case-service

## Release
2026.07.13-001

## Pre-Checks
- PR approved
- staging migration successful
- backup/restore point confirmed
- migration SQL reviewed
- high-risk DDL approved
- runtime and migration secrets verified

## Execution
1. Apply migration Job
2. Wait for completion
3. Check migration logs
4. Run schema smoke checks
5. Deploy app
6. Run API smoke tests
7. Monitor DB/app

## Failure Before App Deploy
- Do not deploy app
- Inspect migration job logs
- Run flyway/liquibase info/status
- Decide fix-forward or approved recovery

## Failure After App Deploy
- Determine app/schema compatibility
- Roll app back only if DB remains compatible
- Otherwise deploy fix-forward app or DB migration

## Evidence
- Migration job log
- Migration report
- Smoke test result
- Approval links
```

---

## 44. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Why Dangerous | Better |
|---|---|---|
| App pod startup runs migration | Multi-pod race and privilege leak. | One-time migration Job. |
| Runtime user has DDL | App exploit can alter schema. | Separate runtime/migration roles. |
| No ephemeral PostgreSQL in CI | Migration bugs found in staging/prod. | Run real PostgreSQL in PR. |
| H2-only tests | Misses PostgreSQL behavior. | PostgreSQL integration tests. |
| Helm rollback assumed to rollback DB | DB rollback not automatic/safe. | Roll-forward plan. |
| Long backfill in schema migration | Deploy timeout and lock risk. | Separate backfill Job. |
| Same secret for app and migration | Privilege leakage. | Separate secrets. |
| Migration Job name static | Job collision/immutability issue. | Release-specific Job name. |
| No log retention | Lost evidence after TTL cleanup. | Centralized logs/artifacts. |
| `clean` enabled in prod | Catastrophic data loss risk. | Disable clean. |
| Multiple prod deploys concurrently | Migration race. | CI concurrency lock. |
| No high-risk DDL gate | Destructive change slips in. | DDL detector + reviewer. |

---

## 45. Practical Recommendation for Your Stack

Untuk stack **Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + PostgreSQL + Kubernetes**, saya rekomendasikan standard ini:

```text id="l40lvr"
CI:
  - real PostgreSQL service container
  - Flyway/Liquibase validate
  - migrate fresh DB
  - MyBatis integration tests
  - Jersey/API integration tests
  - dangerous DDL gate

Artifact:
  - app image
  - migration image, or same image with explicit migrate command
  - same release version/git SHA

CD:
  - run Kubernetes Job once
  - wait for Job complete
  - verify migration state
  - deploy app
  - smoke test

Security:
  - migration DB role != runtime DB role
  - migration Kubernetes secret != runtime secret
  - migration service account != runtime service account

Operations:
  - no app startup migration
  - no large backfill in schema migration
  - no production clean
  - roll-forward-first recovery
```

---

## 46. Reference Deployment Flow

```text id="a74r86"
Pull Request
  └─ CI validates migration on PostgreSQL

Merge to main
  └─ build app + migration images

Deploy staging
  ├─ run migration job
  ├─ verify schema
  ├─ deploy app
  └─ smoke test

Approval
  └─ review migration report and staging result

Deploy production
  ├─ acquire deployment lock
  ├─ confirm backup/restore point
  ├─ run migration job
  ├─ wait complete
  ├─ verify schema/tool status
  ├─ deploy app
  ├─ smoke test
  └─ monitor
```

---

## 47. Production Checklist

| Area | Required |
|---|---|
| PR validation | Migration applied to fresh PostgreSQL. |
| Migration tool | `validate` and `migrate/update` tested. |
| App tests | MyBatis and API integration tests pass. |
| DDL risk | Dangerous DDL gate passed or approved. |
| Secrets | Migration/runtime credentials separated. |
| Kubernetes | Migration runs as Job, not app startup. |
| Job | Release-specific name, `backoffLimit` intentional. |
| Logs | Shipped and retained. |
| Backup | Restore point confirmed for high-risk release. |
| Roll-forward | Recovery plan documented. |
| Concurrency | Only one prod deployment per service. |
| Backfill | Separate job if large. |
| Contract | Expand/contract sequence if breaking change. |
| Smoke | Schema and API smoke checks after migration. |

---

## 48. Ringkasan Seri 16

| Area | Kesimpulan |
|---|---|
| Execution model | Migration harus one-time controlled operation. |
| Kubernetes | Gunakan Job, bukan app startup/init container untuk production DB migration. |
| CI | Jalankan migration pada PostgreSQL asli, bukan H2/mock. |
| Flyway/Liquibase | Gunakan validate + migrate/update + status/info. |
| MyBatis | Mapper tests wajib setelah migration. |
| Jersey/API | API integration/smoke test setelah schema berubah. |
| Secrets | Pisahkan runtime dan migration secret. |
| DB roles | Runtime role tidak boleh punya DDL. |
| Helm hook | Bisa dipakai, tapi bukan rollback strategy. |
| Backfill | Large data movement harus Job terpisah. |
| Rollback | App rollback tidak sama dengan DB rollback. |
| Observability | Migration logs/report/status harus tersimpan. |
| Production | Gunakan approval gates, deployment lock, backup, dan roll-forward plan. |

**Seri 17 - Application Compatibility with Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis + Camunda**

Fokusnya: bagaimana perubahan schema memengaruhi mapper SQL, DTO/API contract, transaction boundary, Camunda running process, worker retry, deployment order, feature flag, dan compatibility testing untuk old app/new app selama rolling deployment.

## Response:
7/13/2026, 8:06:20 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 17 dari 18**: Application Compatibility. Fokusnya menjembatani DB migration dengan MyBatis mapper, Jersey API contract, rolling deployment, feature flag, dan Camunda running process.
