## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:42:57 PM

Saya akan buat cheat sheet Liquibase yang praktis untuk Java/Maven/Jersey/PostgreSQL, mencakup command, changelog syntax, changeset attributes, preconditions, contexts/labels, rollback, Java API classes, Maven config, dan properties penting.

Berikut **cheat sheet Liquibase** untuk konteks **Java 17+, Maven, PostgreSQL, MyBatis, Jersey/JAX-RS, Kubernetes**.

Catatan penting: Liquibase **bukan framework yang annotation-heavy** seperti JPA/CDI. “Annotation” di Liquibase terutama relevan kalau Anda membuat **custom Liquibase extension / custom Change Type** di Java. Untuk penggunaan normal, yang paling penting adalah **changelog syntax, changeset attributes, command, Maven config, properties, dan Java API classes**.

---

# 1. Core Mental Model

| Konsep | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `databaseChangeLog` | Root file yang berisi daftar perubahan DB. Bisa XML, YAML, JSON, atau formatted SQL. |
| `changeSet` | Unit perubahan DB. Diidentifikasi oleh kombinasi `id`, `author`, dan file path. |
| `Change Type` | Operasi DB seperti `createTable`, `addColumn`, `createIndex`, `sqlFile`, dll. |
| `DATABASECHANGELOG` | Table metadata untuk mencatat changeset yang sudah dieksekusi. |
| `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK` | Lock table agar hanya satu Liquibase instance berjalan pada satu waktu. |
| `preConditions` | Guard/check sebelum changeset dijalankan. |
| `contexts` | Filter eksekusi berbasis environment, misalnya `dev`, `test`, `prod`. |
| `labels` | Filter eksekusi berbasis kategori/fitur/release. |
| `rollback` | Instruksi membatalkan changeset, otomatis atau manual. |

Liquibase menggunakan changelog yang berisi changeset, dan changeset dapat dikontrol dengan preconditions, contexts, labels, serta atribut lain. Liquibase mencatat changeset yang sudah dijalankan di `DATABASECHANGELOG`; lock eksekusi dikelola lewat `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK`. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-a-changelog?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Maven Dependency & Plugin

## Maven Plugin

```xml
<plugin>
    <groupId>org.liquibase</groupId>
    <artifactId>liquibase-maven-plugin</artifactId>
    <version>${liquibase.version}</version>
    <configuration>
        <changeLogFile>src/main/resources/db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml</changeLogFile>
        <url>${db.url}</url>
        <username>${db.migration.username}</username>
        <password>${db.migration.password}</password>
        <driver>org.postgresql.Driver</driver>
        <defaultSchemaName>case_app</defaultSchemaName>
        <liquibaseSchemaName>case_migration</liquibaseSchemaName>
    </configuration>
    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
            <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
            <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</plugin>
```

## Runtime / Embedded Dependency

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.liquibase</groupId>
    <artifactId>liquibase-core</artifactId>
    <version>${liquibase.version}</version>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
    <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
    <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Untuk production Kubernetes, saya tetap rekomendasikan Liquibase dijalankan oleh **CI/CD atau Kubernetes Job**, bukan otomatis dari setiap Jersey app pod startup.

---

# 3. Command Cheat Sheet

## Common CLI Commands

| Command | Fungsi | Production Use |
|---|---|---|
| `liquibase update` | Apply pending changesets. | Ya |
| `liquibase update-sql` / `updateSQL` | Generate SQL preview tanpa apply. | Sangat disarankan untuk review |
| `liquibase validate` | Validasi changelog sebelum update. | Wajib |
| `liquibase status --verbose` | Lihat pending changesets. | Wajib sebelum deploy |
| `liquibase history` | Lihat riwayat changeset. | Useful |
| `liquibase tag` | Tag posisi database. | Useful sebelum release |
| `liquibase rollback --tag=...` | Rollback ke tag tertentu. | Hanya kalau tested |
| `liquibase rollback-sql` / `rollbackSQL` | Generate SQL rollback preview. | Disarankan sebelum rollback |
| `liquibase update-testing-rollback` | Apply lalu test rollback. | Non-prod only |
| `liquibase clear-checksums` | Nullify checksum agar dihitung ulang. | Incident path only |
| `liquibase release-locks` | Release lock stuck. | Incident path only |
| `liquibase changelog-sync` | Mark changesets as executed tanpa menjalankan. | Baseline/incident only |
| `liquibase diff` | Compare DB/reference DB. | Drift analysis |
| `liquibase diff-changelog` | Generate changelog dari diff. | Baseline/drift, review ketat |
| `liquibase generate-changelog` | Generate changelog dari existing DB. | Initial adoption only |

`update` menerapkan changeset yang belum ada di `DATABASECHANGELOG`; `validate` dipakai untuk mendeteksi masalah changelog sebelum update; rollback dapat menarget tag/date/count dan menghapus row terkait dari `DATABASECHANGELOG`; `clear-checksums` mengosongkan checksum agar dihitung ulang pada update berikutnya. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-0/init-update-and-rollback-commands/update?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Maven Goals

```bash
mvn liquibase:validate
mvn liquibase:status
mvn liquibase:updateSQL
mvn liquibase:update
mvn liquibase:rollback
mvn liquibase:rollbackSQL
mvn liquibase:tag
mvn liquibase:updateTestingRollback
mvn liquibase:clearCheckSums
mvn liquibase:releaseLocks
```

`updateTestingRollback` berguna untuk mengetes rollback di non-production, sedangkan `clearCheckSums` dan `releaseLocks` harus diperlakukan sebagai recovery/incident commands, bukan step deployment normal. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/oss/integration-guide-4-33/updatetestingrollback?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. `liquibase.properties` / Defaults File

Contoh praktis:

```properties
# Connection
url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
username=case_app_migration
password=${DB_PASSWORD}
driver=org.postgresql.Driver

# Changelog
changeLogFile=src/main/resources/db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml
searchPath=src/main/resources

# Schema
defaultSchemaName=case_app
liquibaseSchemaName=case_migration

# Runtime filters
contextFilter=dev
labelFilter=case-service

# Logging
logLevel=info

# Safety
databaseChangelogTableName=DATABASECHANGELOG
databaseChangelogLockTableName=DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK
```

Liquibase mendukung defaults file bernama `liquibase.properties` untuk menyimpan URL, username, password, dan setting lain; command-line arguments override nilai di file properties. Untuk Liquibase 4.24+ gunakan `--context-filter`, sementara versi lama menggunakan `--contexts`. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-the-liquibase-properties-file?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Changelog Structure

## Recommended Project Structure

```text
src/main/resources/db/changelog/
  db.changelog-master.yaml

  000-bootstrap/
    001-schema.yaml

  100-tables/
    101-enforcement-case.yaml

  200-indexes/
    201-enforcement-case-indexes.yaml

  300-constraints/
    301-case-status-fk.yaml

  400-seed/
    401-case-status-reference.yaml

  500-views/
    501-v-case-summary.yaml
    sql/
      v_case_summary.sql

  600-materialized-views/
    601-mv-case-dashboard.yaml

  700-functions/
    701-fn-normalize-code.yaml
    sql/
      fn_normalize_code.sql

  800-procedures/
    801-proc-refresh-metrics.yaml
    sql/
      proc_refresh_metrics.sql

  900-triggers/
    901-trg-fn-set-updated-at.yaml
    902-create-updated-at-trigger.yaml

  990-governance/
    991-event-trigger-audit.yaml
```

## Master Changelog

```yaml
databaseChangeLog:
  - property:
      name: app.schema
      value: case_app

  - property:
      name: migration.schema
      value: case_migration

  - include:
      file: db/changelog/000-bootstrap/001-schema.yaml

  - include:
      file: db/changelog/100-tables/101-enforcement-case.yaml

  - include:
      file: db/changelog/200-indexes/201-enforcement-case-indexes.yaml

  - include:
      file: db/changelog/300-constraints/301-case-status-fk.yaml

  - include:
      file: db/changelog/400-seed/401-case-status-reference.yaml

  - include:
      file: db/changelog/500-views/501-v-case-summary.yaml
```

## `include` vs `includeAll`

| Tag | Use |
|---|---|
| `include` | Explicit ordering. Recommended untuk production. |
| `includeAll` | Include semua file di folder. Praktis, tapi ordering harus benar-benar dikontrol. |

Untuk microservice production, saya lebih suka `include` eksplisit agar urutan DDL, seed, function, view, trigger, dan grants tidak ambigu.

---

# 6. Changeset Attributes

```yaml
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 20260715-001-add-risk-score
      author: case-service-team
      labels: case,risk-score
      contextFilter: dev,test,staging,prod
      runInTransaction: true
      failOnError: true
      comments: Add nullable risk_score for expand phase
      changes:
        - addColumn:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
            columns:
              - column:
                  name: risk_score
                  type: integer
      rollback:
        - dropColumn:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
            columnName: risk_score
```

| Attribute | Fungsi | Production Note |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | Identifier changeset. | Harus unik dalam kombinasi `author + file path`. |
| `author` | Pemilik changeset. | Gunakan team/service name. |
| `contextFilter` / `context` | Environment gating. | Pisahkan `dev/test/demo/prod`. |
| `labels` | Tag fitur/domain/release. | Berguna untuk filtering dan audit. |
| `dbms` | Batasi DB tertentu. | Misalnya `postgresql`. |
| `runOnChange` | Rerun saat checksum berubah. | Cocok untuk view/function/procedure. |
| `runAlways` | Run setiap deployment. | Hati-hati; jarang untuk DDL. |
| `runInTransaction` | Run dalam satu transaction jika DB mendukung. | Set `false` untuk operasi seperti PostgreSQL `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`. |
| `failOnError` | Apakah error menggagalkan migration. | Default fail; jangan set `false` sembarangan. |
| `logicalFilePath` | Logical path untuk identity changeset. | Berguna saat file dipindah. |
| `validCheckSum` | Checksum yang dianggap valid. | Incident/compatibility path; hati-hati. |
| `objectQuotingStrategy` | Strategy quoting object. | Gunakan konsisten. |
| `comments` | Dokumentasi changeset. | Wajib untuk high-risk migration. |

Liquibase tidak menjalankan ulang changeset setelah berhasil, kecuali diberi `runAlways` atau `runOnChange`; default changeset berjalan dalam satu transaction jika memungkinkan dan dapat dikontrol dengan `runInTransaction`. `runOnChange` menjalankan ulang changeset saat isinya berubah, sedangkan `runAlways` menjalankan changeset setiap deployment. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-a-changeset?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Contexts & Labels

## Context Example

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 401-seed-test-fixture
    author: case-service-team
    contextFilter: test
    changes:
      - sql:
          sql: |
            INSERT INTO case_app.test_fixture_case(id, name)
            VALUES ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001', 'TEST CASE');
```

Run:

```bash
liquibase update --context-filter=test
```

## Label Example

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 402-seed-risk-score-permission
    author: security-team
    labels: security,permission,risk-score
    changes:
      - sqlFile:
          path: db/changelog/400-seed/sql/permission_risk_score.sql
```

Run:

```bash
liquibase update --label-filter="security and risk-score"
```

Contexts dan labels sama-sama mengontrol apakah changeset eligible untuk dijalankan. Contexts sering dipakai untuk environment seperti `dev/test/prod`; labels lebih cocok untuk feature, release, module, atau ownership. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2-1/changelog-attributes/what-are-contexts?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Preconditions

## Common Preconditions

| Precondition | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `dbms` | Hanya jalan di DB tertentu. |
| `runningAs` | Pastikan user DB tertentu. |
| `changeLogPropertyDefined` | Pastikan property tersedia. |
| `tableExists` | Check table exists. |
| `columnExists` | Check column exists. |
| `indexExists` | Check index exists. |
| `sequenceExists` | Check sequence exists. |
| `viewExists` | Check view exists. |
| `foreignKeyConstraintExists` | Check FK exists. |
| `primaryKeyExists` | Check PK exists. |
| `uniqueConstraintExists` | Check unique constraint exists. |
| `changeSetExecuted` | Check changeset tertentu sudah executed. |
| `sqlCheck` | Custom SQL predicate. |
| `rowCount` | Check jumlah row. |
| `not`, `and`, `or` | Logical composition. |

## Example

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 301-add-case-status-fk
    author: case-service-team
    preConditions:
      onFail: HALT
      onError: HALT
      - tableExists:
          schemaName: case_app
          tableName: enforcement_case
      - tableExists:
          schemaName: case_app
          tableName: case_status_reference
      - sqlCheck:
          expectedResult: "0"
          sql: |
            SELECT COUNT(*)
            FROM case_app.enforcement_case c
            LEFT JOIN case_app.case_status_reference r
              ON r.code = c.status
            WHERE r.code IS NULL
    changes:
      - addForeignKeyConstraint:
          baseTableSchemaName: case_app
          baseTableName: enforcement_case
          baseColumnNames: status
          referencedTableSchemaName: case_app
          referencedTableName: case_status_reference
          referencedColumnNames: code
          constraintName: fk_enforcement_case_status
```

Preconditions mengontrol eksekusi update berdasarkan state database; jika precondition pada changeset gagal, changeset tersebut tidak dideploy. Ini penting sebelum destructive change seperti `dropTable`, constraint validation, atau migration yang mengasumsikan data bersih. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-1-1/what-are-preconditions?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Core Change Types

Liquibase Change Type adalah representasi database-independent untuk operasi DB, misalnya `CREATE TABLE`; best practice-nya satu logical Change Type per changeset agar failure dan rollback lebih predictable. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2/change-types/what-is-a-change-type?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Schema / Table

| Change Type | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `createSchema` | Create schema. |
| `dropSchema` | Drop schema. |
| `createTable` | Create table. |
| `dropTable` | Drop table. |
| `renameTable` | Rename table. |
| `setTableRemarks` | Comment/remarks table. |

```yaml
- createTable:
    schemaName: case_app
    tableName: enforcement_case
    columns:
      - column:
          name: id
          type: uuid
          constraints:
            primaryKey: true
            primaryKeyName: pk_enforcement_case
      - column:
          name: case_number
          type: varchar(64)
          constraints:
            nullable: false
      - column:
          name: status
          type: varchar(32)
          constraints:
            nullable: false
```

`createTable` membuat table dan biasanya berisi nested `column`; `column` dipakai oleh banyak Change Type seperti `createTable`, `addColumn`, `createIndex`, `insert`, `loadData`, dan `update`. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-1/change-types/createtable?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Column

| Change Type | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `addColumn` | Tambah column. |
| `dropColumn` | Drop column. |
| `renameColumn` | Rename column. |
| `modifyDataType` | Ubah tipe data. |
| `addDefaultValue` | Tambah default. |
| `dropDefaultValue` | Drop default. |
| `addNotNullConstraint` | Tambah NOT NULL. |
| `dropNotNullConstraint` | Drop NOT NULL. |
| `setColumnRemarks` | Comment/remarks column. |

```yaml
- addColumn:
    schemaName: case_app
    tableName: enforcement_case
    columns:
      - column:
          name: risk_score
          type: integer
          remarks: "Risk score calculated by risk engine"
```

`addColumn` menambahkan column ke table existing; pada nested `column`, `defaultValue` mengatur default column, sedangkan `value` mengisi existing rows tanpa mengubah default definition. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2/change-types/addcolumn?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Constraints

| Change Type | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `addPrimaryKey` | Tambah PK. |
| `dropPrimaryKey` | Drop PK. |
| `addUniqueConstraint` | Tambah unique. |
| `dropUniqueConstraint` | Drop unique. |
| `addForeignKeyConstraint` | Tambah FK. |
| `dropForeignKeyConstraint` | Drop FK. |
| `addCheckConstraint` | Tambah CHECK. |
| `dropCheckConstraint` | Drop CHECK. |
| `addNotNullConstraint` | Tambah NOT NULL. |
| `dropNotNullConstraint` | Drop NOT NULL. |

```yaml
- addCheckConstraint:
    schemaName: case_app
    tableName: enforcement_case
    constraintName: chk_enforcement_case_risk_score
    constraintBody: "risk_score IS NULL OR (risk_score >= 0 AND risk_score <= 100)"
    validate: false
```

Untuk enterprise PostgreSQL, banyak constraint lebih aman dibuat staged: clean data dulu, tambah constraint dengan strategi validation yang sesuai, lalu validate setelah data siap.

---

## Index / Sequence

| Change Type | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `createIndex` | Create index. |
| `dropIndex` | Drop index. |
| `createSequence` | Create sequence. |
| `dropSequence` | Drop sequence. |
| `alterSequence` | Alter sequence. |

```yaml
- createIndex:
    schemaName: case_app
    tableName: enforcement_case
    indexName: idx_enforcement_case_status_created_at
    columns:
      - column:
          name: status
      - column:
          name: created_at
          descending: true
```

`createIndex` membuat index pada existing column atau set of columns; order column dalam definition memengaruhi index yang dibuat. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2-1/change-types/createindex?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## Data / Seed

| Change Type | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `insert` | Insert static row. |
| `update` | Update row. |
| `delete` | Delete row. |
| `loadData` | Load CSV-style data. |
| `loadUpdateData` | Load/update data by key. |
| `sql` | Inline SQL. |
| `sqlFile` | SQL external file. |

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 401-seed-case-status
    author: case-service-team
    labels: seed,reference
    changes:
      - sql:
          splitStatements: false
          sql: |
            INSERT INTO case_app.case_status_reference(code, label, is_active)
            VALUES
              ('OPEN', 'Open', true),
              ('UNDER_REVIEW', 'Under Review', true),
              ('CLOSED', 'Closed', true)
            ON CONFLICT (code) DO UPDATE
            SET
              label = EXCLUDED.label,
              is_active = EXCLUDED.is_active;
```

Untuk PostgreSQL production, saya sering memilih `sql` / `sqlFile` untuk seed idempotent karena dapat memakai `ON CONFLICT`.

---

## View / Function / Procedure / Trigger

| Object | Recommended Liquibase Style |
|---|---|
| View | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true` |
| Materialized view | `sqlFile`; split definition/index/refresh |
| PL/pgSQL function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true` |
| Stored procedure | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true` |
| Trigger function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange: true` |
| Trigger binding | versioned `sql` / `sqlFile` |
| Event trigger | versioned `sql`; DBA/platform owned |

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 701-fn-normalize-code
    author: case-service-team
    runOnChange: true
    labels: function,plpgsql
    changes:
      - sqlFile:
          path: db/changelog/700-functions/sql/fn_normalize_code.sql
          splitStatements: false
          stripComments: false
    rollback:
      - sql:
          sql: |
            DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS case_app.fn_normalize_code(text);
```

Untuk PostgreSQL-specific object seperti function/procedure/trigger/materialized view, `sqlFile` biasanya lebih jelas daripada memaksakan modeled changelog. Pastikan `splitStatements: false` untuk PL/pgSQL body yang punya banyak semicolon.

---

# 10. `sql` and `sqlFile`

## Inline SQL

```yaml
- sql:
    splitStatements: false
    stripComments: false
    sql: |
      CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_app;
```

## External SQL File

```yaml
- sqlFile:
    path: db/changelog/500-views/sql/v_case_summary.sql
    splitStatements: false
    stripComments: false
    relativeToChangelogFile: true
```

| Attribute | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `path` | SQL file path. |
| `splitStatements` | Apakah Liquibase memecah SQL berdasarkan delimiter. |
| `stripComments` | Apakah comment dihapus. |
| `endDelimiter` | Statement delimiter custom. |
| `relativeToChangelogFile` | Resolve path relatif terhadap changelog file. |
| `dbms` | Batasi SQL file ke DB tertentu. |

Untuk PL/pgSQL, trigger, procedure, dan function, default aman adalah:

```yaml
splitStatements: false
stripComments: false
```

---

# 11. Rollback Cheat Sheet

## Manual Rollback

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 201-create-risk-score-index
    author: case-service-team
    changes:
      - createIndex:
          schemaName: case_app
          tableName: enforcement_case
          indexName: idx_enforcement_case_risk_score
          columns:
            - column:
                name: risk_score
    rollback:
      - dropIndex:
          schemaName: case_app
          indexName: idx_enforcement_case_risk_score
```

## Rollback SQL

```bash
liquibase rollback-sql --tag=before-risk-score-release
```

## Tag

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: tag-before-risk-score-release
    author: release-team
    changes:
      - tagDatabase:
          tag: before-risk-score-release
```

Liquibase dapat otomatis menghasilkan rollback untuk banyak modeled Change Type seperti `createTable`, `renameColumn`, dan `addColumn`, tetapi Change Type tertentu memerlukan rollback manual. Untuk SQL/formatted SQL yang kompleks, definisikan rollback eksplisit. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2-1/init-update-and-rollback-commands/rollback?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. Formatted SQL Changelog

## Basic

```sql
--liquibase formatted sql

--changeset case-service-team:20260715-001-create-schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS case_app;

--rollback DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS case_app;
```

## With Attributes

```sql
--liquibase formatted sql

--changeset case-service-team:20260715-002-create-view runOnChange:true labels:view,case contextFilter:dev,test,staging,prod
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.case_number,
    c.status,
    c.created_at,
    c.updated_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c;

--rollback DROP VIEW IF EXISTS case_app.v_case_summary;
```

## PL/pgSQL

```sql
--liquibase formatted sql

--changeset case-service-team:20260715-003-fn-normalize-code runOnChange:true splitStatements:false stripComments:false
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.fn_normalize_code(p_code text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
STRICT
AS $$
    SELECT upper(trim(p_code));
$$;

--rollback DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS case_app.fn_normalize_code(text);
```

Liquibase mendukung changelog dalam format SQL selain XML/YAML/JSON. Formatted SQL cocok untuk PostgreSQL-specific object, tetapi governance rollback dan statement splitting harus diperhatikan. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-1-1/sql-changelog-example?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 13. Important Runtime Parameters

| Parameter | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `--url` | JDBC URL. |
| `--username` | DB user. |
| `--password` | DB password. |
| `--changelog-file` | Root changelog file. |
| `--search-path` | Lokasi pencarian changelog/file. |
| `--classpath` | Classpath untuk driver/extensions. |
| `--driver` | JDBC driver class. |
| `--default-schema-name` | Default schema untuk object. |
| `--liquibase-schema-name` | Schema untuk Liquibase tracking tables. |
| `--database-changelog-table-name` | Nama table `DATABASECHANGELOG`. |
| `--database-changelog-lock-table-name` | Nama table `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK`. |
| `--context-filter` | Runtime filter untuk contexts. |
| `--label-filter` | Runtime filter untuk labels. |
| `--log-level` | Logging level. |
| `--defaults-file` | File properties custom. |
| `--changelog-lock-wait-time-in-minutes` | Berapa lama menunggu lock Liquibase. |
| `--dbclhistory-enabled` | Enable `DATABASECHANGELOGHISTORY` jika tersedia/edition mendukung. |

Command parameters seperti `--changelog-file`, `--password`, `--username`, dan `--reference-url` ditempatkan setelah command, sedangkan global parameters ditempatkan sebelum command. `liquibase-schema-name` memisahkan table tracking Liquibase dari managed schema. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-1/parameters/working-with-command-parameters?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 14. Java API Classes

## Core Classes

| Class / Interface | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `liquibase.Liquibase` | High-level facade untuk operasi Liquibase. |
| `liquibase.command.CommandScope` | Command API untuk menjalankan command seperti `update`, `rollback`, `generateChangelog`. |
| `liquibase.Scope` | Cascading configuration scope. |
| `liquibase.database.Database` | Abstraksi database target. |
| `liquibase.database.DatabaseFactory` | Factory untuk membuat `Database` dari connection. |
| `liquibase.database.jvm.JdbcConnection` | Wrap JDBC `Connection`. |
| `liquibase.resource.ResourceAccessor` | Abstraksi akses file/resource. |
| `liquibase.resource.ClassLoaderResourceAccessor` | Resource dari classpath. |
| `liquibase.resource.DirectoryResourceAccessor` | Resource dari directory. |
| `liquibase.Contexts` | Runtime context filter. |
| `liquibase.LabelExpression` | Runtime label filter. |
| `liquibase.changelog.DatabaseChangeLog` | Object model changelog. |
| `liquibase.changelog.ChangeSet` | Object model changeset. |
| `liquibase.exception.LiquibaseException` | Base exception. |

`Liquibase` adalah facade utama dan mayoritas method-nya wrapper di atas `CommandScope`; `CommandScope` adalah high-level facade untuk menjalankan command dengan pattern create scope, add arguments, lalu `execute()`. ([Liquibase Javadocs](https://javadocs.liquibase.com/liquibase-core/liquibase/Liquibase.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Embedded Example - Prefer for Test/Tooling, Not App Pod Startup

```java
import liquibase.Liquibase;
import liquibase.Contexts;
import liquibase.LabelExpression;
import liquibase.database.Database;
import liquibase.database.DatabaseFactory;
import liquibase.database.jvm.JdbcConnection;
import liquibase.resource.ClassLoaderResourceAccessor;

import java.sql.Connection;

public final class LiquibaseRunner {
    public void migrate(Connection connection) throws Exception {
        Database database = DatabaseFactory.getInstance()
                .findCorrectDatabaseImplementation(new JdbcConnection(connection));

        try (Liquibase liquibase = new Liquibase(
                "db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml",
                new ClassLoaderResourceAccessor(),
                database
        )) {
            liquibase.validate();
            liquibase.update(new Contexts("prod"), new LabelExpression("case-service"));
        }
    }
}
```

## CommandScope Example

```java
import liquibase.command.CommandScope;

public final class LiquibaseCommandRunner {
    public void runUpdate() throws Exception {
        new CommandScope("update")
                .addArgumentValue("changelogFile", "db/changelog/db.changelog-master.yaml")
                .addArgumentValue("url", "jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app")
                .addArgumentValue("username", "case_app_migration")
                .addArgumentValue("password", "secret")
                .execute();
    }
}
```

Catatan: detail argument names bisa berbeda antar versi minor. Untuk production tooling, pastikan dicek dengan JavaDoc/CLI help sesuai versi Liquibase yang dipakai.

---

# 15. Custom Change Classes

## Interfaces

| Interface | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `CustomTaskChange` | Custom task yang menjalankan logic Java. |
| `CustomSqlChange` | Custom change yang menghasilkan SQL statements. |
| `CustomTaskRollback` | Rollback untuk `CustomTaskChange`. |
| `CustomSqlRollback` | Rollback SQL untuk `CustomSqlChange`. |
| `CustomChange` | Base/custom lifecycle. |

## Example

```java
import liquibase.Scope;
import liquibase.change.custom.CustomTaskChange;
import liquibase.change.custom.CustomTaskRollback;
import liquibase.database.Database;
import liquibase.exception.CustomChangeException;
import liquibase.exception.RollbackImpossibleException;
import liquibase.exception.SetupException;
import liquibase.exception.ValidationErrors;
import liquibase.resource.ResourceAccessor;

public class ExampleCustomTaskChange implements CustomTaskChange, CustomTaskRollback {
    private String message;
    private ResourceAccessor resourceAccessor;

    public String getMessage() {
        return message;
    }

    public void setMessage(String message) {
        this.message = message;
    }

    @Override
    public void execute(Database database) throws CustomChangeException {
        Scope.getCurrentScope()
                .getLog(getClass())
                .info("Executing custom task: " + message);
    }

    @Override
    public void rollback(Database database)
            throws CustomChangeException, RollbackImpossibleException {
        Scope.getCurrentScope()
                .getLog(getClass())
                .info("Rollback custom task: " + message);
    }

    @Override
    public String getConfirmationMessage() {
        return "Custom task executed";
    }

    @Override
    public void setUp() throws SetupException {
    }

    @Override
    public void setFileOpener(ResourceAccessor resourceAccessor) {
        this.resourceAccessor = resourceAccessor;
    }

    @Override
    public ValidationErrors validate(Database database) {
        return new ValidationErrors();
    }
}
```

Changelog:

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 900-custom-task-example
    author: platform-team
    changes:
      - customChange:
          class: com.example.db.ExampleCustomTaskChange
          message: "hello from custom change"
```

`customChange` memungkinkan integrasi custom Java code dengan changeset; class biasanya mengimplementasikan `CustomSqlChange` atau `CustomTaskChange`, dan rollback dapat disediakan melalui interface rollback terkait. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/reference-guide-5-2-1/change-types/customchange?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 16. Liquibase Java Annotations

Sekali lagi: ini untuk **extension/custom Change Type authors**, bukan untuk migration YAML biasa.

## Important Annotations

| Annotation | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@DatabaseChange` | `liquibase.change.DatabaseChange` | Metadata untuk custom Change Type. |
| `@DatabaseChangeProperty` | `liquibase.change.DatabaseChangeProperty` | Metadata untuk property/parameter Change Type. |
| `@DatabaseChangeProperties` | `liquibase.change.DatabaseChangeProperties` | Container untuk multiple `@DatabaseChangeProperty`. |
| `@DatabaseChangeNote` | `liquibase.change.DatabaseChangeNote` | Documentation note untuk DB tertentu. |

`@DatabaseChange` digunakan oleh `AbstractChange` untuk deklarasi `ChangeMetaData` seperti `name`, `description`, dan `priority`; `@DatabaseChangeProperty` dipasang pada getter untuk metadata parameter seperti description, exampleValue, since, supported database, required database, mustEqualExisting, dan serializationType. ([Liquibase Javadocs](https://javadocs.liquibase.com/liquibase-core/liquibase/change/DatabaseChange.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Custom Change Type Skeleton

```java
import liquibase.change.AbstractChange;
import liquibase.change.DatabaseChange;
import liquibase.change.DatabaseChangeProperty;
import liquibase.database.Database;
import liquibase.exception.ValidationErrors;
import liquibase.statement.SqlStatement;
import liquibase.statement.core.RawSqlStatement;

@DatabaseChange(
        name = "createCaseAuditPolicy",
        description = "Creates case audit policy objects",
        priority = 1
)
public class CreateCaseAuditPolicyChange extends AbstractChange {
    private String schemaName;
    private String tableName;

    @DatabaseChangeProperty(
            description = "Schema containing the target table",
            exampleValue = "case_app"
    )
    public String getSchemaName() {
        return schemaName;
    }

    public void setSchemaName(String schemaName) {
        this.schemaName = schemaName;
    }

    @DatabaseChangeProperty(
            description = "Target table name",
            exampleValue = "enforcement_case"
    )
    public String getTableName() {
        return tableName;
    }

    public void setTableName(String tableName) {
        this.tableName = tableName;
    }

    @Override
    public SqlStatement[] generateStatements(Database database) {
        String sql = """
            COMMENT ON TABLE %s.%s IS 'Audit policy managed by Liquibase custom change'
            """.formatted(schemaName, tableName);

        return new SqlStatement[] {
                new RawSqlStatement(sql)
        };
    }

    @Override
    public String getConfirmationMessage() {
        return "Case audit policy comment created";
    }

    @Override
    public ValidationErrors validate(Database database) {
        ValidationErrors errors = new ValidationErrors();

        if (schemaName == null || schemaName.isBlank()) {
            errors.addError("schemaName is required");
        }

        if (tableName == null || tableName.isBlank()) {
            errors.addError("tableName is required");
        }

        return errors;
    }
}
```

Untuk enterprise app, custom Change Type sebaiknya jarang. Prefer `sqlFile` untuk PostgreSQL-specific DDL kecuali Anda benar-benar butuh reusable extension.

---

# 17. Important Internal / Metadata Tables

## `DATABASECHANGELOG`

Important columns:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `ID` | Changeset id. |
| `AUTHOR` | Changeset author. |
| `FILENAME` | Changelog file path. |
| `DATEEXECUTED` | Execution timestamp. |
| `ORDEREXECUTED` | Execution order. |
| `EXECTYPE` | `EXECUTED`, `RERAN`, `MARK_RAN`, etc. |
| `MD5SUM` | Checksum. |
| `DESCRIPTION` | Description generated from changes. |
| `COMMENTS` | Comments. |
| `TAG` | Tag if applied. |
| `LIQUIBASE` | Liquibase version. |
| `DEPLOYMENT_ID` | Deployment id. |

Liquibase identifies each row in `DATABASECHANGELOG` by `id`, `author`, and `filename`; there is no primary key by default, but the composite identity is unique across rows. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-the-databasechangelog-table?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK`

Important columns:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `ID` | Lock row id. |
| `LOCKED` | Whether lock is active. |
| `LOCKGRANTED` | Lock timestamp. |
| `LOCKEDBY` | Who holds lock. |

Liquibase uses `DATABASECHANGELOGLOCK` to prevent multiple Liquibase instances from updating the database at the same time. ([Liquibase Docs](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-the-database-changelog-lock-table?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 18. PostgreSQL Production Patterns

## Pattern A - Add Nullable Column

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 20260715-001-add-risk-score
    author: case-service-team
    changes:
      - addColumn:
          schemaName: case_app
          tableName: enforcement_case
          columns:
            - column:
                name: risk_score
                type: integer
    rollback:
      - dropColumn:
          schemaName: case_app
          tableName: enforcement_case
          columnName: risk_score
```

## Pattern B - Create Index Concurrently

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 20260715-002-create-case-status-index-concurrently
    author: case-service-team
    runInTransaction: false
    changes:
      - sql:
          splitStatements: false
          sql: |
            CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_enforcement_case_status
            ON case_app.enforcement_case(status);
    rollback:
      - sql:
          splitStatements: false
          sql: |
            DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF EXISTS case_app.idx_enforcement_case_status;
```

Satu statement per changeset untuk non-transactional PostgreSQL DDL.

## Pattern C - View with `runOnChange`

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 20260715-003-v-case-summary
    author: case-service-team
    runOnChange: true
    labels: view
    changes:
      - sqlFile:
          path: db/changelog/500-views/sql/v_case_summary.sql
          splitStatements: false
          stripComments: false
```

## Pattern D - PL/pgSQL Function

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 20260715-004-fn-calculate-risk-score
    author: case-service-team
    runOnChange: true
    labels: function,plpgsql
    changes:
      - sqlFile:
          path: db/changelog/700-functions/sql/fn_calculate_risk_score.sql
          splitStatements: false
          stripComments: false
    rollback:
      - sql:
          sql: |
            DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS case_app.fn_calculate_risk_score(uuid);
```

## Pattern E - Trigger Binding as Versioned

```yaml
- changeSet:
    id: 20260715-005-create-updated-at-trigger
    author: case-service-team
    labels: trigger
    changes:
      - sql:
          sql: |
            CREATE TRIGGER trg_010_enforcement_case_set_updated_at
            BEFORE UPDATE ON case_app.enforcement_case
            FOR EACH ROW
            EXECUTE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at();
    rollback:
      - sql:
          sql: |
            DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_010_enforcement_case_set_updated_at
            ON case_app.enforcement_case;
```

---

# 19. CI/CD Recommended Commands

## PR Validation

```bash
mvn -B liquibase:validate \
  -Dliquibase.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app \
  -Dliquibase.username=case_app_migration \
  -Dliquibase.password="$DB_PASSWORD"

mvn -B liquibase:updateSQL \
  -Dliquibase.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app \
  -Dliquibase.username=case_app_migration \
  -Dliquibase.password="$DB_PASSWORD"

mvn -B liquibase:update \
  -Dliquibase.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app \
  -Dliquibase.username=case_app_migration \
  -Dliquibase.password="$DB_PASSWORD"

mvn -B verify
```

## Production Job

```bash
liquibase validate
liquibase status --verbose
liquibase update-sql
liquibase update
liquibase status --verbose
```

---

# 20. Do / Don’t

## Do

| Do | Reason |
|---|---|
| Use one logical change per changeset. | Easier rollback/failure recovery. |
| Use explicit `include` ordering. | Safer dependencies. |
| Use `sqlFile` for PL/pgSQL/function/view/procedure. | Clearer PostgreSQL support. |
| Use `runOnChange` for stable-signature functions/views. | Source of truth stays current. |
| Use versioned changeset for trigger binding, constraints, table/column changes. | Structural changes should be auditable. |
| Use contexts for dev/test/demo/prod separation. | Prevent fixture leakage to prod. |
| Use labels for feature/domain ownership. | Better review/filtering. |
| Use preconditions for destructive/high-risk operations. | Fail early with explanation. |
| Use rollbackSQL/updateSQL review. | Safer production approval. |
| Keep migration user separate from app runtime user. | Least privilege. |

## Don’t

| Don’t | Why |
|---|---|
| Don’t edit already deployed changesets. | Causes checksum mismatch. |
| Don’t use `clearCheckSums` casually. | Can hide drift. |
| Don’t manually edit `DATABASECHANGELOG`. | Corrupts migration history. |
| Don’t put test/demo seed in prod context. | Data leakage. |
| Don’t use `runAlways` for structural DDL. | Repeated DDL risk. |
| Don’t mix many unrelated changes in one changeset. | Hard rollback/debug. |
| Don’t disable `failOnError` to “get green”. | Partial silent failure risk. |
| Don’t run Liquibase from every app pod. | Multi-pod race + privilege leak. |
| Don’t use rollback in prod unless tested. | Logical rollback may be unsafe. |

---

# 21. Minimal Production-Grade Template

```yaml
databaseChangeLog:
  - changeSet:
      id: 20260715-001-case-add-risk-score
      author: case-service-team
      labels: case,risk-score
      contextFilter: dev,test,staging,prod
      runInTransaction: true
      failOnError: true
      comments: >
        Expand phase. Adds nullable risk_score.
        App must tolerate NULL until backfill is complete.
      preConditions:
        onFail: HALT
        onError: HALT
        - tableExists:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
        - not:
            columnExists:
              schemaName: case_app
              tableName: enforcement_case
              columnName: risk_score
      changes:
        - addColumn:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
            columns:
              - column:
                  name: risk_score
                  type: integer
                  remarks: Risk score, nullable during expand/backfill phase
      rollback:
        - dropColumn:
            schemaName: case_app
            tableName: enforcement_case
            columnName: risk_score
```

---

# 22. Quick Reference: Best Mapping for PostgreSQL Objects

| PostgreSQL Object | Liquibase Style |
|---|---|
| Schema | `createSchema` or `sql` |
| Table | `createTable` |
| Column | `addColumn`, `dropColumn`, `renameColumn` |
| Check constraint | `addCheckConstraint` or `sql` for PG-specific `NOT VALID` |
| FK | `addForeignKeyConstraint` or `sql` for PG-specific staged validation |
| Index small | `createIndex` |
| Index concurrent | `sql` + `runInTransaction:false` |
| View | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange:true` |
| Materialized view | `sqlFile`; split definition/index/refresh |
| Function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange:true` |
| Procedure | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange:true` |
| Trigger function | `sqlFile` + `runOnChange:true` |
| Trigger binding | versioned `sql` |
| Event trigger | versioned `sql`, DBA/platform owned |
| Seed/reference data | idempotent `sql` or modeled `insert/loadUpdateData` |
| Large backfill | Separate Kubernetes Job, not normal migration SQL |

---

## Rekomendasi Materi Selanjutnya

Setelah Liquibase cheat sheet ini, materi yang paling relevan adalah:

**Cheat Sheet Liquibase PostgreSQL Production Recipes**

Isinya bisa fokus ke contoh siap pakai: `NOT VALID` constraint, `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`, view/function/procedure `sqlFile`, trigger migration, materialized view refresh, seed idempotent, rollback template, dan GitHub Actions/Kubernetes Job template.

**Sources:**

- [Secure 5.1: What is a Changelog?](https://docs.liquibase.com/secure/user-guide-5-2-1/what-is-a-changelog?utm_source=chatgpt.com)
- [Class Liquibase](https://javadocs.liquibase.com/liquibase-core/liquibase/Liquibase.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:47:15 PM

Saya akan buat cheat sheet Flyway yang praktis untuk Java/Maven/PostgreSQL, mencakup command, naming convention, Maven/CLI config, Java API classes, callback, placeholders, properties, dan production patterns.

Berikut **cheat sheet Flyway** untuk konteks **Java 17+, Maven, PostgreSQL, MyBatis, Jersey/JAX-RS, Kubernetes**.

Catatan awal: Flyway **tidak annotation-heavy**. Berbeda dengan JPA/CDI/JAX-RS, Flyway core hampir tidak memakai annotation untuk migration biasa. Yang paling penting adalah **file naming convention, command, configuration properties, Java API classes, Java migration classes, callback classes, dan schema history behavior**.

---

# 1. Core Mental Model

| Konsep | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Versioned migration | Migration yang berjalan sekali berdasarkan version. Biasanya `V001__create_table.sql`. |
| Repeatable migration | Migration yang rerun saat checksum berubah. Biasanya `R__view_case_summary.sql`. |
| Baseline | Menandai existing database agar Flyway mulai dari version tertentu. |
| Schema history table | Table metadata Flyway, default `flyway_schema_history`. |
| Migrate | Apply pending migrations. |
| Validate | Membandingkan migration yang tersedia dengan yang sudah applied. |
| Repair | Memperbaiki metadata schema history setelah failure/metadata mismatch; bukan command rutin. |
| Clean | Menghapus object dalam schema target; harus disabled di production. |

Flyway migration dapat berupa **versioned**, **repeatable**, atau **baseline**; versioned migration juga memiliki bentuk regular dan undo, tergantung edition/fitur yang dipakai. Repeatable migration rerun ketika checksum berubah dan sering dipakai untuk view, procedure, function, package, atau bulk reference data. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/migrations-271585107.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Migration Naming Convention

## Versioned SQL Migration

```text
V001__create_schema.sql
V002__create_enforcement_case_table.sql
V003__add_risk_score_column.sql
V004__create_case_status_index.sql
```

Format umum:

```text
V<version>__<description>.sql
```

Contoh production-style:

```text
V202607150001__case_add_risk_score_column.sql
V202607150002__case_create_status_reference_table.sql
V202607150003__case_add_status_fk_not_valid.sql
```

## Repeatable SQL Migration

```text
R__010_fn_normalize_code.sql
R__020_view_case_summary.sql
R__030_proc_refresh_case_metrics.sql
R__040_trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
```

Format umum:

```text
R__<description>.sql
```

Repeatable migration dieksekusi setelah pending versioned migrations dan diurutkan berdasarkan description; karena itu saya rekomendasikan prefix angka seperti `010`, `020`, `030` untuk dependency ordering. ([GitHub](https://github.com/flyway/flywaydb.org/blob/gh-pages/documentation/concepts/migrations.md?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Baseline Migration

```text
B001__baseline_existing_schema.sql
```

Cocok untuk existing legacy DB yang ingin mulai dikelola Flyway tanpa menjalankan semua historical DDL dari nol.

## Undo Migration

```text
U001__undo_create_schema.sql
```

Undo migration tersedia dalam model Flyway tertentu/edition tertentu; untuk production enterprise, jangan mengandalkan undo sebagai strategi utama sebelum benar-benar dites.

---

# 3. Recommended Folder Structure

```text
src/main/resources/db/migration/
  versioned/
    V202607150001__case_create_tables.sql
    V202607150002__case_add_indexes.sql
    V202607150003__case_add_constraints.sql

  repeatable/
    R__010_case_fn_normalize_code.sql
    R__020_case_view_case_summary.sql
    R__030_case_proc_refresh_dashboard_metrics.sql
    R__040_case_trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql

  callback/
    beforeMigrate.sql
    afterMigrate.sql
```

Atau default simple:

```text
src/main/resources/db/migration/
  V001__create_schema.sql
  V002__create_tables.sql
  R__view_case_summary.sql
```

Untuk production microservice, saya lebih suka folder dipisah:

```text
db/migration/versioned
db/migration/repeatable
```

lalu atur `locations`.

---

# 4. Maven Dependencies & Plugin

Untuk PostgreSQL modern, Flyway membutuhkan module database PostgreSQL terpisah. Dokumentasi Redgate menyebut PostgreSQL support berada di module `flyway-database-postgresql`. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/postgresql-database-277579325.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```xml
<properties>
    <flyway.version>...</flyway.version>
    <postgresql.version>...</postgresql.version>
</properties>

<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.flywaydb</groupId>
        <artifactId>flyway-core</artifactId>
        <version>${flyway.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.flywaydb</groupId>
        <artifactId>flyway-database-postgresql</artifactId>
        <version>${flyway.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
        <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
        <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Maven plugin:

```xml
<build>
    <plugins>
        <plugin>
            <groupId>org.flywaydb</groupId>
            <artifactId>flyway-maven-plugin</artifactId>
            <version>${flyway.version}</version>
            <configuration>
                <url>${db.url}</url>
                <user>${db.migration.user}</user>
                <password>${db.migration.password}</password>

                <locations>
                    <location>classpath:db/migration/versioned</location>
                    <location>classpath:db/migration/repeatable</location>
                </locations>

                <defaultSchema>case_app</defaultSchema>
                <schemas>
                    <schema>case_app</schema>
                </schemas>

                <table>flyway_schema_history</table>
                <cleanDisabled>true</cleanDisabled>
                <validateOnMigrate>true</validateOnMigrate>
            </configuration>
        </plugin>
    </plugins>
</build>
```

Flyway Maven plugin mendukung Maven 3.x pada Java 17; dokumentasi saat ini juga memberi catatan bahwa Flyway v13 akan membutuhkan Java 21. Ini penting untuk stack Anda yang masih menarget Java 17. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/maven-goal-277579365.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Command Cheat Sheet

| Command | Fungsi | Production Note |
|---|---|---|
| `flyway info` | Lihat status migration. | Wajib sebelum/after deploy. |
| `flyway validate` | Validasi applied vs available migrations. | Wajib. |
| `flyway migrate` | Apply pending migrations. | Main production command. |
| `flyway baseline` | Baseline existing DB. | Initial adoption only. |
| `flyway repair` | Repair schema history metadata. | Incident path only. |
| `flyway clean` | Drop/clean configured schemas. | Disable di production. |
| `flyway add` | Membuat migration script mengikuti convention. | Useful untuk developer workflow. |
| `flyway undo` | Undo migration. | Edition/strategy dependent; test dulu. |

`migrate` membawa schema ke versi terbaru dan akan membuat schema history table jika belum ada; `validate` gagal jika ada perbedaan nama/type/checksum, migration applied yang tidak resolved secara lokal, atau migration resolved yang belum applied; `repair` memperbaiki schema history table seperti menghapus failed migration entry, realign checksum/description/type, dan menandai missing migration sebagai deleted. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/migrate-277578887.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Maven goals:

```bash
mvn flyway:info
mvn flyway:validate
mvn flyway:migrate
mvn flyway:baseline
mvn flyway:repair
mvn flyway:clean
```

---

# 6. `flyway.conf` / Properties Cheat Sheet

Contoh:

```properties
flyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
flyway.user=case_app_migration
flyway.password=${DB_PASSWORD}

flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable
flyway.defaultSchema=case_app
flyway.schemas=case_app
flyway.table=flyway_schema_history

flyway.cleanDisabled=true
flyway.validateOnMigrate=true
flyway.baselineOnMigrate=false
flyway.outOfOrder=false
flyway.group=false
flyway.mixed=false

flyway.placeholderReplacement=true
flyway.placeholders.app_schema=case_app
flyway.placeholders.audit_schema=audit
flyway.placeholderPrefix=${
flyway.placeholderSuffix=}

flyway.connectRetries=10
flyway.encoding=UTF-8
```

Important properties:

| Property | Fungsi | Production Recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| `url` | JDBC URL target DB. | Secret/config external. |
| `user` | DB user. | Gunakan migration role, bukan runtime role. |
| `password` | DB password. | Secret manager/Kubernetes Secret. |
| `locations` | Folder scan migration. | Pisahkan versioned/repeatable. |
| `defaultSchema` | Default schema dan lokasi history table di multi-schema mode. | Set eksplisit. |
| `schemas` | Schema yang dikelola Flyway. | Set eksplisit. |
| `table` | Nama schema history table. | Default `flyway_schema_history` biasanya cukup. |
| `cleanDisabled` | Disable `clean`. | `true` di production. |
| `validateOnMigrate` | Validate sebelum migrate. | `true`. |
| `baselineOnMigrate` | Auto baseline non-empty schema tanpa history table. | `false` kecuali initial adoption terkendali. |
| `baselineVersion` | Version baseline. | Misalnya `1`. |
| `outOfOrder` | Izinkan migration versi lama jalan setelah versi baru. | `false` default; avoid prod. |
| `group` | Bungkus semua pending migrations dalam satu transaction jika DB support. | Hati-hati. |
| `mixed` | Izinkan transactional dan non-transactional statements dalam group yang sama. | Hati-hati. |
| `placeholderReplacement` | Enable placeholder. | Useful. |
| `placeholders.*` | Nilai placeholder. | Untuk schema/env names. |
| `sqlMigrationPrefix` | Prefix versioned migration. | Default `V`. |
| `repeatableSqlMigrationPrefix` | Prefix repeatable migration. | Default `R`. |
| `sqlMigrationSeparator` | Separator version/description. | Default `__`. |
| `sqlMigrationSuffixes` | Suffix migration files. | Default `.sql`. |
| `callbacks` | Java callbacks. | Jarang, governance only. |
| `skipDefaultCallbacks` | Disable default SQL callback loading. | Jarang. |

`locations` adalah array lokasi yang discan recursive untuk migrations; `table` mengatur nama schema history table dan default-nya `flyway_schema_history`; `cleanDisabled` default-nya `true` dan berguna untuk production agar command `clean` tidak berjalan; `baselineOnMigrate` otomatis baseline non-empty schema tanpa schema history table bila diaktifkan. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-locations-setting-277579008.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Maven Configuration Properties

Contoh via CLI:

```bash
mvn -B flyway:migrate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://db:5432/case_app \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app_migration \
  -Dflyway.password="$DB_PASSWORD" \
  -Dflyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable \
  -Dflyway.defaultSchema=case_app \
  -Dflyway.schemas=case_app \
  -Dflyway.cleanDisabled=true
```

Contoh profile Maven:

```xml
<profiles>
    <profile>
        <id>local</id>
        <properties>
            <db.url>jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app</db.url>
            <db.migration.user>case_app_migration</db.migration.user>
            <db.migration.password>case_app_migration_password</db.migration.password>
        </properties>
    </profile>
</profiles>
```

# 8. Java API Classes

## Core Classes

| Class / Interface | Package | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `Flyway` | `org.flywaydb.core.Flyway` | Main API untuk `migrate`, `validate`, `info`, `repair`, `clean`, dll. |
| `FluentConfiguration` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.configuration.FluentConfiguration` | Fluent API untuk konfigurasi Flyway. |
| `Configuration` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.configuration.Configuration` | Interface configuration. |
| `MigrationInfo` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.MigrationInfo` | Info satu migration. |
| `MigrationInfoService` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.MigrationInfoService` | Hasil `flyway.info()`. |
| `MigrateResult` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.output.MigrateResult` | Result dari `migrate()`. |
| `ValidateResult` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.output.ValidateResult` | Result dari `validateWithResult()`. |
| `RepairResult` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.output.RepairResult` | Result dari `repair()`. |
| `CleanResult` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.output.CleanResult` | Result dari `clean()`. |
| `FlywayException` | `org.flywaydb.core.api.FlywayException` | Base exception. |

`org.flywaydb.core.Flyway` adalah entry point utama Java API dan menjadi pusat untuk fungsi penting seperti `clean`, `validate`, dan `migrate`; `FluentConfiguration` adalah cara yang direkomendasikan untuk konfigurasi API Flyway. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/api-java-277579358.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Java API Methods

## Basic Programmatic Migration

```java
import org.flywaydb.core.Flyway;

public final class FlywayMigrationRunner {
    public void migrate() {
        Flyway flyway = Flyway.configure()
                .dataSource(
                        "jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app",
                        "case_app_migration",
                        "secret"
                )
                .locations(
                        "classpath:db/migration/versioned",
                        "classpath:db/migration/repeatable"
                )
                .defaultSchema("case_app")
                .schemas("case_app")
                .table("flyway_schema_history")
                .cleanDisabled(true)
                .validateOnMigrate(true)
                .load();

        flyway.info();
        flyway.validate();
        flyway.migrate();
    }
}
```

## Important `Flyway` Methods

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `Flyway.configure()` | Mulai fluent configuration. |
| `load()` | Build `Flyway` instance dari configuration. |
| `migrate()` | Apply pending migrations. |
| `validate()` | Validate applied vs available migrations. |
| `validateWithResult()` | Validate dan return structured result. |
| `info()` | Return migration info service. |
| `repair()` | Repair schema history. |
| `clean()` | Clean schema; production disabled. |
| `baseline()` | Baseline existing schema. |
| `undo()` | Undo migration jika available/edition mendukung. |

## Important `FluentConfiguration` Methods

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `dataSource(url, user, password)` | Set DB connection. |
| `dataSource(DataSource)` | Pakai existing DataSource. |
| `locations(...)` | Set migration locations. |
| `defaultSchema(...)` | Set default schema. |
| `schemas(...)` | Set managed schemas. |
| `table(...)` | Set schema history table name. |
| `baselineOnMigrate(boolean)` | Auto baseline non-empty schema. |
| `baselineVersion(String)` | Set baseline version. |
| `cleanDisabled(boolean)` | Disable clean. |
| `validateOnMigrate(boolean)` | Validate before migrate. |
| `outOfOrder(boolean)` | Allow out-of-order migration. |
| `group(boolean)` | Group all pending migrations in one transaction if supported. |
| `mixed(boolean)` | Allow mixed transactional/non-transactional migrations. |
| `placeholders(Map<String, String>)` | Set placeholders. |
| `placeholderPrefix(String)` | Set placeholder prefix. |
| `placeholderSuffix(String)` | Set placeholder suffix. |
| `callbacks(...)` | Register Java callbacks. |
| `javaMigrations(...)` | Register pre-instantiated Java migrations. |
| `encoding(...)` | Set migration file encoding. |
| `connectRetries(int)` | Retry DB connection. |

Flyway API juga bisa menerima pre-instantiated Java migrations lewat property `javaMigrations`, berguna bila migration Java perlu dibuat/dikonfigurasi oleh DI container. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/api-hooks-277579366.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 10. Java-Based Migrations

Flyway Java migration dipakai untuk transformasi yang sulit atau tidak nyaman di SQL. Untuk Flyway, Java-based migration harus mengimplementasikan `JavaMigration`; kebanyakan user disarankan extend `BaseJavaMigration` agar mengikuti naming convention default dan version/description bisa diekstrak dari class name. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/java-based-migrations-273973387.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Important Classes

| Class / Interface | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `JavaMigration` | Interface migration Java. |
| `BaseJavaMigration` | Convenience base class. |
| `Context` | Memberi akses ke JDBC connection dan config saat migration. |
| `BaselineJavaMigration` | Java baseline migration. |
| `UndoJavaMigration` | Java undo migration, jika digunakan. |

## Example

Class name harus mengikuti convention seperti SQL migration, tanpa suffix `.sql`.

```java
package db.migration;

import org.flywaydb.core.api.migration.BaseJavaMigration;
import org.flywaydb.core.api.migration.Context;

import java.sql.PreparedStatement;

public class V202607150004__backfill_case_risk_score_small
        extends BaseJavaMigration {

    @Override
    public void migrate(Context context) throws Exception {
        try (PreparedStatement ps = context.getConnection().prepareStatement("""
            UPDATE case_app.enforcement_case
            SET risk_score = 0
            WHERE risk_score IS NULL
              AND created_at >= now() - interval '7 days'
            """)) {
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }
}
```

## Practical Guidance

| Use Java Migration For | Avoid Java Migration For |
|---|---|
| Small deterministic transformation needing Java logic. | Large backfill with millions of rows. |
| LOB/file processing. | DDL that is clearer in SQL. |
| Controlled one-time data normalization. | Business workflow hidden in migration. |
| Complex calculation not expressible in SQL cleanly. | Network/external API calls during migration. |

Untuk large backfill, lebih baik Kubernetes Job/worker resumable daripada Java migration di Flyway.

---

# 11. Annotations

## Core Flyway

Flyway core migration **tidak membutuhkan annotation**. Tidak ada annotation seperti:

```java
@Migration
@FlywayMigration
@ChangeSet
```

untuk penggunaan Flyway core normal.

Yang dipakai adalah:

```text
- SQL file naming convention
- Java class naming convention
- Java interface/base class
- configuration properties
```

## Framework-Specific Annotation

Beberapa framework punya annotation sendiri untuk integrasi Flyway/DataSource, tetapi itu bukan Flyway core. Untuk stack Jersey/JAX-RS plain atau HK2/CDI/Weld, jangan desain migration berdasarkan annotation; desainlah berdasarkan CI/CD/Kubernetes Job.

---

# 12. SQL Callbacks

Flyway callbacks dapat berupa SQL atau Java dan dipakai untuk hook lifecycle seperti sebelum/sesudah migrate. SQL callback dimuat dari configured locations dengan naming pattern tertentu; konfigurasi `callbacks` dipakai untuk Java callbacks, sedangkan SQL callbacks ditemukan berdasarkan file name pattern. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-callbacks-setting-277578977.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Common SQL Callback Files

```text
beforeMigrate.sql
afterMigrate.sql
beforeEachMigrate.sql
afterEachMigrate.sql
beforeValidate.sql
afterValidate.sql
beforeClean.sql
afterClean.sql
```

Example:

```sql
-- db/migration/callback/beforeMigrate.sql
SELECT current_database(), current_user, now();
```

Example after migrate audit:

```sql
-- db/migration/callback/afterMigrate.sql
INSERT INTO case_app.deployment_migration_audit (
    service_name,
    release_version,
    migration_tool,
    status,
    completed_at
)
VALUES (
    '${service_name}',
    '${release_version}',
    'flyway',
    'SUCCESS',
    now()
);
```

## Callback Use Cases

| Callback | Use Case |
|---|---|
| `beforeMigrate.sql` | Check DB identity, set session config. |
| `afterMigrate.sql` | Insert audit record, run smoke query. |
| `beforeEachMigrate.sql` | Set lock/statement timeout per migration. |
| `afterEachMigrate.sql` | Lightweight logging. |

Jangan pakai callback untuk heavy business logic.

---

# 13. Java Callbacks

## Important Classes

| Class / Interface | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `Callback` | Main callback interface. |
| `Event` | Lifecycle event enum. |
| `Context` | Callback context, termasuk connection. |

`Callback` adalah interface utama untuk menangani Flyway lifecycle events; context diteruskan ke `handle(Event, Context)`. ([Javadoc](https://javadoc.io/doc/org.flywaydb/flyway-core/11.7.2/org/flywaydb/core/api/callback/Callback.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Example:

```java
package com.example.db;

import org.flywaydb.core.api.callback.Callback;
import org.flywaydb.core.api.callback.Context;
import org.flywaydb.core.api.callback.Event;

public final class MigrationAuditCallback implements Callback {

    @Override
    public boolean supports(Event event, Context context) {
        return event == Event.AFTER_MIGRATE;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean canHandleInTransaction(Event event, Context context) {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void handle(Event event, Context context) throws Exception {
        try (var ps = context.getConnection().prepareStatement("""
            INSERT INTO case_app.deployment_migration_audit (
                service_name,
                migration_tool,
                status,
                completed_at
            )
            VALUES ('case-service', 'flyway', 'SUCCESS', now())
            """)) {
            ps.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String getCallbackName() {
        return "migration-audit-callback";
    }
}
```

Configuration:

```properties
flyway.callbacks=com.example.db.MigrationAuditCallback
```

Use Java callbacks sparingly. SQL callbacks are usually simpler for DB-local audit.

---

# 14. Placeholders

## Config

```properties
flyway.placeholderReplacement=true
flyway.placeholders.app_schema=case_app
flyway.placeholders.audit_schema=audit
flyway.placeholders.runtime_role=case_app_runtime
flyway.placeholderPrefix=${
flyway.placeholderSuffix=}
```

## SQL

```sql
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS ${app_schema};

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA ${app_schema}
TO ${runtime_role};
```

Flyway placeholders replace tokens in SQL migrations; default token shape memakai prefix/suffix sehingga placeholder `key1` menjadi `${key1}`. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-placeholders-namespace-277579022.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Best Practice

| Do | Avoid |
|---|---|
| Placeholder untuk schema/role/env. | Placeholder untuk object name yang membuat SQL sulit direview. |
| Keep values explicit in pipeline. | Secret in migration files. |
| Use consistent names. | Random placeholder per file. |

---

# 15. Transaction Handling

Flyway biasanya membungkus setiap migration script dalam satu transaction dan menjalankannya berurutan; untuk database tertentu, `group=true` dapat membungkus semua pending migrations dalam satu transaction. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/migration-transaction-handling-273973399.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## PostgreSQL Notes

| Operation | Transactional? | Flyway Handling |
|---|---:|---|
| `CREATE TABLE` | Ya | Normal migration. |
| `ALTER TABLE` | Ya | Normal migration. |
| `CREATE INDEX` | Ya | Normal migration. |
| `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Tidak boleh dalam transaction block | Perlu non-transactional migration. |
| `DROP INDEX CONCURRENTLY` | Tidak boleh dalam transaction block | Perlu non-transactional migration. |
| View/function/procedure create | Ya | Repeatable migration. |

## Non-Transactional Migration Pattern

```sql
-- V202607150010__create_case_status_index_concurrently.sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_enforcement_case_status
ON case_app.enforcement_case(status);
```

Untuk Flyway, pisahkan file ini sendiri. Jangan campur dengan DDL lain.

---

# 16. Schema History Table

Default table:

```text
flyway_schema_history
```

Important states:

| State | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `Pending` | Resolved locally, belum applied. |
| `Success` | Applied sukses. |
| `Failed` | Migration gagal. |
| `Out of Order` | Applied out of order. |
| `Outdated` | Repeatable migration outdated dan perlu re-apply. |
| `Future` | Applied di DB tapi version lebih tinggi dari resolved lokal. |
| `Missing` | Applied di DB tapi file tidak ada lokal. |
| `Deleted` | Missing migration yang sudah ditandai oleh repair. |
| `Baseline` | Baseline marker. |

Schema history table melacak migration state; dokumentasi Flyway mencantumkan state seperti `Out of Order`, `Outdated`, `Future`, dan `Failed`. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-schema-history-table-273973417.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Query:

```sql
SELECT
    installed_rank,
    version,
    description,
    type,
    script,
    checksum,
    installed_by,
    installed_on,
    execution_time,
    success
FROM flyway_schema_history
ORDER BY installed_rank DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Jangan edit table ini manual kecuali incident path dengan approval.

---

# 17. Baseline

## CLI

```bash
flyway baseline \
  -baselineVersion=1000 \
  -baselineDescription="baseline existing production schema"
```

## Config

```properties
flyway.baselineVersion=1000
flyway.baselineDescription=baseline existing production schema
flyway.baselineOnMigrate=false
```

`baselineOnMigrate=true` otomatis menjalankan baseline saat `migrate` menemukan non-empty schema tanpa schema history table, lalu hanya migration di atas `baselineVersion` yang akan dijalankan. Ini berguna untuk initial adoption, tetapi berbahaya jika aktif tanpa sadar di production. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-baseline-on-migrate-setting-277578974.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 18. Repair

`repair` melakukan hal seperti menghapus failed migrations dari schema history table, realign checksum/description/type dengan migration lokal, dan menandai missing migrations sebagai deleted; user object yang tertinggal akibat failed migration tetap harus dibersihkan manual. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/repair-277578892.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Use Cases

| Use Case | Repair? |
|---|---:|
| Failed migration entry setelah object cleanup manual | Ya, dengan approval. |
| Checksum mismatch karena applied migration diedit | Jangan langsung; restore file atau buat migration baru. |
| Missing migration file sengaja dihapus | Bisa, tapi governance ketat. |
| Production routine deploy | Tidak. |

Command:

```bash
flyway repair
```

Rule:

```text
repair = incident recovery command
not normal deployment command
```

---

# 19. Clean

`clean` menghapus object dalam configured schemas dan `cleanDisabled` default-nya `true`; dokumentasi sendiri menyebut setting ini sangat berguna di production karena menjalankan clean bisa menjadi “career limiting move”. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/flyway-clean-disabled-setting-277578981.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Production config:

```properties
flyway.cleanDisabled=true
```

Non-prod only:

```bash
flyway clean
flyway migrate
```

Jangan pernah enable clean di production pipeline.

---

# 20. PostgreSQL Production Recipes

## Add Nullable Column

```sql
-- V202607150001__case_add_risk_score.sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD COLUMN risk_score integer;
```

## Add Constraint Staged

```sql
-- V202607150002__case_add_risk_score_check_not_valid.sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
ADD CONSTRAINT chk_enforcement_case_risk_score
CHECK (risk_score IS NULL OR (risk_score BETWEEN 0 AND 100))
NOT VALID;
```

```sql
-- V202607150003__case_validate_risk_score_check.sql
ALTER TABLE case_app.enforcement_case
VALIDATE CONSTRAINT chk_enforcement_case_risk_score;
```

## Create Index Concurrently

```sql
-- V202607150004__case_create_status_index_concurrently.sql
CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY IF NOT EXISTS idx_enforcement_case_status
ON case_app.enforcement_case(status);
```

## Repeatable View

```sql
-- R__020_case_view_case_summary.sql
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW case_app.v_case_summary AS
SELECT
    c.id,
    c.case_number,
    c.status,
    c.priority,
    c.created_at,
    c.updated_at
FROM case_app.enforcement_case c;
```

## Repeatable Function

```sql
-- R__010_case_fn_normalize_code.sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.fn_normalize_code(p_code text)
RETURNS text
LANGUAGE sql
IMMUTABLE
STRICT
AS $$
    SELECT upper(trim(p_code));
$$;
```

## Trigger Function + Binding

```sql
-- R__040_case_trg_fn_set_updated_at.sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at := now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;
```

```sql
-- V202607150005__case_create_updated_at_trigger.sql
CREATE TRIGGER trg_010_enforcement_case_set_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON case_app.enforcement_case
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION case_app.trg_fn_set_updated_at();
```

---

# 21. Recommended Object Mapping

| PostgreSQL Object | Flyway Migration Type |
|---|---|
| Schema | Versioned |
| Table | Versioned |
| Column | Versioned |
| Constraint | Versioned |
| Index | Versioned |
| Concurrent index | Versioned, one statement per file |
| Reference seed | Versioned or repeatable depending source-of-truth |
| View | Repeatable |
| Materialized view definition | Versioned or repeatable; often v2 for breaking change |
| Materialized view refresh | Operational job/procedure, not always migration |
| PL/pgSQL function | Repeatable if same signature |
| Function signature change | Versioned v2 |
| Stored procedure | Repeatable if same signature/intent |
| Trigger function | Repeatable |
| Trigger binding | Versioned |
| Event trigger | Versioned, DBA/platform-owned |
| Large backfill | Separate Kubernetes Job |

---

# 22. CI/CD Pattern

## Pull Request

```bash
mvn -B test

mvn -B flyway:validate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app_migration \
  -Dflyway.password="$DB_PASSWORD"

mvn -B flyway:migrate \
  -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app \
  -Dflyway.user=case_app_migration \
  -Dflyway.password="$DB_PASSWORD"

mvn -B verify
```

## Production Job

```bash
flyway info
flyway validate
flyway migrate
flyway info
```

## Kubernetes Rule

```text
Run Flyway once as CI/CD step or Kubernetes Job.
Do not run Flyway from every Jersey application pod startup.
```

Flyway documentation promotes Java API integration before application startup, but in Kubernetes multi-replica production this can create operational risks: multi-pod startup contention, migration credentials inside runtime pods, and migration logs mixed with app logs. For this stack, a one-time Job/pipeline migration is safer operationally. ([Redgate Documentation](https://documentation.red-gate.com/fd/api-java-277579358.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 23. GitHub Actions Example

```yaml
name: db-migration-check

on:
  pull_request:

jobs:
  migration-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:17
        env:
          POSTGRES_DB: case_app
          POSTGRES_USER: case_app_migration
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: case_app_migration_password
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U case_app_migration -d case_app"
          --health-interval 5s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 20

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: temurin
          java-version: "17"
          cache: maven

      - name: Check dangerous DDL
        run: ./scripts/check-dangerous-ddl.sh src/main/resources/db/migration

      - name: Flyway validate
        run: >
          mvn -B flyway:validate
          -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
          -Dflyway.user=case_app_migration
          -Dflyway.password=case_app_migration_password

      - name: Flyway migrate
        run: >
          mvn -B flyway:migrate
          -Dflyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5432/case_app
          -Dflyway.user=case_app_migration
          -Dflyway.password=case_app_migration_password

      - name: Run integration tests
        run: mvn -B verify
```

# 24. Dangerous DDL Detector

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="${1:-src/main/resources/db/migration}"

PATTERN="DROP TABLE|DROP COLUMN|TRUNCATE|CASCADE|ALTER COLUMN .* TYPE|CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY|DROP FUNCTION|DROP PROCEDURE|DROP TRIGGER|DISABLE TRIGGER|DROP MATERIALIZED VIEW|ALTER TABLE .* SET NOT NULL"

if grep -RniE "$PATTERN" "$ROOT"; then
  echo "High-risk database migration detected. DB review required."
  exit 1
fi

echo "No high-risk DDL pattern detected."
```

---

# 25. Do / Don’t

## Do

| Do | Reason |
|---|---|
| Use SQL migrations for PostgreSQL DDL. | Transparent and reviewable. |
| Use repeatable migrations for view/function/procedure with stable signature. | Clean source-of-truth. |
| Use versioned migrations for table/column/index/constraint/trigger binding. | Structural auditability. |
| Use one file per concurrent index. | PostgreSQL transaction limitation. |
| Set `cleanDisabled=true`. | Production safety. |
| Run `validate` before `migrate`. | Detect drift/checksum/missing files. |
| Run migration in CI with real PostgreSQL. | MyBatis/PostgreSQL compatibility. |
| Keep migration role separate from runtime role. | Least privilege. |
| Use `info` before/after production migration. | Evidence and traceability. |
| Treat `repair` as incident command. | Avoid hiding drift. |

## Don’t

| Don’t | Why |
|---|---|
| Don’t edit applied versioned migrations. | Checksum mismatch/drift. |
| Don’t run Flyway from every app pod. | Race, privilege leak, noisy failure mode. |
| Don’t enable clean in prod. | Data loss risk. |
| Don’t rely on `outOfOrder=true` as normal practice. | History becomes harder to reason about. |
| Don’t put large backfill in migration file. | Long transaction/WAL/lock risk. |
| Don’t use repeatable migration for structural table changes. | Rerun danger. |
| Don’t hide business workflow in Java migration. | Hard to observe/retry/version. |
| Don’t manually edit `flyway_schema_history`. | Corrupts migration memory. |

---

# 26. Minimal Production Template

```properties
flyway.url=jdbc:postgresql://${DB_HOST}:${DB_PORT}/${DB_NAME}
flyway.user=${DB_MIGRATION_USER}
flyway.password=${DB_MIGRATION_PASSWORD}

flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/versioned,classpath:db/migration/repeatable
flyway.defaultSchema=case_app
flyway.schemas=case_app
flyway.table=flyway_schema_history

flyway.cleanDisabled=true
flyway.validateOnMigrate=true
flyway.baselineOnMigrate=false
flyway.outOfOrder=false
flyway.group=false
flyway.mixed=false

flyway.placeholderReplacement=true
flyway.placeholders.app_schema=case_app
flyway.placeholders.runtime_role=case_app_runtime
```

Recommended command sequence:

```bash
flyway info
flyway validate
flyway migrate
flyway info
```

---

# 27. Quick Reference Summary

| Area | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Annotation | Tidak ada core Flyway annotation untuk migration biasa. |
| SQL migration | Default utama untuk PostgreSQL. |
| Java migration | Extend `BaseJavaMigration`; gunakan sparingly. |
| Callback | SQL callback lebih sederhana; Java callback untuk logic khusus. |
| Versioned file | `V<version>__description.sql`. |
| Repeatable file | `R__description.sql`. |
| Metadata table | `flyway_schema_history`. |
| PostgreSQL module | Tambahkan `flyway-database-postgresql`. |
| Production command | `info → validate → migrate → info`. |
| Prod safety | `cleanDisabled=true`, separate migration role, no pod-startup migration. |
| View/function/procedure | Repeatable if stable signature. |
| Table/column/constraint | Versioned. |
| Large backfill | Kubernetes Job, not Flyway migration. |
| Repair | Incident path only. |

Materi lanjutan yang paling berguna setelah ini:

**Flyway PostgreSQL Production Recipes**

Fokusnya: contoh siap pakai untuk `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`, `NOT VALID` constraint, repeatable view/function/procedure, trigger migration, materialized view migration, seed idempotent, callback audit, Maven/GitHub Actions/Kubernetes Job, dan failure recovery.
